<file path="src/components/BottomNav.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import { Layers, Star, Calendar, Share2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

export type NavTab = 'tools' | 'saved' | 'calendar' | 'share';

interface BottomNavProps {
  activeTab: NavTab;
  onSelectTab: (tab: NavTab) => void;
  savedCount?: number;
}

export function BottomNav({ activeTab, onSelectTab, savedCount = 0 }: BottomNavProps) {
  const { t } = useApp();

  const tabs: { id: NavTab; labelKey: string; defaultLabel: string; icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }> }[] = [
    { id: 'tools', labelKey: 'nav_tools', defaultLabel: 'Tools', icon: Layers },
    { id: 'saved', labelKey: 'nav_saved', defaultLabel: 'Saved', icon: Star },
    { id: 'calendar', labelKey: 'nav_calendar', defaultLabel: 'Calendar', icon: Calendar },
    { id: 'share', labelKey: 'nav_share_app', defaultLabel: 'Share App', icon: Share2 },
  ];

  return (
    <nav
      aria-label="Bottom Navigation"
      className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 right-0 z-40 border-t border-zinc-200 bg-white/95 backdrop-blur-lg dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 transition-colors"
    >
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-xl items-center justify-around px-2 pt-2 pb-3 sm:pb-3">
        {tabs.map((tab) => {
          const isActive = activeTab === tab.id;
          const Icon = tab.icon;
          const label = t(tab.labelKey, tab.defaultLabel);

          return (
            <button
              key={tab.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectTab(tab.id)}
              aria-label={label}
              aria-current={isActive ? 'page' : undefined}
              className={`relative flex flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-2xl py-1.5 min-h-[52px] transition-all active:scale-95 ${
                isActive
                  ? 'text-zinc-950 dark:text-white font-bold'
                  : 'text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="relative">
                <div
                  className={`flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl transition-all ${
                    isActive
                      ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 shadow-sm'
                      : 'bg-transparent text-current'
                  }`}
                >
                  <Icon className="h-4 w-4" />
                </div>
                {/* Saved Count Badge */}
                {tab.id === 'saved' && savedCount > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-1 -right-1.5 flex h-4 min-w-[16px] items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-amber-500 px-1 text-[10px] font-black text-white shadow-xs">
                    {savedCount > 99 ? '99+' : savedCount}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
              <span className={`text-[11px] tracking-tight leading-none ${isActive ? 'font-bold' : 'font-medium'}`}>
                {label}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </nav>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/EventDetailsModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import {
  X,
  Calendar,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  Video,
  Navigation,
  Share2,
  ExternalLink,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { GeneratedScheduleEvent } from '@/lib/calendarGenerator';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

interface EventDetailsModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  event: GeneratedScheduleEvent | null;
}

export function EventDetailsModal({ isOpen, onClose, event }: EventDetailsModalProps) {
  const { getLocalized } = useApp();

  if (!isOpen || !event) return null;

  const title = getLocalized(event.title);
  const locationOrLink = event.location_or_link || '';
  const isUrl = locationOrLink.startsWith('http');
  const isZoom = locationOrLink.includes('zoom.us') || locationOrLink.includes('zoom');
  const venueName = event.venue || (isUrl ? 'Online Meeting' : locationOrLink);
  const physicalAddress = !isUrl ? locationOrLink || event.venue || '' : '';

  const handleShareWhatsApp = () => {
    const lines = [
      `📅 *${title}*`,
      `🗓️ ${event.date} | ⏰ ${event.time}`,
      event.speaker ? `🎙️ Speaker: ${event.speaker}` : '',
      venueName ? `📍 Venue: ${venueName}` : '',
      locationOrLink && !isUrl ? `🗺️ Map: https://maps.google.com/?q=${encodeURIComponent(locationOrLink)}` : '',
      isUrl ? `🔗 Link: ${locationOrLink}` : '',
      event.description ? `\n📝 ${event.description}` : '',
      `\n_Shared via AA Team Hub_`,
    ].filter(Boolean);

    const text = lines.join('\n');
    window.location.href = `https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(text)}`;
  };

  const gmapsUrl = `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(physicalAddress)}`;
  const wazeUrl = `https://waze.com/ul?q=${encodeURIComponent(physicalAddress)}`;
  const embedMapsUrl = `https://maps.google.com/maps?q=${encodeURIComponent(physicalAddress)}&output=embed`;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg max-h-[90vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        {event.image_url ? (
          <div className="relative h-44 sm:h-52 w-full bg-zinc-950 flex-shrink-0">
            <Image
              src={event.image_url}
              alt={title}
              fill
              unoptimized
              className="object-cover"
            />
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 via-black/20 to-transparent" />
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="absolute top-3 right-3 rounded-full bg-black/60 p-2 text-white hover:bg-black/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <div className="absolute bottom-3 left-4 right-4">
              <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-500 text-white uppercase tracking-wider mb-1">
                {event.is_recurring ? 'Recurring Meeting' : 'Special Event'}
              </span>
              <h2 className="text-lg sm:text-xl font-bold text-white line-clamp-2 leading-tight">
                {title}
              </h2>
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70 flex-shrink-0">
            <div>
              <span className="inline-block px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 uppercase tracking-wider mb-0.5">
                {event.is_recurring ? 'Recurring Meeting' : 'Special Event'}
              </span>
              <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 leading-snug">
                {title}
              </h2>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4">
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-2.5 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/50 p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
              <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{event.date}</span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
              <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{event.time}</span>
            </div>
            {event.speaker && (
              <div className="col-span-1 sm:col-span-2 flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                <span className="text-base leading-none">🎙️</span>
                <span>{event.speaker}</span>
              </div>
            )}
            {venueName && (
              <div className="col-span-1 sm:col-span-2 flex items-start gap-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                <MapPin className="w-4 h-4 text-red-500 flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                <span className="leading-snug">{venueName}</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {event.description && (
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 mb-1">
                Meeting Details
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-line bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900">
                {event.description}
              </p>
            </div>
          )}

          {physicalAddress && (
            <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
              <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400">
                Location & Navigation
              </h4>

              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                <a
                  href={gmapsUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-xs"
                >
                  <Navigation className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Google Maps</span>
                </a>
                <a
                  href={wazeUrl}
                  target="_blank"
                  rel="noopener noreferrer"
                  className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-cyan-600 hover:bg-cyan-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-xs"
                >
                  <Navigation className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>Waze</span>
                </a>
              </div>

              <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 h-44 w-full">
                <iframe
                  title="Venue Map Preview"
                  width="100%"
                  height="100%"
                  style={{ border: 0 }}
                  loading="lazy"
                  src={embedMapsUrl}
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {isUrl && (
            <div className="pt-1">
              <a
                href={locationOrLink}
                target="_blank"
                rel="noopener noreferrer"
                className="w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-3 rounded-2xl bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md transition-all active:scale-98"
              >
                {isZoom ? <Video className="w-4 h-4" /> : <ExternalLink className="w-4 h-4" />}
                <span>Join Online Meeting</span>
              </a>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="p-4 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70 flex items-center justify-between gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
          >
            Close
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleShareWhatsApp}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-md shadow-emerald-600/20"
          >
            <Share2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span>Share via WhatsApp</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/FilterBar.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Search,
  X,
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  Tag,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp, type ContentLangFilter } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { Section } from '@/types/app';

interface FilterBarProps {
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchChange: (q: string) => void;
  sections: Section[];
  activeSectionId: string;
  onSelectSection: (id: string) => void;
  contentLang: ContentLangFilter;
  onContentLangChange: (lang: ContentLangFilter) => void;
  topicTags: string[];
  activeTopic: string | null;
  onSelectTopic: (topic: string | null) => void;
  hasTamil?: boolean;
}

const SECTION_ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
};

export function FilterBar({
  searchQuery,
  onSearchChange,
  sections,
  activeSectionId,
  onSelectSection,
  contentLang,
  onContentLangChange,
  topicTags,
  activeTopic,
  onSelectTopic,
  hasTamil = false,
}: FilterBarProps) {
  const { t, getLocalized } = useApp();
  const searchInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const langFilters: { value: ContentLangFilter; label: string }[] = [
    { value: 'all', label: t('all', 'All') },
    { value: 'en', label: 'EN' },
    { value: 'bm', label: 'BM' },
    { value: 'cn', label: '中文' },
    ...(hasTamil ? [{ value: 'ta' as ContentLangFilter, label: 'தமிழ்' }] : []),
  ];

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-3 py-2">
      {/* Search Input Bar */}
      <div className="relative px-4">
        <div className="relative flex items-center">
          <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 h-4 w-4 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500" />
          <input
            ref={searchInputRef}
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => onSearchChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={t('search_placeholder', 'Search topic, speaker, or tool...')}
            className="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 bg-zinc-50 pl-10 pr-10 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-950 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:border-zinc-900 focus:bg-white focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-zinc-900 dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 dark:focus:border-zinc-100 dark:focus:bg-zinc-950 dark:focus:ring-zinc-100 transition-all shadow-xs"
          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                onSearchChange('');
                searchInputRef.current?.focus();
              }}
              aria-label="Clear search"
              className="absolute right-3 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-300 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700"
            >
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
      {/* Horizontal Scrollable Section Pills */}
      <div className="no-scrollbar flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4 py-1">
        {/* All Section Pill */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onSelectSection('all')}
          className={`flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
            activeSectionId === 'all'
              ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-sm dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950'
              : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50'
          }`}
        >
          <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          <span>{t('all', 'All')}</span>
        </button>

        {/* Dynamic Section Pills */}
        {sections.map((sec) => {
          const isActive = activeSectionId === sec.id;
          const IconComp = SECTION_ICON_MAP[sec.icon_name] || Folder;
          const sectionTitle = getLocalized(sec.title) || sec.slug;

          return (
            <button
              key={sec.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectSection(sec.id)}
              className={`flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white shadow-sm dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950'
                  : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50'
              }`}
            >
              <IconComp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              <span>{sectionTitle}</span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* Sub-Filter Row: Compact Content Language Filter + Dynamic Topic Tags */}
      <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 px-4">
        {/* Content Language Pills */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar py-0.5">
          <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mr-1 flex-shrink-0">
            Lang:
          </span>
          {langFilters.map((lf) => {
            const isSelected = contentLang === lf.value;
            return (
              <button
                key={lf.value}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onContentLangChange(lf.value)}
                className={`min-h-[28px] rounded-lg px-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all flex-shrink-0 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-900'
                    : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700'
                }`}
              >
                {lf.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Dynamic Topic Tag Pills */}
        {topicTags.length > 0 && (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar py-0.5">
            <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 uppercase tracking-wider mr-1 flex-shrink-0 flex items-center gap-1">
              <Tag className="h-3 w-3" />
            </span>
            {topicTags.map((topic) => {
              const isSelected = activeTopic === topic;
              return (
                <button
                  key={topic}
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => onSelectTopic(isSelected ? null : topic)}
                  className={`min-h-[26px] flex-shrink-0 rounded-full px-3 text-[11px] font-semibold transition-all ${
                    isSelected
                      ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white shadow-xs font-bold'
                      : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 border border-zinc-200/50 dark:border-zinc-700/50'
                  }`}
                >
                  #{topic}
                </button>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/ImageLightbox.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import { X, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react';

interface ImageLightboxProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  imageUrl: string;
  title?: string;
}

export function ImageLightbox({ isOpen, onClose, imageUrl, title }: ImageLightboxProps) {
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    // Prevent body scroll when open
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow;
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  if (!isOpen || !imageUrl) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-label={title || 'Image preview'}
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-black/90 p-4 backdrop-blur-md animate-in fade-in duration-200"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="relative flex max-h-[92vh] max-w-2xl w-full flex-col items-center justify-center"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Top Controls */}
        <div className="mb-3 flex w-full items-center justify-between px-1 text-white">
          <p className="truncate text-sm font-semibold pr-4">{title || 'Preview'}</p>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <a
              href={imageUrl}
              target="_blank"
              rel="noopener noreferrer"
              aria-label="Open image in new tab"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white transition-colors"
            >
              <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4" />
            </a>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              aria-label="Close image"
              className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 text-white transition-colors"
            >
              <X className="h-5 w-5" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Image Container */}
        <div className="relative max-h-[82vh] w-full overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-zinc-950 flex items-center justify-center border border-zinc-800">
          <Image
            src={imageUrl}
            alt={title || 'Enlarged view'}
            width={1200}
            height={900}
            unoptimized
            className="max-h-[82vh] w-auto max-w-full object-contain"
          />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/QRCodeModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { X, Copy, Check, ExternalLink, QrCode } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

interface QRCodeModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  speakerName?: string;
  language?: string;
  url: string;
}

export function QRCodeModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  title,
  speakerName,
  language,
  url,
}: QRCodeModalProps) {
  const { t } = useApp();
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return;
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onClose();
    };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    const prevOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      document.body.style.overflow = prevOverflow;
    };
  }, [isOpen, onClose]);

  const handleCopy = async () => {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      } else {
        const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
        textarea.value = url;
        document.body.appendChild(textarea);
        textarea.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        document.body.removeChild(textarea);
      }
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Copy failed', err);
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div
      role="dialog"
      aria-modal="true"
      aria-labelledby="qr-modal-title"
      className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/80 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200"
      onClick={onClose}
    >
      <div
        className="relative w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-6 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 shadow-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800"
        onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
      >
        {/* Close Button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label={t('close', 'Close')}
          className="absolute right-4 top-4 flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 transition-colors"
        >
          <X className="h-4 w-4" />
        </button>

        {/* Header */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-3 pr-8">
          <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
            <QrCode className="h-4 w-4" />
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {t('btn_qr', 'QR Code')}
          </span>
        </div>

        {/* Title & Metadata */}
        <div className="mb-4">
          <h2 id="qr-modal-title" className="text-base font-bold leading-snug line-clamp-2">
            {title}
          </h2>
          <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
            {speakerName && (
              <span className="inline-flex items-center font-medium">
                🎙️ {speakerName}
              </span>
            )}
            {language && (
              <span className="rounded-md bg-zinc-100 px-2 py-0.5 font-semibold text-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 uppercase">
                {language}
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* QR Code Container - High Contrast White Card */}
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white p-5 shadow-inner border border-zinc-200">
          <QRCodeSVG
            value={url || 'https://teamhub.app'}
            size={220}
            level="M"
            marginSize={1}
            className="h-auto w-full max-w-[220px]"
          />
          <p className="mt-3 text-center text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-500">
            {t('scan_to_view', 'Scan with camera or phone to view')}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="mt-4 flex gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleCopy}
            className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 px-3 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
          >
            {copied ? (
              <>
                <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" />
                <span className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                  {t('btn_copied', 'Copied!')}
                </span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Copy className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400" />
                <span>{t('btn_copy', 'Copy Link')}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>

          <a
            href={url}
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:bg-zinc-100 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            <span>{t('btn_open', 'Open')}</span>
          </a>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/context/AppContext.tsx">
'use client';

import React, {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useMemo,
  useSyncExternalStore,
  type ReactNode,
} from 'react';
import type { LanguageCode, LocalizedString, UiDictionary } from '@/types/app';
import { DEFAULT_LANGUAGE } from '@/types/app';
import { DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY } from '@/lib/dictionary';

export type ContentLangFilter = 'all' | LanguageCode;

export interface AppContextType {
  lang: LanguageCode;
  setLang: (lang: LanguageCode) => void;
  contentLangFilter: ContentLangFilter;
  setContentLangFilter: (filter: ContentLangFilter) => void;
  savedToolIds: string[];
  toggleSavedTool: (id: string) => void;
  isToolSaved: (id: string) => boolean;
  t: (key: string, fallback?: string) => string;
  getLocalized: (obj?: Partial<LocalizedString> | null, targetLang?: LanguageCode) => string;
  dictionary: Record<string, UiDictionary>;
  isHydrated: boolean;
}

const LANG_KEY = 'teamhub_lang';
const SAVED_TOOLS_KEY = 'teamhub_saved_tools';
const FILTER_KEY = 'teamhub_content_filter';
const STORAGE_EVENT = 'teamhub_storage_update';

function notifyStorageChange() {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.dispatchEvent(new Event(STORAGE_EVENT));
  }
}

function subscribeStorage(callback: () => void) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
  window.addEventListener('storage', callback);
  window.addEventListener(STORAGE_EVENT, callback);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('storage', callback);
    window.removeEventListener(STORAGE_EVENT, callback);
  };
}

let cachedSavedToolsRaw: string | null = null;
let cachedSavedTools: string[] = [];
const SERVER_SAVED_TOOLS: string[] = [];

function getSavedToolsSnapshot(): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SAVED_TOOLS_KEY);
    if (raw !== cachedSavedToolsRaw) {
      cachedSavedToolsRaw = raw;
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        cachedSavedTools = Array.isArray(parsed)
          ? parsed.filter((item): item is string => typeof item === 'string')
          : [];
      } else {
        cachedSavedTools = [];
      }
    }
  } catch {
    cachedSavedTools = [];
  }
  return cachedSavedTools;
}

export function getLocalized(
  obj?: Partial<LocalizedString> | null,
  targetLang?: LanguageCode,
  activeLang: LanguageCode = DEFAULT_LANGUAGE
): string {
  if (!obj) return '';
  const langToUse = targetLang || activeLang;
  const val = obj[langToUse]?.trim();
  if (val) return val;

  if (obj.en?.trim()) return obj.en.trim();
  if (obj.bm?.trim()) return obj.bm.trim();
  if (obj.cn?.trim()) return obj.cn.trim();
  if (obj.ta?.trim()) return obj.ta.trim();
  return '';
}

export interface AppProviderProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  initialDictionary?: UiDictionary[] | Record<string, UiDictionary>;
  defaultLang?: LanguageCode;
  defaultContentFilter?: ContentLangFilter;
}

export function AppProvider({
  children,
  initialDictionary,
  defaultLang = DEFAULT_LANGUAGE,
  defaultContentFilter = 'all',
}: AppProviderProps) {
  const getLangSnapshot = useCallback((): LanguageCode => {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem(LANG_KEY);
      if (stored && ['en', 'bm', 'cn', 'ta'].includes(stored)) {
        return stored as LanguageCode;
      }
    } catch {}
    return defaultLang;
  }, [defaultLang]);

  const getFilterSnapshot = useCallback((): ContentLangFilter => {
    try {
      const stored = localStorage.getItem(FILTER_KEY);
      if (stored && ['all', 'en', 'bm', 'cn', 'ta'].includes(stored)) {
        return stored as ContentLangFilter;
      }
    } catch {}
    return defaultContentFilter;
  }, [defaultContentFilter]);

  const lang = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeStorage, getLangSnapshot, () => defaultLang);
  const contentLangFilter = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeStorage,
    getFilterSnapshot,
    () => defaultContentFilter
  );
  const savedToolIds = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeStorage,
    getSavedToolsSnapshot,
    () => SERVER_SAVED_TOOLS
  );
  const isHydrated = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeStorage, () => true, () => false);

  const dictionaryMap = useMemo<Record<string, UiDictionary>>(() => {
    const map: Record<string, UiDictionary> = { ...DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY };
    if (Array.isArray(initialDictionary)) {
      for (const row of initialDictionary) {
        if (row?.key) map[row.key] = row;
      }
    } else if (initialDictionary && typeof initialDictionary === 'object') {
      Object.assign(map, initialDictionary);
    }
    return map;
  }, [initialDictionary]);

  const setLang = useCallback((newLang: LanguageCode) => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(LANG_KEY, newLang);
      notifyStorageChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[AppContext] Persist lang error:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  const setContentLangFilter = useCallback((filter: ContentLangFilter) => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(FILTER_KEY, filter);
      notifyStorageChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[AppContext] Persist filter error:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleSavedTool = useCallback((id: string) => {
    const current = getSavedToolsSnapshot();
    const exists = current.includes(id);
    const next = exists ? current.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...current, id];
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(SAVED_TOOLS_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
      notifyStorageChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[AppContext] Persist tools error:', err);
    }
  }, []);

  const isToolSaved = useCallback(
    (id: string) => savedToolIds.includes(id),
    [savedToolIds]
  );

  const t = useCallback(
    (key: string, fallback?: string): string => {
      const row = dictionaryMap[key];
      if (row) {
        const activeVal = row[lang]?.trim();
        if (activeVal) return activeVal;
        const enVal = row.en?.trim();
        if (enVal) return enVal;
        const bmVal = row.bm?.trim();
        if (bmVal) return bmVal;
        const cnVal = row.cn?.trim();
        if (cnVal) return cnVal;
        const taVal = row.ta?.trim();
        if (taVal) return taVal;
      }
      return fallback !== undefined ? fallback : key;
    },
    [dictionaryMap, lang]
  );

  const boundGetLocalized = useCallback(
    (obj?: Partial<LocalizedString> | null, targetLang?: LanguageCode): string => {
      return getLocalized(obj, targetLang, lang);
    },
    [lang]
  );

  const contextValue = useMemo<AppContextType>(
    () => ({
      lang,
      setLang,
      contentLangFilter,
      setContentLangFilter,
      savedToolIds,
      toggleSavedTool,
      isToolSaved,
      t,
      getLocalized: boundGetLocalized,
      dictionary: dictionaryMap,
      isHydrated,
    }),
    [
      lang,
      setLang,
      contentLangFilter,
      setContentLangFilter,
      savedToolIds,
      toggleSavedTool,
      isToolSaved,
      t,
      boundGetLocalized,
      dictionaryMap,
      isHydrated,
    ]
  );

  return <AppContext.Provider value={contextValue}>{children}</AppContext.Provider>;
}

export function useApp(): AppContextType {
  const context = useContext(AppContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useApp must be used within an AppProvider');
  }
  return context;
}

export { DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY };

export const AppContext = createContext<AppContextType | undefined>(undefined);
</file>

@import "tailwindcss";

:root {
  --background: #ffffff;
  --foreground: #09090b;

  --muted: #f4f4f5;

@theme inline {
  --color-background: var(--background);
  --color-foreground: var(--foreground);
  --color-card: var(--card);
  --color-card-foreground: var(--card-foreground);

  color: var(--foreground);
  font-family: var(--font-sans), -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", Roboto, Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif;
  -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
  -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/AdminPinLogin.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Lock, Unlock, ArrowLeft, Delete, Sparkles, AlertCircle, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react';
import { verifyPinAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';
import type { AdminSession } from '@/types/app';

const KEYPAD_BUTTONS = [
  { label: '1', value: '1' },
  { label: '2', value: '2' },
  { label: '3', value: '3' },
  { label: '4', value: '4' },
  { label: '5', value: '5' },
  { label: '6', value: '6' },
  { label: '7', value: '7' },
  { label: '8', value: '8' },
  { label: '9', value: '9' },
  { label: 'C', value: 'clear', isAction: true },
  { label: '0', value: '0' },
  { label: '⌫', value: 'backspace', isAction: true },
];

interface AdminPinLoginProps {
  onLoginSuccess: (session: AdminSession) => void;
}

export function AdminPinLogin({ onLoginSuccess }: AdminPinLoginProps) {
  const [pin, setPin] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [shake, setShake] = useState(false);

  const handleSubmit = useCallback(
    async (pinToVerify?: string) => {
      const targetPin = (pinToVerify !== undefined ? pinToVerify : pin).trim();
      if (!targetPin) {
        setError('Please enter your 6-digit access PIN');
        setShake(true);
        setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500);
        return;
      }

      setLoading(true);
      setError(null);

      try {
        const res = await verifyPinAction(targetPin);
        if (res.success && res.session) {
          onLoginSuccess(res.session);
        } else {
          const errMsg = !res.success ? res.error : 'Invalid or deactivated PIN';
          setError(errMsg);
          setShake(true);
          setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500);
          setPin('');
        }
      } catch (err) {
        setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Authentication service error');
        setShake(true);
        setTimeout(() => setShake(false), 500);
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    },
    [pin, onLoginSuccess]
  );

  const handleDigit = useCallback(
    (digit: string) => {
      if (loading) return;
      setError(null);
      setPin((prev) => {
        if (prev.length >= 8) return prev;
        const next = prev + digit;
        if (next.length === 6) {
          setTimeout(() => handleSubmit(next), 80);
        }
        return next;
      });
    },
    [loading, handleSubmit]
  );

  const handleBackspace = useCallback(() => {
    if (loading) return;
    setError(null);
    setPin((prev) => prev.slice(0, -1));
  }, [loading]);

  const handleClear = useCallback(() => {
    if (loading) return;
    setError(null);
    setPin('');
  }, [loading]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.target instanceof HTMLInputElement || e.target instanceof HTMLTextAreaElement) {
        return;
      }
      if (/^[0-9]$/.test(e.key)) {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleDigit(e.key);
      } else if (e.key === 'Backspace') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleBackspace();
      } else if (e.key === 'Enter') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleSubmit();
      } else if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleClear();
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown);
  }, [handleDigit, handleBackspace, handleSubmit, handleClear]);

  return (
    <div className="min-h-[85vh] flex flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 py-8">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm mb-6 flex justify-between items-center">
        <Link
          href="/"
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 transition-colors"
        >
          <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          <span>View Live App</span>
        </Link>
        <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 uppercase tracking-widest">
          Secure Portal
        </span>
      </div>

      <div
        className={`w-full max-w-sm rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/90 dark:bg-zinc-900/90 backdrop-blur-xl p-6 sm:p-8 shadow-xl shadow-zinc-200/50 dark:shadow-black/50 transition-all ${
          shake ? 'animate-shake' : ''
        }`}
      >
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center mb-6">
          <AALogo size="lg" className="mb-3 shadow-lg shadow-blue-900/25" />
          <h1 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
            Admin Control Center
          </h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-1">
            Enter your 6-digit access PIN to unlock the dashboard
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* PIN Indicators */}
        <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col items-center">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
            {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((idx) => (
              <div
                key={idx}
                className={`h-4 w-4 rounded-full transition-all duration-200 ${
                  pin.length > idx
                    ? 'bg-amber-500 scale-110 shadow-sm shadow-amber-500/40'
                    : 'border-2 border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800'
                }`}
              />
            ))}
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div className="mt-4 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60">
              <AlertCircle className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Sleek Numeric Keypad */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 mb-5">
          {KEYPAD_BUTTONS.map((btn) => {
            const isBack = btn.value === 'backspace';
            const isClr = btn.value === 'clear';
            const handleClick = () => {
              if (isBack) handleBackspace();
              else if (isClr) handleClear();
              else handleDigit(btn.value);
            };

            return (
              <button
                key={btn.value}
                type="button"
                onClick={handleClick}
                disabled={loading}
                aria-label={btn.label}
                className={`flex h-13 sm:h-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl text-lg sm:text-xl font-semibold transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 select-none ${
                  btn.isAction
                    ? 'bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700'
                    : 'bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/90 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-700/80 hover:bg-white dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 shadow-sm'
                }`}
              >
                {isBack ? <Delete className="w-5 h-5" /> : btn.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Unlock Action Button */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => handleSubmit()}
          disabled={loading || pin.length === 0}
          className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 py-3 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-md shadow-orange-500/25 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all disabled:opacity-40 disabled:pointer-events-none"
        >
          {loading ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin" />
              <span>Verifying PIN...</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Unlock className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Unlock Dashboard</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>

        {/* Security Notice */}
        <div className="mt-5 pt-4 border-t border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800/80 text-center">
          <p className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
            Protected Admin Portal • Authorized Access Only
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/AdminTopBar.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import {
  Crown,
  ShieldCheck,
  Edit,
  ExternalLink,
  LogOut,
  Sun,
  Moon,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import type { AdminSession, AccessPinRole } from '@/types/app';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';

interface AdminTopBarProps {
  session: AdminSession;
  onLogout: () => void;
  loggingOut?: boolean;
}

export function AdminTopBar({ session, onLogout, loggingOut }: AdminTopBarProps) {
  const { theme, setTheme, resolvedTheme } = useTheme();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = React.useState(false);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  const isDark = mounted && (theme === 'dark' || resolvedTheme === 'dark');

  const roleConfig: Record<
    AccessPinRole,
    { label: string; badgeClass: string; icon: React.ReactNode }
  > = {
    superadmin: {
      label: 'Super Admin',
      badgeClass:
        'bg-purple-100 text-purple-800 border-purple-200 dark:bg-purple-950/70 dark:text-purple-300 dark:border-purple-800/60',
      icon: <Crown className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />,
    },
    admin: {
      label: 'Admin',
      badgeClass:
        'bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200 dark:bg-blue-950/70 dark:text-blue-300 dark:border-blue-800/60',
      icon: <ShieldCheck className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />,
    },
    editor: {
      label: 'Editor',
      badgeClass:
        'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/70 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800/60',
      icon: <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5 flex-shrink-0" />,
    },
  };

  const currentRole = roleConfig[session.role] || roleConfig.editor;

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 w-full border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/95 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 backdrop-blur-md transition-colors">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-3 sm:px-6">
        {/* Brand & User identity */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 sm:gap-3 min-w-0">
          <AALogo size="md" />

          <div className="flex flex-col min-w-0">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="truncate text-sm sm:text-base font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                {session.label || 'Team Leader'}
              </span>

              {/* Role Badge */}
              <span
                className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[11px] font-semibold border ${currentRole.badgeClass}`}
              >
                {currentRole.icon}
                <span>{currentRole.label}</span>
              </span>
            </div>
            <span className="hidden sm:inline-block text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
              Admin Control Center
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Right actions: View Live App + Theme Toggle + Logout */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          {/* View Live App */}
          <Link
            href="/"
            target="_blank"
            rel="noopener noreferrer"
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900 px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
            title="Open Live App in a new tab"
          >
            <ExternalLink className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-500" />
            <span className="hidden xs:inline sm:inline">View Live App</span>
            <span className="xs:hidden sm:hidden">Live</span>
          </Link>

          {/* Theme Toggle */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark')}
            className="flex h-8 w-8 sm:h-9 sm:w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors"
            aria-label="Toggle theme"
          >
            {mounted ? (
              isDark ? (
                <Sun className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-400" />
              ) : (
                <Moon className="h-4 w-4 text-zinc-600" />
              )
            ) : (
              <span className="h-4 w-4" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Logout */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onLogout}
            disabled={loggingOut}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/50 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 px-2.5 sm:px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-100 dark:hover:bg-red-900/40 transition-colors disabled:opacity-50"
            title="Log out of admin session"
          >
            <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
            <span className="hidden sm:inline">Logout</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/admin/EventHighlightModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { X, Calendar, AlertCircle, Upload, Loader2, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadMediaAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { CalendarEvent } from '@/types/app';

interface EventHighlightModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (event: CalendarEvent) => void;
  event?: CalendarEvent | null;
}

export function EventHighlightModal({
  isOpen,
  onClose,
  onSave,
  event,
}: EventHighlightModalProps) {
  const [date, setDate] = useState('');
  const [time, setTime] = useState('');
  const [speaker, setSpeaker] = useState('');
  const [venue, setVenue] = useState('');
  const [locationOrLink, setLocationOrLink] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState('');
  const [titleEn, setTitleEn] = useState('');
  const [titleCn, setTitleCn] = useState('');
  const [titleBm, setTitleBm] = useState('');
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (event) {
      setDate(event.date || '');
      setTime(event.time || '');
      setSpeaker(event.speaker || '');
      setVenue(event.venue || '');
      setLocationOrLink(event.location_or_link || '');
      setDescription(event.description || '');
      setImageUrl(event.image_url || '');
      setTitleEn(event.title?.en || '');
      setTitleCn(event.title?.cn || '');
      setTitleBm(event.title?.bm || '');
    } else {
      setDate('');
      setTime('');
      setSpeaker('');
      setVenue('');
      setLocationOrLink('');
      setDescription('');
      setImageUrl('');
      setTitleEn('');
      setTitleCn('');
      setTitleBm('');
    }
    setError(null);
  }, [event, isOpen]);

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      const res = await uploadMediaAction(formData);
      if (res.success && res.url) {
        setImageUrl(res.url);
      }
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!titleEn.trim()) {
      setError('English title is required');
      return;
    }
    if (!date.trim()) {
      setError('Date is required (YYYY-MM-DD)');
      return;
    }

    const newEvent: CalendarEvent = {
      id: event?.id || `event-${Date.now()}`,
      date: date.trim(),
      time: time.trim(),
      speaker: speaker.trim(),
      venue: venue.trim(),
      location_or_link: locationOrLink.trim(),
      description: description.trim(),
      image_url: imageUrl.trim(),
      title: {
        en: titleEn.trim(),
        bm: titleBm.trim() || titleEn.trim(),
        cn: titleCn.trim() || titleEn.trim(),
        ta: titleEn.trim(),
      },
    };

    onSave(newEvent);
    onClose();
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
            <h3 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
              {event ? 'Edit Special Event' : 'Add Special Event'}
            </h3>
          </div>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100">
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 space-y-3.5">
          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2.5 text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 rounded-xl">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              English Title *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={titleEn}
              onChange={(e) => setTitleEn(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. AA Annual Champions Rally & Gala"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 font-medium"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Chinese Title (optional)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={titleCn}
                onChange={(e) => setTitleCn(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. AA 团队年度领袖总动员"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                BM Title (optional)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={titleBm}
                onChange={(e) => setTitleBm(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. Perhimpunan Juara AA"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Date (YYYY-MM-DD) *
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={date}
                onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="2026-11-28"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 font-mono"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Time
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={time}
                onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="2:00 PM - 6:00 PM"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Speaker / Host
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={speaker}
                onChange={(e) => setSpeaker(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. President Council"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
              />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Venue Name
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={venue}
                onChange={(e) => setVenue(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="e.g. Sunway Pyramid Convention"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Address (for Google Maps / Waze) or Zoom URL
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={locationOrLink}
              onChange={(e) => setLocationOrLink(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Sunway Pyramid Convention Centre, Petaling Jaya"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Rich Description (supports line breaks)
            </label>
            <textarea
              rows={2}
              value={description}
              onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Event agenda, keynote topics, ticketing info..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 resize-none"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                Attached Event Image URL (optional)
              </label>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={uploading}
                className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-600 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1"
              >
                {uploading ? <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" /> : <Upload className="w-3 h-3" />}
                <span>Upload Image</span>
              </button>
              <input ref={fileInputRef} type="file" className="hidden" accept="image/*" onChange={handleFileUpload} />
            </div>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={imageUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setImageUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://images.unsplash.com/... or uploaded cover"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 active:scale-95"
            >
              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Save Event</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/RecurringRuleModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Loader2, Upload } from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadMediaAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { MeetingSeries, MeetingFrequency } from '@/types/app';

interface RecurringRuleModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (rule: MeetingSeries) => void;
  rule?: MeetingSeries | null;
}

const DOW_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 0, label: 'Sunday' },
  { value: 1, label: 'Monday' },
  { value: 2, label: 'Tuesday' },
  { value: 3, label: 'Wednesday' },
  { value: 4, label: 'Thursday' },
  { value: 5, label: 'Friday' },
  { value: 6, label: 'Saturday' },
];

const NTH_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 1, label: '1st Week' },
  { value: 2, label: '2nd Week' },
  { value: 3, label: '3rd Week' },
  { value: 4, label: '4th Week' },
  { value: 5, label: 'Last Week' },
];

export function RecurringRuleModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSave, rule }: RecurringRuleModalProps) {
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [frequency, setFrequency] = useState<MeetingFrequency>('weekly');
  const [date, setDate] = useState('');
  const [dayOfWeek, setDayOfWeek] = useState(4);
  const [nthWeek, setNthWeek] = useState(3);
  const [dayOfMonth, setDayOfMonth] = useState(15);
  const [time, setTime] = useState('');
  const [venue, setVenue] = useState('');
  const [addressOrLink, setAddressOrLink] = useState('');
  const [speaker, setSpeaker] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [imageUrl, setImageUrl] = useState('');
  const [isActive, setIsActive] = useState(true);

  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (rule) {
      setTitle(rule.title || '');
      setFrequency(rule.frequency || 'weekly');
      setDate(rule.date || '');
      setDayOfWeek(rule.day_of_week ?? 4);
      setNthWeek(rule.nth_week ?? 3);
      setDayOfMonth(rule.day_of_month ?? 15);
      setTime(rule.time || '');
      setVenue(rule.venue || '');
      setAddressOrLink(rule.address_or_link || '');
      setSpeaker(rule.speaker || '');
      setDescription(rule.description || '');
      setImageUrl(rule.image_url || '');
      setIsActive(rule.is_active !== false);
    } else {
      setTitle('');
      setFrequency('weekly');
      setDate('');
      setDayOfWeek(4);
      setNthWeek(3);
      setDayOfMonth(15);
      setTime('');
      setVenue('');
      setAddressOrLink('');
      setSpeaker('');
      setDescription('');
      setImageUrl('');
      setIsActive(true);
    }
  }, [rule, isOpen]);

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      const res = await uploadMediaAction(formData);
      if (res.success && res.url) {
        setImageUrl(res.url);
      }
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim()) return;

    onSave({
      id: rule?.id || `rule-${Date.now()}`,
      title: title.trim(),
      frequency,
      date: frequency === 'once' ? date.trim() : undefined,
      day_of_week:
        frequency === 'weekly' || frequency === 'monthly_nth_weekday' ? dayOfWeek : undefined,
      nth_week: frequency === 'monthly_nth_weekday' ? nthWeek : undefined,
      day_of_month: frequency === 'monthly_date' ? dayOfMonth : undefined,
      time: time.trim(),
      venue: venue.trim(),
      address_or_link: addressOrLink.trim(),
      speaker: speaker.trim(),
      description: description.trim(),
      image_url: imageUrl.trim(),
      is_active: isActive,
    });
    onClose();
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
          <h3 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
            {rule ? 'Edit Recurring Meeting' : 'Add Recurring Meeting'}
          </h3>
          <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200">
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-3.5">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Meeting Title *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. Daily Core Check-in (DCC) or Weekly ASAP"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Pattern / Frequency *
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFrequency('daily')}
                className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all text-center ${
                  frequency === 'daily' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500 shadow-xs' : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
                }`}
              >
                Daily
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFrequency('weekly')}
                className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all text-center ${
                  frequency === 'weekly' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500 shadow-xs' : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
                }`}
              >
                Weekly
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFrequency('monthly_nth_weekday')}
                className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all text-center ${
                  frequency === 'monthly_nth_weekday' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500 shadow-xs' : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
                }`}
              >
                Monthly Nth Day
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFrequency('once')}
                className={`py-1.5 px-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border transition-all text-center ${
                  frequency === 'once' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500 shadow-xs' : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
                }`}
              >
                One-Time
              </button>
            </div>
            <div className="mt-1.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFrequency('monthly_date')}
                className={`py-1 px-2.5 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-bold border transition-all ${
                  frequency === 'monthly_date' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-500' : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-500'
                }`}
              >
                Or Day X of Month
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          {frequency === 'once' && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Date (YYYY-MM-DD) *</label>
              <input
                type="date"
                required
                value={date}
                onChange={(e) => setDate(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          {frequency === 'weekly' && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Day of Week</label>
              <select value={dayOfWeek} onChange={(e) => setDayOfWeek(Number(e.target.value))} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs">
                {DOW_OPTIONS.map((o) => (<option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>))}
              </select>
            </div>
          )}

          {frequency === 'monthly_nth_weekday' && (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Which Week</label>
                <select value={nthWeek} onChange={(e) => setNthWeek(Number(e.target.value))} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs">
                  {NTH_OPTIONS.map((o) => (<option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label} Week</option>))}
                </select>
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Day of Week</label>
                <select value={dayOfWeek} onChange={(e) => setDayOfWeek(Number(e.target.value))} className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs">
                  {DOW_OPTIONS.map((o) => (<option key={o.value} value={o.value}>{o.label}</option>))}
                </select>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {frequency === 'monthly_date' && (
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Day of Month (1 - 31)</label>
              <input
                type="number"
                min={1}
                max={31}
                value={dayOfMonth}
                onChange={(e) => setDayOfMonth(Number(e.target.value))}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Time</label>
              <input type="text" value={time} onChange={(e) => setTime(e.target.value)} placeholder="7:30 AM - 8:00 AM" className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Speaker</label>
              <input type="text" value={speaker} onChange={(e) => setSpeaker(e.target.value)} placeholder="COEV Leaders" className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs" />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Venue Name</label>
            <input type="text" value={venue} onChange={(e) => setVenue(e.target.value)} placeholder="Zoom Online Meeting or Wisma Central" className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Maps Address or Zoom URL</label>
            <input type="text" value={addressOrLink} onChange={(e) => setAddressOrLink(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://zoom.us/... or Jalan Ampang, KL" className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">Rich Description</label>
            <textarea rows={2} value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} placeholder="Agenda and notes..." className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs resize-none" />
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">Attached Image URL (optional)</label>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()} disabled={uploading} className="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-600 hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                {uploading ? <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" /> : <Upload className="w-3 h-3" />}
                <span>Upload</span>
              </button>
              <input ref={fileInputRef} type="file" className="hidden" accept="image/*" onChange={handleFileUpload} />
            </div>
            <input type="text" value={imageUrl} onChange={(e) => setImageUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://..." className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs" />
          </div>

          <div className="pt-1">
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 cursor-pointer text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={isActive} onChange={(e) => setIsActive(e.target.checked)} className="rounded text-amber-500" />
              <span>Active in monthly calendar expansion</span>
            </label>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100">Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 active:scale-95">
              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Save Recurring Meeting</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
