<file path="src/components/admin/TabSectionsTools.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  Folder,
  Wrench,
  Plus,
  ArrowUp,
  ArrowDown,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Edit,
  Trash2,
  Star,
  Search,
  Check,
  AlertCircle,
  Video,
  FileText,
  Music,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  Image as ImageIcon,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { SectionModal } from './SectionModal';
import { ToolModal } from './ToolModal';
import { ToolAdminCard } from './ToolAdminCard';
import {
  upsertSectionAction,
  deleteSectionAction,
  reorderSectionsAction,
  upsertToolAction,
  deleteToolAction,
} from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { Section, Tool } from '@/types/app';

interface TabSectionsToolsProps {
  sections: Section[];
  setSections: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Section[]>>;
  tools: Tool[];
  setTools: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<Tool[]>>;
  onNotify: (msg: string, isError?: boolean) => void;
}

export function TabSectionsTools({
  sections,
  setSections,
  tools,
  setTools,
  onNotify,
}: TabSectionsToolsProps) {
  const [subTab, setSubTab] = useState<'sections' | 'tools'>('sections');

  // Modals state
  const [sectionModalOpen, setSectionModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingSection, setEditingSection] = useState<Section | null>(null);

  const [toolModalOpen, setToolModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingTool, setEditingTool] = useState<Tool | null>(null);

  // Tools filter state
  const [selectedSectionFilter, setSelectedSectionFilter] = useState<string>('all');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');

  // Reorder sections
  const handleMoveSection = async (index: number, direction: 'up' | 'down') => {
    const targetIndex = direction === 'up' ? index - 1 : index + 1;
    if (targetIndex < 0 || targetIndex >= sections.length) return;

    const reordered = [...sections];
    const [moved] = reordered.splice(index, 1);
    reordered.splice(targetIndex, 0, moved);

    const updated = reordered.map((sec, idx) => ({ ...sec, sort_order: idx + 1 }));
    setSections(updated);

    const res = await reorderSectionsAction(updated.map((s) => ({ id: s.id, sort_order: s.sort_order })));
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify('Sections reordered successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to save order in database', true);
    }
  };

  // Toggle section visibility
  const handleToggleSectionVisibility = async (sec: Section) => {
    const newVis = !sec.is_visible;
    const updated = sections.map((s) => (s.id === sec.id ? { ...s, is_visible: newVis } : s));
    setSections(updated);

    const res = await upsertSectionAction({ ...sec, is_visible: newVis });
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify(`Section is now ${newVis ? 'visible' : 'hidden'}`);
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to update visibility', true);
    }
  };

  // Delete section
  const handleDeleteSection = async (sec: Section) => {
    if (!confirm(`Are you sure you want to delete section "${sec.title.en}"? All associated tools will also be deleted.`)) {
      return;
    }

    const res = await deleteSectionAction(sec.id);
    if (res.success) {
      setSections((prev) => prev.filter((s) => s.id !== sec.id));
      setTools((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.section_id !== sec.id));
      onNotify('Section and tools deleted successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to delete section', true);
    }
  };

  // Save section
  const handleSaveSection = async (payload: Partial<Section>): Promise<boolean> => {
    const res = await upsertSectionAction(payload);
    if (res.success && res.data) {
      const saved = res.data;
      setSections((prev) => {
        const exists = prev.some((s) => s.id === saved.id);
        if (exists) {
          return prev.map((s) => (s.id === saved.id ? saved : s));
        }
        return [...prev, saved];
      });
      onNotify('Section saved successfully');
      return true;
    }
    const errMsg = !res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to save section';
    onNotify(errMsg, true);
    return false;
  };

  // Toggle tool featured
  const handleToggleToolFeatured = async (tool: Tool) => {
    const newFeatured = !tool.is_featured;
    setTools((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === tool.id ? { ...t, is_featured: newFeatured } : t)));

    const res = await upsertToolAction({ ...tool, is_featured: newFeatured });
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify(`Tool ${newFeatured ? 'featured on top' : 'unfeatured'}`);
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to update tool', true);
    }
  };

  // Toggle tool visibility
  const handleToggleToolVisibility = async (tool: Tool) => {
    const newVis = !tool.is_visible;
    setTools((prev) => prev.map((t) => (t.id === tool.id ? { ...t, is_visible: newVis } : t)));

    const res = await upsertToolAction({ ...tool, is_visible: newVis });
    if (res.success) {
      onNotify(`Tool is now ${newVis ? 'visible' : 'hidden'}`);
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to update tool visibility', true);
    }
  };

  // Delete tool
  const handleDeleteTool = async (tool: Tool) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete tool "${tool.title.en}"?`)) return;

    const res = await deleteToolAction(tool.id);
    if (res.success) {
      setTools((prev) => prev.filter((t) => t.id !== tool.id));
      onNotify('Tool deleted successfully');
    } else {
      onNotify(!res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to delete tool', true);
    }
  };

  // Save tool
  const handleSaveTool = async (payload: Partial<Tool>): Promise<boolean> => {
    const res = await upsertToolAction(payload);
    if (res.success && res.data) {
      const saved = res.data;
      setTools((prev) => {
        const exists = prev.some((t) => t.id === saved.id);
        if (exists) {
          return prev.map((t) => (t.id === saved.id ? saved : t));
        }
        return [saved, ...prev];
      });
      onNotify('Tool saved successfully');
      return true;
    }
    const errMsg = !res.success ? res.error : 'Failed to save tool';
    onNotify(errMsg, true);
    return false;
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Subtab Toggle & Global Add Buttons */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
        <div className="flex rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 p-1 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSubTab('sections')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              subTab === 'sections'
                ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Folder className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
            <span>Manage Sections ({sections.length})</span>
          </button>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setSubTab('tools')}
            className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
              subTab === 'tools'
                ? 'bg-white text-zinc-950 shadow-sm dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50'
                : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-200'
            }`}
          >
            <Wrench className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500" />
            <span>Manage Tools ({tools.length})</span>
          </button>
        </div>

        <div>
          {subTab === 'sections' ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setEditingSection(null);
                setSectionModalOpen(true);
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Add Section</span>
            </button>
          ) : (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setEditingTool(null);
                setToolModalOpen(true);
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-sm transition-all active:scale-95"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
              <span>Add Tool</span>
            </button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>

      {subTab === 'sections' ? (
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-500">
            <span>Drag or use arrows to reorder sections. Changes save immediately.</span>
            <span>{sections.length} total sections</span>
          </div>

          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            {sections.map((sec, idx) => {
              const secToolsCount = tools.filter((t) => t.section_id === sec.id).length;
              return (
                <div
                  key={sec.id}
                  className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-all gap-3"
                >
                  <div className="flex items-start sm:items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                    <div className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 items-center justify-center">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleMoveSection(idx, 'up')}
                        disabled={idx === 0}
                        aria-label="Move Up"
                        className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-20"
                      >
                        <ArrowUp className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleMoveSection(idx, 'down')}
                        disabled={idx === sections.length - 1}
                        aria-label="Move Down"
                        className="p-1 rounded-lg text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 disabled:opacity-20"
                      >
                        <ArrowDown className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex h-10 w-10 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 font-bold flex-shrink-0">
                      <Folder className="w-5 h-5" />
                    </div>

                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                          {sec.title?.en || 'Untitled'}
                        </span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">/{sec.slug}</span>
                        <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full text-[10px] font-semibold bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                          {secToolsCount} {secToolsCount === 1 ? 'tool' : 'tools'}
                        </span>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 flex-wrap">
                        {sec.title?.bm && <span>BM: {sec.title.bm}</span>}
                        {sec.title?.cn && <span>• 中文: {sec.title.cn}</span>}
                        {sec.title?.ta && <span>• தமிழ்: {sec.title.ta}</span>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 self-end sm:self-center">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleToggleSectionVisibility(sec)}
                      className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
                        sec.is_visible
                          ? 'bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-900'
                          : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-500 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:border-zinc-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {sec.is_visible ? <Eye className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> : <EyeOff className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />}
                      <span>{sec.is_visible ? 'Visible' : 'Hidden'}</span>
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setEditingSection(sec);
                        setSectionModalOpen(true);
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
                      title="Edit Section"
                    >
                      <Edit className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>

                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDeleteSection(sec)}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-xl text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 transition-colors"
                      title="Delete Section"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Filter Bar */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 items-stretch sm:items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2 flex-1">
              <div className="relative flex-1">
                <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400" />
                <input
                  type="text"
                  value={searchQuery}
                  onChange={(e) => setSearchQuery(e.target.value)}
                  placeholder="Search tools by title or speaker..."
                  className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 shadow-sm"
                />
              </div>

              <select
                value={selectedSectionFilter}
                onChange={(e) => setSelectedSectionFilter(e.target.value)}
                className="rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 shadow-sm"
              >
                <option value="all">All Sections ({tools.length})</option>
                {sections.map((sec) => (
                  <option key={sec.id} value={sec.id}>
                    {sec.title?.en || sec.slug}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tools Grid */}
          {(() => {
            const filteredTools = tools.filter((tool) => {
              if (selectedSectionFilter !== 'all' && tool.section_id !== selectedSectionFilter) {
                return false;
              }
              if (searchQuery.trim()) {
                const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
                const matchSpeaker = tool.speaker_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
                const matchTitle = Object.values(tool.title || {}).some(
                  (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
                );
                return matchSpeaker || matchTitle;
              }
              return true;
            });

            if (filteredTools.length === 0) {
              return (
                <div className="text-center py-12 rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/50 p-6">
                  <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-500">No tools match your criteria.</p>
                </div>
              );
            }

            return (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
                {filteredTools.map((tool) => {
                  const section = sections.find((s) => s.id === tool.section_id);
                  return (
                    <ToolAdminCard
                      key={tool.id}
                      tool={tool}
                      sectionName={section?.title?.en || 'Section'}
                      onToggleFeatured={handleToggleToolFeatured}
                      onToggleVisibility={handleToggleToolVisibility}
                      onEdit={(t) => {
                        setEditingTool(t);
                        setToolModalOpen(true);
                      }}
                      onDelete={handleDeleteTool}
                    />
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            );
          })()}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modals */}
      <SectionModal
        isOpen={sectionModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setSectionModalOpen(false)}
        onSave={handleSaveSection}
        section={editingSection}
      />

      <ToolModal
        isOpen={toolModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setToolModalOpen(false)}
        onSave={handleSaveTool}
        tool={editingTool}
        sections={sections}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/InstallAppBanner.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { Share, PlusSquare, X, Download } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';

interface BeforeInstallPromptEvent extends Event {
  prompt: () => Promise<void>;
  userChoice: Promise<{ outcome: 'accepted' | 'dismissed'; platform: string }>;
}

const PWA_DISMISSED_KEY = 'teamhub_pwa_dismissed';
const PWA_EVENT = 'teamhub_pwa_event';

function notifyPwaChange() {
  if (typeof window !== 'undefined') window.dispatchEvent(new Event(PWA_EVENT));
}

function subscribePwa(callback: () => void) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return () => {};
  window.addEventListener('storage', callback);
  window.addEventListener(PWA_EVENT, callback);
  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener('storage', callback);
    window.removeEventListener(PWA_EVENT, callback);
  };
}

function getDismissedSnapshot(): boolean {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(PWA_DISMISSED_KEY) === 'true';
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

function getStandaloneSnapshot(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return true;
  return (
    window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
    Boolean((navigator as unknown as { standalone?: boolean }).standalone)
  );
}

function getIsIosSnapshot(): boolean {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return false;
  return /iphone|ipad|ipod/.test(window.navigator.userAgent.toLowerCase());
}

export function InstallAppBanner() {
  const { t, isHydrated } = useApp();
  const [deferredPrompt, setDeferredPrompt] = useState<BeforeInstallPromptEvent | null>(null);

  const isDismissed = useSyncExternalStore(subscribePwa, getDismissedSnapshot, () => true);
  const isStandalone = useSyncExternalStore(subscribePwa, getStandaloneSnapshot, () => true);
  const isIOS = useSyncExternalStore(subscribePwa, getIsIosSnapshot, () => false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const onPrompt = (e: Event) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      setDeferredPrompt(e as BeforeInstallPromptEvent);
    };

    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', onPrompt);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', onPrompt);
  }, []);

  const handleDismiss = () => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(PWA_DISMISSED_KEY, 'true');
      notifyPwaChange();
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[InstallAppBanner] Persist failed:', err);
    }
  };

  const handleInstallClick = async () => {
    if (!deferredPrompt) return;
    try {
      await deferredPrompt.prompt();
      const choice = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
      if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') handleDismiss();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('[InstallAppBanner] Prompt error:', err);
    } finally {
      setDeferredPrompt(null);
    }
  };

  if (!isHydrated || isDismissed || isStandalone || (!isIOS && !deferredPrompt)) {
    return null;
  }

  return (
    <aside
      aria-label="Install App"
      className="fixed bottom-4 left-3 right-3 sm:left-auto sm:right-6 sm:max-w-md z-50 animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-bottom-4 duration-300"
    >
      <div className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/95 dark:bg-zinc-900/95 p-4 shadow-2xl backdrop-blur-md">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleDismiss}
          aria-label={t('close')}
          className="absolute top-3 right-3 p-1 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-500 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
        >
          <X className="w-4 h-4" />
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-start gap-3.5 pr-6">
          <AALogo size="lg" />
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 leading-snug">
              {t('install_app')}
            </h3>
            <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 leading-relaxed">
              {t('pwa_banner_desc')}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="mt-3.5 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
          {isIOS ? (
            <div className="space-y-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                  1
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-medium">
                  {t('pwa_ios_share_step')}
                  <Share className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-500 inline-block" />
                </span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className="flex h-5 w-5 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-[10px] font-bold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">
                  2
                </span>
                <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-medium">
                  {t('pwa_ios_add_step')}
                  <PlusSquare className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 inline-block" />
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : deferredPrompt ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleInstallClick}
                className="flex-1 inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-semibold shadow hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-100 transition-colors"
              >
                <Download className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {t('add_to_home_screen')}
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDismiss}
                className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 transition-colors"
              >
                {t('dismiss')}
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
              <span className="font-medium">{t('add_to_home_screen')}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleDismiss}
                className="font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 hover:underline"
              >
                {t('dismiss')}
              </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/SectionControls.tsx">
'use client';

import React from 'react';
import {
  Search,
  X,
  Share2,
  Layers,
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Star,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp, type ContentLangFilter } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { Section } from '@/types/app';

export const SECTION_ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Star,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
};

interface SectionControlsProps {
  searchQuery: string;
  onSearchChange: (q: string) => void;
  visibleSections: Section[];
  activeSectionId: string;
  onSelectSection: (id: string) => void;
  contentLangFilter: ContentLangFilter;
  onContentLangChange: (lang: ContentLangFilter) => void;
  sectionToolCounts: Record<string, number>;
  totalToolsCount: number;
  activeSection: Section | null;
  activeSectionToolsCount: number;
  onShareFullSection: () => void;
  filteredCount?: number;
}

export function SectionControls({
  searchQuery,
  onSearchChange,
  visibleSections,
  activeSectionId,
  onSelectSection,
  contentLangFilter,
  onContentLangChange,
  sectionToolCounts,
  totalToolsCount,
  activeSection,
  activeSectionToolsCount,
  onShareFullSection,
  filteredCount = 0,
}: SectionControlsProps) {
  const { t, getLocalized } = useApp();

  return (
    <div className="space-y-3.5 pt-1">
      {/* 1) Single Smart Bar */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute left-3.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-4 w-4 text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500" />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={searchQuery}
            onChange={(e) => onSearchChange(e.target.value)}
            placeholder={t('search_placeholder', 'Search topic, speaker, or tool...')}
            className="h-11 w-full rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200/90 bg-white pl-10 pr-9 text-xs sm:text-sm font-medium text-zinc-950 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:border-[#1E40AF] focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#1E40AF] dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 dark:placeholder:text-zinc-500 shadow-xs"
          />
          {searchQuery && (
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSearchChange('')}
              aria-label="Clear search"
              className="absolute right-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-300 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400"
            >
              <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* Compact 3-Button Content Language Toggle */}
        <div className="flex items-center rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 p-1 dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800/80 flex-shrink-0">
          {[
            { id: 'all' as ContentLangFilter, label: t('all', 'All') },
            { id: 'en' as ContentLangFilter, label: 'EN' },
            { id: 'cn' as ContentLangFilter, label: '中文' },
          ].map((item) => {
            const isSelected = contentLangFilter === item.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={item.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => onContentLangChange(item.id)}
                className={`h-9 min-w-[36px] sm:min-w-[42px] px-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all active:scale-95 ${
                  isSelected
                    ? 'bg-[#1E40AF] text-white shadow-xs'
                    : 'text-zinc-600 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:text-zinc-100'
                }`}
              >
                {item.label}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* 2) Dynamic Section Selector Grid / Segmented Cards */}
      <div className="no-scrollbar flex items-center gap-2 overflow-x-auto px-4 py-1">
        {/* All Section Card */}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => onSelectSection('all')}
          className={`flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
            activeSectionId === 'all'
              ? 'bg-[#1E40AF] text-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-[#1E40AF]'
              : 'bg-white text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-200/90 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-xs'
          }`}
        >
          <div
            className={`flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${
              activeSectionId === 'all'
                ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300'
            }`}
          >
            <Layers className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </div>
          <span>{t('all', 'All')}</span>
          <span
            className={`rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black ${
              activeSectionId === 'all'
                ? 'bg-white text-[#1E40AF]'
                : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400'
            }`}
          >
            {totalToolsCount}
          </span>
        </button>

        {/* Dynamic Visible Sections from Supabase */}
        {visibleSections.map((sec) => {
          const isActive = activeSectionId === sec.id;
          const IconComp = SECTION_ICON_MAP[sec.icon_name] || Folder;
          const sectionTitle = getLocalized(sec.title) || sec.slug;
          const count = sectionToolCounts[sec.id] || 0;

          return (
            <button
              key={sec.id}
              type="button"
              onClick={() => onSelectSection(sec.id)}
              className={`flex flex-shrink-0 items-center gap-2 rounded-2xl px-3.5 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold transition-all duration-200 active:scale-95 ${
                isActive
                  ? 'bg-[#1E40AF] text-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-[#1E40AF]'
                  : 'bg-white text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-300 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 border border-zinc-200/90 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-xs'
              }`}
            >
              <div
                className={`flex h-6 w-6 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-white/20 text-white'
                    : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-300'
                }`}
              >
                <IconComp className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </div>
              <span className="truncate max-w-[130px]">{sectionTitle}</span>
              <span
                className={`rounded-full px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-black ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-white text-[#1E40AF]'
                    : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-600 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400'
                }`}
              >
                {count}
              </span>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>

      {/* 3) Sleek, Compact Section Header Row */}
      {!searchQuery && (
        <div className="px-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-blue-50/70 via-white to-blue-50/30 dark:from-zinc-900/90 dark:via-zinc-900 dark:to-blue-950/20 border border-blue-100/80 dark:border-zinc-800 px-3.5 py-2.5 shadow-xs">
            {/* Left side: Title + count badge + 1-line subtitle */}
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <h2 className="text-sm sm:text-base font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 truncate">
                  {activeSection
                    ? getLocalized(activeSection.title) || activeSection.slug
                    : t('all', 'All Resources')}
                </h2>
                <span className="text-[11px] font-bold text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-500 flex-shrink-0">
                  ({activeSectionToolsCount})
                </span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 truncate leading-tight">
                {activeSection
                  ? getLocalized(activeSection.description) ||
                    'Browse curated resources and tools for this category'
                  : 'Access all team onboarding, prospecting, eBooks, and audios'}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Right side: Small compact pill button */}
            {activeSectionToolsCount > 0 && (
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={onShareFullSection}
                className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-[#25D366] hover:bg-[#20bd5a] text-white text-xs font-bold inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-shrink-0 shadow-sm active:scale-95 transition-all select-none"
              >
                <Share2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                <span>{t('btn_share_section', 'Share Set')}</span>
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Search Active Notification */}
      {searchQuery && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-4 text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
          <span>
            Searching &quot;{searchQuery}&quot; across all sections ({filteredCount} results)
          </span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => onSearchChange('')}
            className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 dark:text-blue-400 hover:underline"
          >
            Clear search
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/lib/calendarGenerator.ts">
import type {
  MeetingSeries,
  MeetingException,
  BlackoutDate,
  CalendarEvent,
  TeamCalendar,
} from '@/types/app';

export interface GeneratedScheduleEvent extends CalendarEvent {
  series_id?: string;
  rule_id?: string;
  is_recurring?: boolean;
  venue?: string;
  is_modified?: boolean;
  is_blackout?: boolean;
  original_date?: string;
  reason?: string;
}

/**
 * Calculates the ISO cutoff date (YYYY-MM-01) for the first day of two months prior.
 */
export function getTwoMonthsPriorCutoff(baseDate: Date = new Date()): string {
  const year = baseDate.getFullYear();
  const month = baseDate.getMonth();
  const cutoff = new Date(year, month - 2, 1);
  const cutoffYear = cutoff.getFullYear();
  const cutoffMonth = String(cutoff.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
  return `${cutoffYear}-${cutoffMonth}-01`;
}

/**
 * Prunes one-time meeting series, exceptions, blackout dates, and legacy events
 * older than the 2-month prior cutoff.
 */
export function pruneCalendarData(calendar: Partial<TeamCalendar>): Partial<TeamCalendar> {
  const cutoff = getTwoMonthsPriorCutoff();

  const recurringRules = (calendar.recurring_rules || []).filter((series) => {
    if (series.frequency === 'once') {
      return !series.date || series.date >= cutoff;
    }
    return true;
  });

  const exceptions = (calendar.exceptions || []).filter((ex) => {
    const checkDate = ex.new_date || ex.original_date;
    return !checkDate || checkDate >= cutoff;
  });

  const blackoutDates = (calendar.blackout_dates || []).filter((b) => {
    return !b.date || b.date >= cutoff;
  });

  const events = (calendar.events || []).filter((e) => {
    return !e.date || e.date >= cutoff;
  });

  return {
    ...calendar,
    recurring_rules: recurringRules,
    exceptions,
    blackout_dates: blackoutDates,
    events,
  };
}

/**
 * Expands active meeting series for a given year & month (1-indexed),
 * applies schedule overrides & blackout dates, merges events, and sorts.
 */
export function generateMonthSchedule(
  year: number,
  month: number,
  meetingSeries: MeetingSeries[] = [],
  blackoutDates: BlackoutDate[] = [],
  oneOffEvents: CalendarEvent[] = [],
  exceptions: MeetingException[] = []
): GeneratedScheduleEvent[] {
  const daysInMonth = new Date(year, month, 0).getDate();
  const schedule: GeneratedScheduleEvent[] = [];
  const monthPrefix = `${year}-${String(month).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  const exceptionMap = new Map<string, MeetingException>();
  for (const ex of exceptions) {
    exceptionMap.set(`${ex.series_id}::${ex.original_date}`, ex);
  }

  // 1. Expand recurring & once rules for each day in the month
  for (let day = 1; day <= daysInMonth; day++) {
    const dateStr = `${monthPrefix}-${String(day).padStart(2, '0')}`;
    const dateObj = new Date(year, month - 1, day);
    const dayOfWeek = dateObj.getDay();
    const nthWeek = Math.floor((day - 1) / 7) + 1;
    const isLastNthWeek = day + 7 > daysInMonth;

    for (const series of meetingSeries) {
      if (!series.is_active) continue;

      let matchesDay = false;
      if (series.frequency === 'once') {
        matchesDay = series.date === dateStr;
      } else if (series.frequency === 'daily') {
        matchesDay = true;
      } else if (series.frequency === 'weekly') {
        matchesDay = series.day_of_week === dayOfWeek;
      } else if (series.frequency === 'monthly_nth_weekday') {
        matchesDay =
          series.day_of_week === dayOfWeek &&
          (series.nth_week === nthWeek || (series.nth_week === 5 && isLastNthWeek));
      } else if (series.frequency === 'monthly_date') {
        matchesDay = series.day_of_month === day;
      }

      if (!matchesDay) continue;

      const exception =
        exceptionMap.get(`${series.id}::${dateStr}`) ||
        exceptionMap.get(`all::${dateStr}`);

      if (exception?.action === 'cancel') continue;
      if (exception?.action === 'modify' && exception.new_date && exception.new_date !== dateStr) {
        continue;
      }

      const isBlackedOut = blackoutDates.some((b) => {
        if (b.date !== dateStr) return false;
        return !b.rule_id || b.rule_id === 'all' || b.rule_id === series.id;
      });
      if (isBlackedOut) continue;

      const titleEn = exception?.new_title || series.title;
      schedule.push({
        id: `rec-${series.id}-${dateStr}`,
        series_id: series.id,
        rule_id: series.id,
        date: dateStr,
        time: exception?.new_time || series.time,
        title: { en: titleEn, bm: titleEn, cn: titleEn, ta: titleEn },
        speaker: exception?.new_speaker !== undefined ? exception.new_speaker : series.speaker || '',
        venue: exception?.new_venue !== undefined ? exception.new_venue : series.venue || '',
        location_or_link:
          exception?.new_address_or_link !== undefined
            ? exception.new_address_or_link
            : series.address_or_link || '',
        description:
          exception?.new_description !== undefined ? exception.new_description : series.description || '',
        image_url:
          exception?.new_image_url !== undefined ? exception.new_image_url : series.image_url || '',
        is_recurring: series.frequency !== 'once',
        is_modified: Boolean(exception),
        original_date: dateStr,
        reason: exception?.reason,
      });
    }
  }

  // 2. Add rescheduled instances that were moved INTO this month
  for (const ex of exceptions) {
    if (ex.action !== 'modify' || !ex.new_date || !ex.new_date.startsWith(monthPrefix)) {
      continue;
    }
    if (ex.new_date === ex.original_date) {
      continue;
    }

    const parentSeries = meetingSeries.find((s) => s.id === ex.series_id);
    const baseTitle = ex.new_title || parentSeries?.title || 'Rescheduled Meeting';
    const baseSpeaker = ex.new_speaker !== undefined ? ex.new_speaker : parentSeries?.speaker || '';
    const baseVenue = ex.new_venue !== undefined ? ex.new_venue : parentSeries?.venue || '';
    const baseLink =
      ex.new_address_or_link !== undefined ? ex.new_address_or_link : parentSeries?.address_or_link || '';
    const baseDesc =
      ex.new_description !== undefined ? ex.new_description : parentSeries?.description || '';
    const baseImg = ex.new_image_url !== undefined ? ex.new_image_url : parentSeries?.image_url || '';

    schedule.push({
      id: `mod-${ex.id}-${ex.new_date}`,
      series_id: ex.series_id,
      rule_id: ex.series_id,
      date: ex.new_date,
      time: ex.new_time || parentSeries?.time || '',
      title: {
        en: baseTitle,
        bm: baseTitle,
        cn: baseTitle,
        ta: baseTitle,
      },
      speaker: baseSpeaker,
      venue: baseVenue,
      location_or_link: baseLink,
      description: baseDesc,
      image_url: baseImg,
      is_recurring: true,
      is_modified: true,
      original_date: ex.original_date,
      reason: ex.reason,
    });
  }

  // 3. Merge legacy one-off events occurring in this month
  for (const evt of oneOffEvents) {
    if (evt.date && evt.date.startsWith(monthPrefix)) {
      schedule.push({
        ...evt,
        is_recurring: false,
      });
    }
  }

  // 4. Sort chronologically by date then time
  schedule.sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.date !== b.date) {
      return a.date.localeCompare(b.date);
    }
    return (a.time || '').localeCompare(b.time || '');
  });

  return schedule;
}

/**
 * Convenient wrapper from a TeamCalendar object
 */
export function getEventsForCalendar(
  calendar: TeamCalendar | null | undefined,
  year: number,
  month: number
): GeneratedScheduleEvent[] {
  if (!calendar) return [];
  return generateMonthSchedule(
    year,
    month,
    calendar.recurring_rules || [],
    calendar.blackout_dates || [],
    calendar.events || [],
    calendar.exceptions || []
  );
}

/**
 * Divides generated events into:
 * 1) The next 2 upcoming meetings (from today's date forward)
 * 2) The rest of the meetings for this month
 */
export function splitUpcomingMeetings(
  events: GeneratedScheduleEvent[],
  todayIsoDate: string
): {
  nextTwo: GeneratedScheduleEvent[];
  remaining: GeneratedScheduleEvent[];
} {
  const upcoming = events.filter((e) => e.date >= todayIsoDate);
  const nextTwo = upcoming.slice(0, 2);
  const remaining = upcoming.slice(2);
  return { nextTwo, remaining };
}

/**
 * Unpacks any packed metadata from events JSONB if database columns were not migrated.
 */
export function unpackCalendar(cal: TeamCalendar): TeamCalendar {
  const meta = (cal.events || []).find((e) => e.id === '_meta_calendar');
  const cleanEvents = (cal.events || []).filter((e) => e.id !== '_meta_calendar');
  if (meta && meta.description) {
    try {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(meta.description);
      return {
        ...cal,
        events: cleanEvents,
        recurring_rules:
          cal.recurring_rules && cal.recurring_rules.length > 0
            ? cal.recurring_rules
            : parsed.recurring_rules || [],
        exceptions:
          cal.exceptions && cal.exceptions.length > 0 ? cal.exceptions : parsed.exceptions || [],
        blackout_dates:
          cal.blackout_dates && cal.blackout_dates.length > 0
            ? cal.blackout_dates
            : parsed.blackout_dates || [],
        default_view: cal.default_view || parsed.default_view || 'dynamic',
        last_reviewed_month: cal.last_reviewed_month || parsed.last_reviewed_month || '',
      };
    } catch {
      // ignore
    }
  }
  return {
    ...cal,
    events: cleanEvents,
  };
}

<file path="src/lib/supabase.ts">
import { createClient, SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database } from '@/types/app';

const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || 'https://placeholder.supabase.co';
const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || 'placeholder-anon-key';

/**
 * Public Supabase client for browser and unprivileged operations.
 * Operates with Anon key and respects Row Level Security (RLS) policies.
 */
export const supabase: SupabaseClient<Database> = createClient<Database>(
  supabaseUrl,
  supabaseAnonKey
);

/**
 * Helper to instantiate a public browser Supabase client.
 */
export function getSupabaseBrowserClient(): SupabaseClient<Database> {
  const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || supabaseUrl;
  const anonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || supabaseAnonKey;

  return createClient<Database>(url, anonKey);
}

/**
 * Factory for server-side admin client using the SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY.
 * Falls back to NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY if service role key is missing/invalid,
 * ensuring PIN verification and Admin operations never fail.
 *
 * NOTE: Strictly for use within Server Actions, Route Handlers, or trusted
 * server-side environments. NEVER import or call this on the client side.
 */
export function getSupabaseAdminClient(): SupabaseClient<Database> {
  const serviceKey =
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY &&
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY !== 'placeholder-service-role-key'
      ? process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY
      : process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || supabaseAnonKey;
  const url = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || supabaseUrl;

  return createClient<Database>(url, serviceKey, {
    auth: {
      autoRefreshToken: false,
      persistSession: false,
    },
  });
}

const defaultAdminKey =
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY &&
  process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY !== 'placeholder-service-role-key'
    ? process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY
    : process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || supabaseAnonKey;

/**
 * Server-side admin client instance for direct server action usage.
 * Falls back to Anon key when service role key is not configured.
 */
export const supabaseAdmin: SupabaseClient<Database> = createClient<Database>(
  supabaseUrl,
  defaultAdminKey,
  {
    auth: {
      autoRefreshToken: false,
      persistSession: false,
    },
  }
);
</file>

<file path="src/app/page.tsx">
export const instant = false;

import { connection } from 'next/server';
import { supabase, supabaseAdmin } from '@/lib/supabase';
import type { Section, Tool, TeamCalendar } from '@/types/app';
import {
  DEFAULT_SECTIONS,
  DEFAULT_TOOLS,
  DEFAULT_CALENDAR,
} from '@/lib/mockData';
import { MainView } from '@/components/MainView';
import { unpackCalendar } from '@/lib/calendarGenerator';

async function getAppData(): Promise<{
  sections: Section[];
  tools: Tool[];
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
}> {
  await connection();
  let sections: Section[] = DEFAULT_SECTIONS;
  let tools: Tool[] = DEFAULT_TOOLS;
  let calendar: TeamCalendar = DEFAULT_CALENDAR;

  try {
    const client = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ? supabaseAdmin : supabase;

    const [secRes, toolsRes, calRes] = await Promise.all([
      client.from('sections').select('*').order('sort_order', { ascending: true }),
      client.from('tools').select('*').order('sort_order', { ascending: true }),
      client.from('team_calendar').select('*').limit(1),
    ]);

    if (!secRes.error && secRes.data && secRes.data.length > 0) {
      sections = secRes.data as Section[];
    }

    if (!toolsRes.error && toolsRes.data && toolsRes.data.length > 0) {
      tools = toolsRes.data as Tool[];
    }

    if (!calRes.error && calRes.data && calRes.data.length > 0) {
      calendar = unpackCalendar(calRes.data[0] as TeamCalendar);
    }
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[Home page] Error loading Supabase data, using default seeds:', err);
  }

  return { sections, tools, calendar };
}

export default async function Home() {
  const { sections, tools, calendar } = await getAppData();

  return (
    <MainView
      initialSections={sections}
      initialTools={tools}
      initialCalendar={calendar}
    />
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/SectionModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { X, Check, Folder, Loader2, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';
import { LANGUAGES } from './ToolBasicInfo';
import type { Section, LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

const COMMON_ICONS = [
  'UserPlus',
  'Rocket',
  'Award',
  'Sparkles',
  'Folder',
  'BookOpen',
  'Video',
  'Calendar',
  'Star',
  'Compass',
  'Megaphone',
  'FileText',
];

interface SectionModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (section: Partial<Section>) => Promise<boolean>;
  section?: Section | null;
}

export function SectionModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSave, section }: SectionModalProps) {
  const [titles, setTitles] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [descriptions, setDescriptions] = useState<Record<LanguageCode, string>>({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [slug, setSlug] = useState('');
  const [iconName, setIconName] = useState('Folder');
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(true);

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (section) {
      setTitles({
        en: section.title?.en || '',
        bm: section.title?.bm || '',
        cn: section.title?.cn || '',
        ta: section.title?.ta || '',
      });
      setDescriptions({
        en: section.description?.en || '',
        bm: section.description?.bm || '',
        cn: section.description?.cn || '',
        ta: section.description?.ta || '',
      });
      setSlug(section.slug || '');
      setIconName(section.icon_name || 'Folder');
      setIsVisible(section.is_visible !== false);
    } else {
      setTitles({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setDescriptions({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
      setSlug('');
      setIconName('Folder');
      setIsVisible(true);
    }
    setError(null);
  }, [section, isOpen]);

  const handleSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!titles.en?.trim()) {
      setError('Section title in English is required.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);

    const generatedSlug =
      slug.trim() ||
      titles.en
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/^-|-$/g, '') ||
      `section-${Date.now()}`;

    const payload: Partial<Section> = {
      ...(section?.id ? { id: section.id } : {}),
      slug: generatedSlug,
      icon_name: iconName,
      is_visible: isVisible,
      title: {
        en: titles.en.trim(),
        bm: titles.bm.trim() || titles.en.trim(),
        cn: titles.cn.trim() || titles.en.trim(),
        ta: titles.ta.trim() || titles.en.trim(),
      },
      description: {
        en: descriptions.en.trim(),
        bm: descriptions.bm.trim() || descriptions.en.trim(),
        cn: descriptions.cn.trim() || descriptions.en.trim(),
        ta: descriptions.ta.trim() || descriptions.en.trim(),
      },
    };

    const success = await onSave(payload);
    setSaving(false);
    if (success) {
      onClose();
    } else {
      setError('Failed to save section. Please check all fields.');
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
              <Folder className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
              {section ? 'Edit Section' : 'Add New Section'}
            </h2>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-zinc-500 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4">
          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 rounded-2xl">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-3.5">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
                Icon Name
              </label>
              <select
                value={iconName}
                onChange={(e) => setIconName(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              >
                {COMMON_ICONS.map((icon) => (
                  <option key={icon} value={icon}>
                    {icon}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
                Custom Slug (Optional)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={slug}
                onChange={(e) => setSlug(e.target.value)}
                placeholder="auto-generated-from-title"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 placeholder:text-zinc-400 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsVisible(!isVisible)}
              className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
                isVisible
                  ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-900 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300 dark:border-emerald-800'
                  : 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-500 border-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500 dark:border-zinc-700'
              }`}
            >
              <span>{isVisible ? 'Section Visible to Users' : 'Section Hidden from Users'}</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {/* Stacked Titles & Descriptions on One Screen */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-2 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            {/* English Title */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                English Title *
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                required
                value={titles.en || ''}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  const val = e.target.value;
                  setTitles((prev) => ({ ...prev, en: val }));
                  if (!section || !slug || slug === titles.en.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '')) {
                    setSlug(val.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''));
                  }
                }}
                placeholder="e.g. Prospecting & Opening"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 font-medium"
              />
            </div>

            {/* BM Title */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                BM Title (optional)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={titles.bm || ''}
                onChange={(e) => setTitles((prev) => ({ ...prev, bm: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="e.g. Prospek & Pembuka"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Chinese Title */}
            <div>
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Chinese Title (optional)
              </label>
              <input
                type="text"
                value={titles.cn || ''}
                onChange={(e) => setTitles((prev) => ({ ...prev, cn: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="e.g. 开发市场与破冰话术"
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Optional Short Description */}
            <div className="pt-2">
              <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
                Section Description (English / Default)
              </label>
              <textarea
                rows={2}
                value={descriptions.en || ''}
                onChange={(e) => setDescriptions((prev) => ({ ...prev, en: e.target.value }))}
                placeholder="Short 1-sentence description for this section..."
                className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={saving}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"

              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"

              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 transition-all shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 disabled:opacity-50"

                  <span>{section ? 'Update Section' : 'Create Section'}</span>
                </>
              )}

      </div>

  );

<file path="src/components/MainView.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { Section, Tool, TeamCalendar } from '@/types/app';
import { Header } from '@/components/Header';
import { ToolCard } from '@/components/ToolCard';
import { SectionControls } from '@/components/SectionControls';
import { BottomNav, type NavTab } from '@/components/BottomNav';
import { CalendarTab } from '@/components/CalendarTab';
import { ShareAppTab } from '@/components/ShareAppTab';
import {
  Star,
  FolderX,
  Search,
  X,
  Share2,
  Layers,
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
} from 'lucide-react';

const SECTION_ICON_MAP: Record<string, React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>> = {
  UserPlus,
  Rocket,
  Award,
  Sparkles,
  Layers,
  Compass,
  Folder,
  BookOpen,
  Video,
  Calendar,
  Star,
  Megaphone,
  FileText,
};

interface MainViewProps {
  initialSections: Section[];
  initialTools: Tool[];
  initialCalendar: TeamCalendar;
}

export function MainView({
  initialSections,
  initialTools,
  initialCalendar,
}: MainViewProps) {
  const {
    lang,
    getLocalized,
    contentLangFilter,
    setContentLangFilter,
    savedToolIds,
    t,
  } = useApp();

  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<NavTab>('tools');
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState('');
  const [activeSectionId, setActiveSectionId] = useState<string>('all');

  // Filter visible sections dynamically from Supabase
  const visibleSections = useMemo(() => {
    return (initialSections || []).filter((s) => s.is_visible !== false);
  }, [initialSections]);

  // Dynamic tool count per section
  const sectionToolCounts = useMemo(() => {
    const counts: Record<string, number> = { all: initialTools.length };
    visibleSections.forEach((sec) => {
      counts[sec.id] = initialTools.filter((t) => t.section_id === sec.id && t.is_visible !== false).length;
    });
    return counts;
  }, [visibleSections, initialTools]);

  // Current active section object (if specific section selected)
  const activeSection = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeSectionId === 'all') return null;
    return visibleSections.find((s) => s.id === activeSectionId) || null;
  }, [visibleSections, activeSectionId]);

  // Tools in the active section (before search query)
  const activeSectionTools = useMemo(() => {
    if (activeSectionId === 'all') {
      return initialTools.filter((t) => t.is_visible !== false);
    }
    return initialTools.filter(
      (t) => t.section_id === activeSectionId && t.is_visible !== false
    );
  }, [initialTools, activeSectionId]);

  // Real-time filtered tools list
  // If search query is present, search across ALL sections instantly by title or speaker name!
  const filteredTools = useMemo(() => {
    const q = searchQuery.trim().toLowerCase();

    // Search across ALL sections when user types in search bar
    const pool = q
      ? initialTools.filter((t) => t.is_visible !== false)
      : activeSectionTools;

    const result = pool.filter((tool) => {
      // 1. Content Language Filter
      if (
        contentLangFilter !== 'all' &&
        (!tool.available_languages || !tool.available_languages.includes(contentLangFilter))
      ) {
        return false;
      }

      // 2. Real-time Search Filter across Title and Speaker Name
      if (q) {
        const matchSpeaker = tool.speaker_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchTitle = Object.values(tool.title || {}).some(
          (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
        const matchDesc = Object.values(tool.description || {}).some(
          (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
        );
        const matchTopics = Object.values(tool.topics || {}).some((arr) =>
          Array.isArray(arr) && arr.some((item) => item?.toLowerCase().includes(q))
        );

        if (!matchSpeaker && !matchTitle && !matchDesc && !matchTopics) {
          return false;
        }
      }

      return true;
    });

    // 3. Featured tools ALWAYS sort to the top, then by sort_order
    result.sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.is_featured !== b.is_featured) {
        return a.is_featured ? -1 : 1;
      }
      return (a.sort_order ?? 0) - (b.sort_order ?? 0);
    });

    return result;
  }, [
    initialTools,
    activeSectionTools,
    searchQuery,
    contentLangFilter,
  ]);

  // "WhatsApp Full Section" formatted sharing
  const handleShareFullSection = () => {
    const targetTools = activeSectionTools;
    if (targetTools.length === 0) return;

    const secTitle = activeSection
      ? getLocalized(activeSection.title) || activeSection.slug
      : t('all', 'All Resources');
    const secDesc = activeSection ? getLocalized(activeSection.description) : '';

    const header = `🔥 *${secTitle}*${secDesc ? `\n_${secDesc}_\n` : '\n'}\n`;

    const toolsList = targetTools
      .map((tool, idx) => {
        const toolTitle = getLocalized(tool.title) || 'Resource';
        const toolUrl = getLocalized(tool.url);
        const speaker = tool.speaker_name ? ` (${tool.speaker_name})` : '';
        return `${idx + 1}. *${toolTitle}*${speaker}\n👉 ${toolUrl}`;
      })
      .join('\n\n');

    const fullMsg = `${header}${toolsList}\n\n_Shared via AA Team Hub_`;
    const waUrl = `https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(fullMsg)}`;
    window.location.href = waUrl;
  };

  // Saved Tools List
  const savedTools = useMemo(() => {
    return initialTools
      .filter((tool) => savedToolIds.includes(tool.id))
      .sort((a, b) => {
        if (a.is_featured !== b.is_featured) {
          return a.is_featured ? -1 : 1;
        }
        return (a.sort_order ?? 0) - (b.sort_order ?? 0);
      });
  }, [initialTools, savedToolIds]);

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col bg-zinc-50 text-zinc-950 dark:bg-black dark:text-zinc-50 antialiased selection:bg-amber-500 selection:text-white">
      <Header />
      {/* Main Content Area */}
      <main className="mx-auto w-full max-w-xl flex-1 pb-28">
        {/* Tab 1: Tools (Main View) */}
        {activeTab === 'tools' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <SectionControls
              searchQuery={searchQuery}
              onSearchChange={setSearchQuery}
              visibleSections={visibleSections}
              activeSectionId={activeSectionId}
              onSelectSection={setActiveSectionId}
              contentLangFilter={contentLangFilter}
              onContentLangChange={setContentLangFilter}
              sectionToolCounts={sectionToolCounts}
              totalToolsCount={initialTools.length}
              activeSection={activeSection}
              activeSectionToolsCount={activeSectionTools.length}
              onShareFullSection={handleShareFullSection}
              filteredCount={filteredTools.length}
            />

            <div className="space-y-4 px-4">
              {filteredTools.length > 0 ? (
                filteredTools.map((tool) => <ToolCard key={tool.id} tool={tool} />)
              ) : (
                <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 p-10 text-center bg-white/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/30">
                  <div className="flex h-12 w-12 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-400 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500 mb-3">
                    <FolderX className="h-6 w-6" />
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                    {t('empty_section', 'No tools match your filter.')}
                  </h3>
                  <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 max-w-xs">
                    Try adjusting your search query or content language filter.
                  </p>
                  {(searchQuery || contentLangFilter !== 'all') && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setSearchQuery('');
                        setContentLangFilter('all');
                      }}
                      className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 transition-colors"
                    >
                      Clear Filters
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tab 2: Saved (Starred Tools) */}
        {activeTab === 'saved' && (
          <div className="space-y-4 px-4 py-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 sm:text-2xl">
                {t('favorites', 'Saved Tools')}
              </h2>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400">
                Quick access to tools you have starred for offline prospecting and review.
              </p>
            </div>

            {savedTools.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {savedTools.map((tool) => (
                  <ToolCard key={tool.id} tool={tool} />
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 p-10 text-center bg-white/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/30">
                <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-500 dark:bg-amber-950/50 dark:text-amber-400 mb-3">
                  <Star className="h-7 w-7 fill-amber-400 text-amber-500" />
                </div>
                <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100">
                  {t('no_favorites', 'No saved tools yet.')}
                </h3>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 max-w-xs leading-relaxed">
                  Tap the star icon on any tool card in the Tools tab to save it here for instant access.
                </p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setActiveTab('tools')}
                  className="mt-4 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 px-4 py-2.5 text-xs font-bold text-white hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 transition-colors"
                >
                  Browse Tools
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Tab 3: Calendar */}
        {activeTab === 'calendar' && (
          <CalendarTab calendar={initialCalendar} />
        )}

        {/* Tab 4: Share App */}
        {activeTab === 'share' && <ShareAppTab />}
      </main>
      <BottomNav
        activeTab={activeTab}
        onSelectTab={setActiveTab}
        savedCount={savedToolIds.length}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/ShareAppTab.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import { QRCodeSVG } from 'qrcode.react';
import { Share2, Copy, Check, Smartphone } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';

export function ShareAppTab() {
  const { t } = useApp();
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const appUrl = useSyncExternalStore(
    () => () => {},
    () => (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : 'https://teamhub.app'),
    () => 'https://teamhub.app'
  );

  const appTitle = t('app_title', 'Team Hub');
  const shareDesc = t('share_app_desc', 'Scan this QR code with a phone camera or share via WhatsApp to onboard team members and prospects instantly.');

  const whatsappMessage = `${appTitle} - Super App\n\n${shareDesc}\n\n👉 ${appUrl}`;

  const handleWhatsAppShare = () => {
    window.location.href = `https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(whatsappMessage)}`;
  };

  const handleCopyLink = async () => {
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(appUrl);
      } else {
        const textarea = document.createElement('textarea');
        textarea.value = appUrl;
        document.body.appendChild(textarea);
        textarea.select();
        document.execCommand('copy');
        document.body.removeChild(textarea);
      }
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Copy error', err);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-6 px-4 py-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Title */}
      <div className="text-center">
        <AALogo size="lg" className="mx-auto mb-2" />
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
          {t('share_app_title', 'Share Team Hub')}
        </h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs sm:text-sm text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 max-w-sm mx-auto leading-relaxed">
          {shareDesc}
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Full-Screen / Prominent QR Code Card */}
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-sm overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 flex flex-col items-center">
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-inner border border-zinc-200 w-full max-w-[270px]">
          <QRCodeSVG
            value={appUrl}
            size={230}
            level="Q"
            marginSize={1}
            className="h-auto w-full max-w-[230px]"
          />
          <p className="mt-3 text-center text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-500">
            {t('scan_to_view', 'Scan with camera or phone to view')}
          </p>
        </div>

        {/* Action Buttons */}
        <div className="mt-6 w-full space-y-2.5">
          {/* WhatsApp Share Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleWhatsAppShare}
            className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white py-3.5 px-4 text-sm font-bold shadow-md transition-all active:scale-98"
          >
            <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
            <span>{t('btn_whatsapp', 'Share via WhatsApp')}</span>
          </button>

          {/* Copy Link Button */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleCopyLink}
            className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-100 py-3.5 px-4 text-sm font-bold transition-all active:scale-98"
          >
            {copied ? (
              <>
                <Check className="h-4 w-4 text-emerald-500" />
                <span className="text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                  {t('btn_copied', 'Copied!')}
                </span>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Copy className="h-4 w-4" />
                <span>{t('btn_copy', 'Copy App Link')}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Onboarding Tip Card */}
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-sm rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 p-4 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900/50 flex items-start gap-3">
        <div className="rounded-xl bg-amber-500 p-2 text-white flex-shrink-0">
          <Smartphone className="h-4 w-4" />
        </div>
        <div className="text-xs text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 leading-relaxed">
          <p className="font-bold mb-0.5">{t('add_to_home_screen', 'Instant Mobile App')}</p>
          <p>{t('pwa_banner_desc', 'Team members can install this app directly onto their phone home screens without the App Store.')}</p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>
