<file path="src/types/app.ts">
/**
 * Team Hub - Application & Supabase Database Type Definitions
 */

export type LanguageCode = 'en' | 'bm' | 'cn' | 'ta';

export type LocalizedString = Record<LanguageCode, string>;

export type LocalizedStringArray = Record<LanguageCode, string[]>;

export type AccessPinRole = 'superadmin' | 'admin' | 'editor';

export type AccessPin = {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  pin_code: string;
  role: AccessPinRole;
  is_active: boolean;
  created_at: string;
};

export type UiDictionary = {
  key: string;
  en: string;
  bm: string;
  cn: string;
  ta: string;
  id?: string;
  created_at?: string;
};

export type Section = {
  id: string;
  slug: string;
  icon_name: string;
  sort_order: number;
  is_visible: boolean;
  title: LocalizedString;
  description: LocalizedString;
  created_at?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
};

export type ToolType = 'youtube' | 'soundcloud' | 'video' | 'pdf' | 'image' | 'link';

export type Tool = {
  id: string;
  section_id: string;
  type: ToolType;
  title: LocalizedString;
  description: LocalizedString;
  speaker_name: string;
  duration: string;
  available_languages: LanguageCode[];
  topics: LocalizedStringArray;
  url: LocalizedString;
  whatsapp_script: LocalizedString;
  thumbnail_url: string;
  is_featured: boolean;
  sort_order: number;
  is_visible: boolean;
  created_at?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
};

export type CalendarEvent = {
  id: string;
  date: string;
  time: string;
  title: LocalizedString;
  speaker: string;
  venue?: string;
  location_or_link: string;
  description?: string;
  image_url?: string;
};

export type MeetingFrequency =
  | 'once'
  | 'daily'
  | 'weekly'
  | 'monthly_nth_weekday'
  | 'monthly_date';

export type MeetingSeries = {
  id: string;
  title: string;
  frequency: MeetingFrequency;
  date?: string; // YYYY-MM-DD for 'once'
  day_of_week?: number; // 0=Sun..6=Sat
  nth_week?: number; // 1=1st, 2=2nd, 3=3rd, 4=4th, 5=Last
  day_of_month?: number; // 1..31
  time: string;
  speaker: string;
  venue: string;
  address_or_link: string;
  description: string;
  image_url: string;
  is_active: boolean;
};

// Backwards-compatible alias for existing codebase
export type RecurringRule = MeetingSeries;

export type MeetingException = {
  id: string;
  series_id: string;
  original_date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  action: 'cancel' | 'modify';
  new_date?: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  new_time?: string;
  new_title?: string;
  new_speaker?: string;
  new_venue?: string;
  new_address_or_link?: string;
  new_description?: string;
  new_image_url?: string;
  reason?: string;
};

export type BlackoutDate = {
  id: string;
  date: string; // YYYY-MM-DD
  reason: string;
  rule_id?: string; // 'all' or specific rule id
};

export type TeamCalendar = {
  id: string;
  month_label: LocalizedString;
  image_url: LocalizedString;
  events: CalendarEvent[];
  recurring_rules?: MeetingSeries[];
  exceptions?: MeetingException[];
  blackout_dates?: BlackoutDate[];
  default_view?: 'flyer' | 'dynamic';
  last_reviewed_month?: string; // YYYY-MM
  created_at?: string;
  updated_at?: string;
};

/**
 * Supabase Database schema definitions
 */
export type Database = {
  public: {
    Tables: {
      access_pins: {
        Row: AccessPin;
        Insert: Omit<AccessPin, 'id' | 'created_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<AccessPin>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      ui_dictionary: {
        Row: UiDictionary;
        Insert: UiDictionary;
        Update: Partial<UiDictionary>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      sections: {
        Row: Section;
        Insert: Omit<Section, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
          updated_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<Section>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      tools: {
        Row: Tool;
        Insert: Omit<Tool, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
          updated_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<Tool>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
      team_calendar: {
        Row: TeamCalendar;
        Insert: Omit<TeamCalendar, 'id' | 'created_at' | 'updated_at'> & {
          id?: string;
          created_at?: string;
          updated_at?: string;
        };
        Update: Partial<TeamCalendar>;
        Relationships: [];
      };
    };
    Views: Record<string, never>;
    Functions: Record<string, never>;
  };
};

/**
 * Supported language metadata
 */
export const SUPPORTED_LANGUAGES: { code: LanguageCode; label: string; nativeLabel: string }[] = [
  { code: 'en', label: 'English', nativeLabel: 'English' },
  { code: 'bm', label: 'Malay', nativeLabel: 'Bahasa Melayu' },
  { code: 'cn', label: 'Chinese', nativeLabel: '中文' },
  { code: 'ta', label: 'Tamil', nativeLabel: 'தமிழ்' },
];

export const DEFAULT_LANGUAGE: LanguageCode = 'en';

export const PIN_SESSION_COOKIE = 'teamhub_admin_session';

export interface AdminSession {
  id: string;
  label: string;
  role: AccessPinRole;
}

export type ActionResponse<T = unknown> =
  | { success: true; data?: T; session?: AdminSession | null; url?: string; path?: string; message?: string }
  | { success: false; error: string; session?: null; data?: null };

export interface AdminDashboardData {
  session: AdminSession;
  sections: Section[];
  tools: Tool[];
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
  dictionary: UiDictionary[];
  pins?: AccessPin[];
}
</file>

<file path="src/app/layout.tsx">
export const instant = false;

import type { Metadata, Viewport } from "next";
import { connection } from "next/server";
import { Geist, Geist_Mono } from "next/font/google";
import "./globals.css";
import { ThemeProvider } from "@/components/ThemeProvider";
import { AppProvider } from "@/context/AppContext";
import { InstallAppBanner } from "@/components/InstallAppBanner";
import { supabase, supabaseAdmin } from "@/lib/supabase";
import type { UiDictionary } from "@/types/app";

const geistSans = Geist({
  variable: "--font-geist-sans",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

const geistMono = Geist_Mono({
  variable: "--font-geist-mono",
  subsets: ["latin"],
});

export const viewport: Viewport = {
  themeColor: [
    { media: "(prefers-color-scheme: light)", color: "#ffffff" },
    { media: "(prefers-color-scheme: dark)", color: "#09090b" },
  ],
  width: "device-width",
  initialScale: 1,
  maximumScale: 1,
  viewportFit: "cover",
};

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "AA Team Hub - Super App",
  description: "Team resource portal, daily tools, and training hub",
  manifest: "/manifest.webmanifest",
  appleWebApp: {
    capable: true,
    statusBarStyle: "default",
    title: "AA Hub",
  },
  formatDetection: {
    telephone: false,
  },
  icons: {
    icon: [
      { url: "/icon", sizes: "32x32", type: "image/png" },
      { url: "/icon-192", sizes: "192x192", type: "image/png" },
      { url: "/icon-512", sizes: "512x512", type: "image/png" },
    ],
    apple: [{ url: "/apple-icon", sizes: "180x180", type: "image/png" }],
  },
};

async function getUiDictionary(): Promise<UiDictionary[]> {
  await connection();
  if (
    !process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL ||
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL.includes("placeholder")
  ) {
    return [];
  }
  try {
    const client = process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY ? supabaseAdmin : supabase;
    const { data, error } = await client.from("ui_dictionary").select("*");
    if (!error && data && data.length > 0) {
      return data as UiDictionary[];
    }
  } catch {
    // Graceful fallback to default dictionary when Supabase is unconfigured or offline
  }
  return [];
}

export default async function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  const initialDictionary = await getUiDictionary();

  return (
    <html
      lang="en"
      suppressHydrationWarning
      className={`${geistSans.variable} ${geistMono.variable} h-full antialiased`}
    >
      <body className="min-h-full flex flex-col font-sans bg-background text-foreground tracking-tight selection:bg-zinc-800 selection:text-white dark:selection:bg-zinc-200 dark:selection:text-black">
        <ThemeProvider
          attribute="class"
          defaultTheme="system"
          enableSystem
          disableTransitionOnChange
        >
          <AppProvider initialDictionary={initialDictionary}>
            {children}
            <InstallAppBanner />
          </AppProvider>
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

<file path="src/components/admin/AdminDashboard.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState } from 'react';
import {
  FolderKanban,
  Calendar,
  Languages,
  KeyRound,
  CheckCircle2,
  AlertCircle,
  X,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { AdminTopBar } from './AdminTopBar';
import { TabSectionsTools } from './TabSectionsTools';
import { TabCalendar } from './TabCalendar';
import { TabDictionary } from './TabDictionary';
import { TabAccessPins } from './TabAccessPins';
import { logoutPinAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { Section, Tool, TeamCalendar, UiDictionary, AccessPin, AdminSession } from '@/types/app';

export interface AdminDashboardProps {
  session: AdminSession;
  initialSections: Section[];
  initialTools: Tool[];
  initialCalendar: TeamCalendar;
  initialDictionary: UiDictionary[];
  initialPins?: AccessPin[];
  onLogoutSuccess: () => void;
}

export type AdminTab = 'sections_tools' | 'calendar' | 'dictionary' | 'pins';

export function AdminDashboard({
  session,
  initialSections,
  initialTools,
  initialCalendar,
  initialDictionary,
  initialPins = [],
  onLogoutSuccess,
}: AdminDashboardProps) {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<AdminTab>('sections_tools');
  const [loggingOut, setLoggingOut] = useState(false);

  // Global state across dashboard tabs
  const [sections, setSections] = useState<Section[]>(initialSections);
  const [tools, setTools] = useState<Tool[]>(initialTools);
  const [calendar, setCalendar] = useState<TeamCalendar>(initialCalendar);
  const [dictionary, setDictionary] = useState<UiDictionary[]>(initialDictionary);
  const [pins, setPins] = useState<AccessPin[]>(initialPins);

  // Notification Toast State
  const [toast, setToast] = useState<{ message: string; isError?: boolean } | null>(null);

  const notify = (message: string, isError = false) => {
    setToast({ message, isError });
    setTimeout(() => {
      setToast((curr) => (curr?.message === message ? null : curr));
    }, 3500);
  };

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    setLoggingOut(true);
    try {
      await logoutPinAction();
      onLogoutSuccess();
    } catch {
      onLogoutSuccess();
    } finally {
      setLoggingOut(false);
    }
  };

  // Role-based visible tabs definition
  const tabs: { id: AdminTab; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode; allowedRoles: string[] }[] = [
    {
      id: 'sections_tools',
      label: 'Sections & Tools',
      icon: <FolderKanban className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'],
    },
    {
      id: 'calendar',
      label: 'Monthly Calendar',
      icon: <Calendar className="w-4 h-4 text-blue-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'],
    },
    {
      id: 'dictionary',
      label: 'UI Text & Translations',
      icon: <Languages className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['admin', 'superadmin'],
    },
    {
      id: 'pins',
      label: 'PIN & Access Control',
      icon: <KeyRound className="w-4 h-4 text-purple-500" />,
      allowedRoles: ['superadmin'],
    },
  ];

  const visibleTabs = tabs.filter((t) => t.allowedRoles.includes(session.role));

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-950 text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-50 transition-colors">
      <AdminTopBar session={session} onLogout={handleLogout} loggingOut={loggingOut} />

      <main className="flex-1 w-full max-w-7xl mx-auto px-3 sm:px-6 py-4 sm:py-6 space-y-6">
        {/* Navigation Tabs Bar */}
        <div className="flex overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-1.5 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-sm gap-1">
          {visibleTabs.map((t) => {
            const isActive = activeTab === t.id;
            return (
              <button
                key={t.id}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveTab(t.id)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 sm:px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all whitespace-nowrap select-none ${
                  isActive
                    ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 shadow-sm scale-[1.01]'
                    : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-100/70 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800/60'
                }`}
              >
                {t.icon}
                <span>{t.label}</span>
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>

        {/* Tab View Panels */}
        <div className="transition-all animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          {activeTab === 'sections_tools' && (
            <TabSectionsTools
              sections={sections}
              setSections={setSections}
              tools={tools}
              setTools={setTools}
              onNotify={notify}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'calendar' && (
            <TabCalendar calendar={calendar} setCalendar={setCalendar} onNotify={notify} />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'dictionary' && (
            <TabDictionary
              dictionary={dictionary}
              setDictionary={setDictionary}
              onNotify={notify}
            />
          )}

          {activeTab === 'pins' && session.role === 'superadmin' && (
            <TabAccessPins
              pins={pins}
              setPins={setPins}
              currentSession={session}
              onNotify={notify}
            />
          )}
        </div>
      </main>

      {/* Floating Notification Toast */}
      {toast && (
        <div className="fixed bottom-6 right-6 z-50 flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-900 dark:bg-zinc-100 text-white dark:text-zinc-900 shadow-xl border border-zinc-700 dark:border-zinc-300 text-xs font-semibold animate-in slide-in-from-bottom-3 duration-200">
          {toast.isError ? (
            <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 text-red-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          ) : (
            <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 flex-shrink-0" />
          )}
          <span>{toast.message}</span>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setToast(null)}
            className="ml-2 p-0.5 rounded-full hover:bg-zinc-800 dark:hover:bg-zinc-200 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/ToolModal.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  X,
  Upload,
  Loader2,
  Check,
  Star,
  Eye,
  EyeOff,
  Video,
  FileText,
  Music,
  Link as LinkIcon,
  ImageIcon,
  Sparkles,
  AlertCircle,
  ChevronDown,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { uploadMediaAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type { Tool, Section, ToolType, LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

interface ToolModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSave: (tool: Partial<Tool>) => Promise<boolean>;
  tool?: Tool | null;
  sections: Section[];
}

const TOOL_TYPES: { type: ToolType; label: string; icon: React.ReactNode }[] = [
  { type: 'youtube', label: 'YouTube', icon: <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500" /> },
  { type: 'pdf', label: 'PDF Document', icon: <FileText className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" /> },
  { type: 'soundcloud', label: 'SoundCloud', icon: <Music className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-orange-500" /> },
  { type: 'video', label: 'Direct Video', icon: <Video className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-blue-500" /> },
  { type: 'image', label: 'Image', icon: <ImageIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-emerald-500" /> },
  { type: 'link', label: 'Web Link', icon: <LinkIcon className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-purple-500" /> },
];

const TOOL_LANG_OPTIONS: { code: LanguageCode; label: string }[] = [
  { code: 'en', label: 'EN' },
  { code: 'cn', label: '中文' },
  { code: 'bm', label: 'BM' },
];

export function ToolModal({ isOpen, onClose, onSave, tool, sections }: ToolModalProps) {
  const [sectionId, setSectionId] = useState('');
  const [type, setType] = useState<ToolType>('youtube');
  const [toolLang, setToolLang] = useState<LanguageCode>('en');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [speakerName, setSpeakerName] = useState('');
  const [mediaUrl, setMediaUrl] = useState('');
  const [isFeatured, setIsFeatured] = useState(false);
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(true);

  // Optional power-user accordion
  const [showAccordion, setShowAccordion] = useState(false);
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [whatsappScript, setWhatsappScript] = useState('');

  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tool) {
      setSectionId(tool.section_id || sections[0]?.id || '');
      setType(tool.type || 'youtube');
      const primaryLang = tool.available_languages?.[0] || 'en';
      setToolLang(primaryLang === 'cn' || primaryLang === 'bm' ? primaryLang : 'en');
      setTitle(tool.title?.[primaryLang] || tool.title?.en || '');
      setSpeakerName(tool.speaker_name || '');
      setMediaUrl(tool.url?.[primaryLang] || tool.url?.en || '');
      setIsFeatured(Boolean(tool.is_featured));
      setIsVisible(tool.is_visible !== false);
      const desc = tool.description?.[primaryLang] || tool.description?.en || '';
      const script = tool.whatsapp_script?.[primaryLang] || tool.whatsapp_script?.en || '';
      setDescription(desc);
      setWhatsappScript(script);
      if (desc || script) setShowAccordion(true);
    } else {
      setSectionId(sections[0]?.id || '');
      setType('youtube');
      setToolLang('en');
      setTitle('');
      setSpeakerName('');
      setMediaUrl('');
      setIsFeatured(false);
      setIsVisible(true);
      setDescription('');
      setWhatsappScript('');
      setShowAccordion(false);
    }
    setError(null);
  }, [tool, sections, isOpen]);

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setUploading(true);
    setError(null);
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      const res = await uploadMediaAction(formData);
      if (res.success && res.url) {
        setMediaUrl(res.url);
      } else {
        const msg = !res.success ? res.error : 'Upload failed';
        setError(msg);
      }
    } catch {
      setError('File upload failed');
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleSave = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!title.trim()) {
      setError('Tool Title is required.');
      return;
    }
    if (!mediaUrl.trim()) {
      setError('Media URL or File Upload is required.');
      return;
    }
    if (!sectionId) {
      setError('Please select a section.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    setError(null);

    const cleanTitle = title.trim();
    const cleanUrl = mediaUrl.trim();
    const cleanDesc = description.trim();
    const cleanScript = whatsappScript.trim();

    const payload: Partial<Tool> = {
      ...(tool?.id ? { id: tool.id } : {}),
      section_id: sectionId,
      type,
      speaker_name: speakerName.trim(),
      duration: type === 'pdf' ? '📄 PDF Guide' : type === 'soundcloud' ? '🎧 Audio' : '🎥 Video',
      is_featured: isFeatured,
      is_visible: isVisible,
      available_languages: [toolLang],
      title: {
        en: cleanTitle,
        bm: cleanTitle,
        cn: cleanTitle,
        ta: cleanTitle,
      },
      url: {
        en: cleanUrl,
        bm: cleanUrl,
        cn: cleanUrl,
        ta: cleanUrl,
      },
      description: {
        en: cleanDesc,
        bm: cleanDesc,
        cn: cleanDesc,
        ta: cleanDesc,
      },
      whatsapp_script: {
        en: cleanScript,
        bm: cleanScript,
        cn: cleanScript,
        ta: cleanScript,
      },
      topics: {
        en: speakerName ? [speakerName.trim()] : [],
        bm: speakerName ? [speakerName.trim()] : [],
        cn: speakerName ? [speakerName.trim()] : [],
        ta: [],
      },
      thumbnail_url: tool?.thumbnail_url || '',
    };

    const success = await onSave(payload);
    setSaving(false);
    if (success) {
      onClose();
    } else {
      setError('Failed to save tool. Please check all fields.');
    }
  };

  if (!isOpen) return null;

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-3 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-sm animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-xl max-h-[92vh] flex flex-col rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 sm:px-6 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50/70 dark:bg-zinc-900/70">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div className="flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
              <Sparkles className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h3 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                {tool ? 'Edit Resource / Tool' : 'Add New Resource / Tool'}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500">Quick single-screen creator</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={onClose}
            className="rounded-full p-1.5 text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
          >
            <X className="w-4 h-4" />
          </button>
        </div>

        <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-5 sm:p-6 space-y-4">
          {error && (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-3 text-xs text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 rounded-2xl">
              <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 flex-shrink-0" />
              <span>{error}</span>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Section *
            </label>
            <select
              value={sectionId}
              onChange={(e) => setSectionId(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 font-medium focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            >
              {sections.map((s) => (
                <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>
                  {s.title.en} ({s.title.cn || s.title.bm})
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
              Media Type *
            </label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-3 sm:grid-cols-6 gap-1.5">
              {TOOL_TYPES.map((t) => {
                const isSelected = type === t.type;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={t.type}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setType(t.type)}
                    className={`flex flex-col items-center justify-center gap-1 p-2 rounded-xl border text-center transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-50/60 text-amber-900 dark:bg-amber-950/40 dark:text-amber-200 dark:border-amber-500 font-bold shadow-2xs'
                        : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-50 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {t.icon}
                    <span className="text-[10px] truncate max-w-full">{t.label}</span>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1.5">
              Tool Language *
            </label>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              {TOOL_LANG_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
                const isSelected = toolLang === opt.code;
                return (
                  <button
                    key={opt.code}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setToolLang(opt.code)}
                    className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold border transition-all ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 border-zinc-900 dark:border-white shadow-2xs font-bold'
                        : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800'
                    }`}
                  >
                    {opt.label}
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Tool Title *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={title}
              onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. W.O.W (KL) - COEV Rayne"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 mb-1">
              Speaker Name
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              value={speakerName}
              onChange={(e) => setSpeakerName(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="e.g. COEV Rayne, Robert Kiyosaki, Selenium..."
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
                Media URL or File Upload *
              </label>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={uploading}
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 hover:underline"
              >
                {uploading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-3 h-3 animate-spin" />
                    <span>Uploading...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Upload className="w-3 h-3" />
                    <span>Upload File</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                className="hidden"
                accept="video/*,audio/*,application/pdf,image/*"
                onChange={handleFileUpload}
              />
            </div>
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={mediaUrl}
              onChange={(e) => setMediaUrl(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="https://youtu.be/... or uploaded file link"
              className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsFeatured(!isFeatured)}
              className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-2xl border transition-all text-left ${
                isFeatured
                  ? 'border-amber-400 bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-900 dark:text-amber-200 font-bold'
                  : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Star className={`w-4 h-4 ${isFeatured ? 'fill-amber-400 text-amber-500' : ''}`} />
                <span className="text-xs">Featured at top</span>
              </div>
              <div className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center ${isFeatured ? 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-500 text-white' : 'border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-600'}`}>
                {isFeatured && <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 stroke-[3]" />}
              </div>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setIsVisible(!isVisible)}
              className={`flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-2xl border transition-all text-left ${
                isVisible
                  ? 'border-emerald-400 bg-emerald-50 dark:bg-emerald-950/40 text-emerald-900 dark:text-emerald-200 font-bold'
                  : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 text-zinc-400'
              }`}
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                {isVisible ? <Eye className="w-4 h-4 text-emerald-500" /> : <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" />}
                <span className="text-xs">Visible to users</span>
              </div>
              <div className={`w-4 h-4 rounded-full border flex items-center justify-center ${isVisible ? 'border-emerald-500 bg-emerald-500 text-white' : 'border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-600'}`}>
                {isVisible && <Check className="w-2.5 h-2.5 stroke-[3]" />}
              </div>
            </button>
          </div>
          <div className="border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-800/40">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setShowAccordion(!showAccordion)}
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-between px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-100/60 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800/80 transition-colors"
            >
              <span>Optional: Short Description & Custom WhatsApp Script</span>
              <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-zinc-400 transition-transform duration-200 ${showAccordion ? 'rotate-180 text-amber-500' : ''}`} />
            </button>
            {showAccordion && (
              <div className="p-4 pt-1 space-y-3 border-t border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-800/80">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1">Short Description</label>
                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={description}
                    onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Brief 1-2 sentence teaser about this tool..."
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-[11px] font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mb-1">Custom WhatsApp Script</label>
                  <textarea
                    rows={2}
                    value={whatsappScript}
                    onChange={(e) => setWhatsappScript(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="e.g. Check out this essential training before our call:"
                    className="w-full rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500 resize-none"
                  />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2.5 pt-3 border-t border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={onClose}
              disabled={saving}
              className="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={saving}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 text-white text-xs font-bold hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 transition-all shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {saving ? (
                <>
                  <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                  <span>Saving Tool...</span>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{tool ? 'Update Tool' : 'Create Tool'}</span>
                </>
              )}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/CalendarTab.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import {
  Calendar as CalendarIcon,
  Download,
  Share2,
  Video,
  MapPin,
  Clock,
  Eye,
  ChevronDown,
  X,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { TeamCalendar } from '@/types/app';
import {
  getEventsForCalendar,
  splitUpcomingMeetings,
  type GeneratedScheduleEvent,
} from '@/lib/calendarGenerator';
import { ImageLightbox } from '@/components/ImageLightbox';
import { EventDetailsModal } from '@/components/EventDetailsModal';

interface CalendarTabProps {
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
}

const MONTH_NAMES = [
  'January', 'February', 'March', 'April', 'May', 'June',
  'July', 'August', 'September', 'October', 'November', 'December'
];

const WEEK_DAYS = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'];

export function CalendarTab({ calendar }: CalendarTabProps) {
  const { t, getLocalized } = useApp();

  // Locked to Current Month Only
  const now = new Date();
  const currentYear = now.getFullYear();
  const currentMonth = now.getMonth() + 1; // 1-12
  const todayDayNum = now.getDate();
  const todayIsoDate = `${currentYear}-${String(currentMonth).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(todayDayNum).padStart(2, '0')}`;

  const [activeView, setActiveView] = useState<'dynamic' | 'flyer'>(
    calendar?.default_view || 'dynamic'
  );

  // Day drawer/modal state
  const [dayDrawerDate, setDayDrawerDate] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // Meeting details modal
  const [selectedEvent, setSelectedEvent] = useState<GeneratedScheduleEvent | null>(null);

  // Lightbox for monthly flyer
  const [isLightboxOpen, setIsLightboxOpen] = useState(false);

  // Accordion state for remaining meetings
  const [showRemainingAccordion, setShowRemainingAccordion] = useState(false);

  const monthLabel = getLocalized(calendar?.month_label) || t('upcoming_events', 'Team Calendar');
  const imageUrl = getLocalized(calendar?.image_url);

  // Generate schedule for the locked current year/month
  const monthlyEvents = useMemo(() => {
    return getEventsForCalendar(calendar, currentYear, currentMonth);
  }, [calendar, currentYear, currentMonth]);

  // Group events by date string
  const eventsByDate = useMemo(() => {
    const map: Record<string, GeneratedScheduleEvent[]> = {};
    for (const ev of monthlyEvents) {
      if (!map[ev.date]) map[ev.date] = [];
      map[ev.date].push(ev);
    }
    return map;
  }, [monthlyEvents]);

  // Split upcoming 2 meetings & remaining meetings
  const { nextTwo, remaining } = useMemo(() => {
    return splitUpcomingMeetings(monthlyEvents, todayIsoDate);
  }, [monthlyEvents, todayIsoDate]);

  // Grid calculation: days in month, starting day of week
  const daysInMonth = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth, 0).getDate();
  const startDayOfWeek = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth - 1, 1).getDay(); // 0 = Sun

  const handleShareFlyer = () => {
    const shareText = `${monthLabel}\n\n${t('share_flyer', 'Check out our monthly team calendar and upcoming events:')}\n${imageUrl || (typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.href : '')}`;
    window.location.href = `https://wa.me/?text=${encodeURIComponent(shareText)}`;
  };

  const handleDownloadFlyer = () => {
    if (imageUrl) {
      window.open(imageUrl, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer');
    }
  };

  const dayDrawerEvents = dayDrawerDate ? eventsByDate[dayDrawerDate] || [] : [];

  return (
    <div className="w-full space-y-5 px-4 py-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
      {/* Top Toggle: Live Calendar vs Monthly Flyer */}
      <div className="flex rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 p-1 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveView('dynamic')}
          className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
            activeView === 'dynamic'
              ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 shadow-sm'
              : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
          }`}
        >
          <CalendarIcon className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-500" />
          <span>📅 Live Calendar</span>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveView('flyer')}
          className={`flex-1 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
            activeView === 'flyer'
              ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 shadow-sm'
              : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
          }`}
        >
          <span className="text-base leading-none">🖼️</span>
          <span>Monthly Flyer</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {activeView === 'dynamic' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {/* Current Month Header (Locked to Current Month Only) */}
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-sm">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                {MONTH_NAMES[currentMonth - 1]} {currentYear}
              </h3>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                Current month schedule • {monthlyEvents.length} meetings
              </p>
            </div>
            <span className="px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-500/10 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 border border-amber-500/20">
              Active Schedule
            </span>
          </div>

          {/* Month Calendar Grid with Meeting Titles Inside Each Day Box */}
          <div className="p-3 sm:p-4 rounded-3xl bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-sm">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 text-center mb-2">
              {WEEK_DAYS.map((d) => (
                <div key={d} className="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-zinc-400 py-1">
                  {d}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>

            <div className="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1 sm:gap-1.5">
              {Array.from({ length: startDayOfWeek }).map((_, i) => (
                <div key={`empty-${i}`} className="min-h-[64px] sm:min-h-[76px] rounded-xl bg-zinc-50/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/40" />
              ))}

              {Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }).map((_, i) => {
                const dayNum = i + 1;
                const dateStr = `${currentYear}-${String(currentMonth).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(dayNum).padStart(2, '0')}`;
                const dayEvents = eventsByDate[dateStr] || [];
                const hasEvents = dayEvents.length > 0;
                const isToday = dayNum === todayDayNum;

                return (
                  <button
                    key={dateStr}
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setDayDrawerDate(dateStr)}
                    className={`min-h-[64px] sm:min-h-[76px] rounded-xl p-1 sm:p-1.5 flex flex-col justify-start items-start text-left transition-all border ${
                      isToday
                        ? 'border-amber-500 bg-amber-50/40 dark:bg-amber-950/20 shadow-xs ring-1 ring-amber-500'
                        : hasEvents
                        ? 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-850 hover:border-amber-400 hover:shadow-xs'
                        : 'border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800/60 bg-zinc-50/60 dark:bg-zinc-900/40 text-zinc-400'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="w-full flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
                      <span
                        className={`text-[11px] font-bold ${
                          isToday
                            ? 'inline-flex items-center justify-center h-5 w-5 rounded-full bg-amber-500 text-white'
                            : 'text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {dayNum}
                      </span>
                      {hasEvents && (
                        <span className="text-[9px] font-bold text-zinc-400">
                          {dayEvents.length}
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>

                    {/* Up to 2 Mini Meeting Title Pills inside the day box */}
                    <div className="w-full space-y-0.5 overflow-hidden">
                      {dayEvents.slice(0, 2).map((ev) => {
                        const titleText = getLocalized(ev.title);
                        return (
                          <div
                            key={ev.id}
                            className="w-full text-[9px] sm:text-[10px] font-semibold px-1 py-0.5 rounded bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 dark:bg-blue-950 dark:text-blue-300 truncate leading-tight"
                            title={titleText}
                          >
                            {titleText}
                          </div>
                        );
                      })}
                      {dayEvents.length > 2 && (
                        <span className="block text-[8px] sm:text-[9px] font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 px-0.5">
                          +{dayEvents.length - 2} more
                        </span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </button>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Upcoming Meetings Section (Next 2 Meetings prominent at top) */}
          <div className="space-y-3 pt-1">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1">
              <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400">
                Next 2 Upcoming Meetings
              </h4>
              <span className="text-[11px] font-medium text-zinc-400">From today onward</span>
            </div>

            {nextTwo.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-6 text-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/60 dark:bg-zinc-900/40">
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">No upcoming meetings remaining this month.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-2.5">
                {nextTwo.map((ev, idx) => {
                  const titleStr = getLocalized(ev.title);
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={ev.id}
                      onClick={() => setSelectedEvent(ev)}
                      className={`cursor-pointer p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all active:scale-[0.99] flex items-start justify-between gap-3 shadow-xs ${
                        idx === 0
                          ? 'border-amber-400/90 dark:border-amber-500/50 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-50/50 via-white to-white dark:from-amber-950/20 dark:via-zinc-900 dark:to-zinc-900'
                          : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-amber-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1.5">
                          <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-500 text-white">
                            {ev.date}
                          </span>
                          {ev.time && (
                            <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-300 font-semibold">
                              <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-amber-500" />
                              <span>{ev.time}</span>
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {ev.is_modified && (
                            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[9px] font-bold rounded bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-950 dark:text-purple-300">
                              Rescheduled
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 leading-snug">
                          {titleStr}
                        </h4>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 flex-wrap">
                          {ev.speaker && <span>🎙️ {ev.speaker}</span>}
                          {ev.venue && <span>📍 {ev.venue}</span>}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="self-center flex-shrink-0 text-amber-600 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40">
                        Details →
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Rest of the Month Accordion */}
            {remaining.length > 0 && (
              <div className="pt-2">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setShowRemainingAccordion(!showRemainingAccordion)}
                  className="w-full flex items-center justify-between p-3.5 rounded-2xl bg-zinc-100/80 dark:bg-zinc-800/60 hover:bg-zinc-200/70 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors text-xs font-bold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300"
                >
                  <span>Show {remaining.length} more meetings this month</span>
                  <ChevronDown className={`w-4 h-4 text-zinc-500 transition-transform ${showRemainingAccordion ? 'rotate-180 text-amber-500' : ''}`} />
                </button>
                {showRemainingAccordion && (
                  <div className="space-y-2 mt-2.5 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
                    {remaining.map((ev) => (
                      <div
                        key={ev.id}
                        onClick={() => setSelectedEvent(ev)}
                        className="cursor-pointer p-3 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 hover:border-amber-300 transition-all flex items-center justify-between gap-3 text-xs"
                      >
                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-0.5">
                            <span className="font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">{ev.date}</span>
                            {ev.time && <span className="text-zinc-400">• {ev.time}</span>}
                          </div>
                          <h5 className="font-bold text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 truncate">{getLocalized(ev.title)}</h5>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-amber-500 font-bold">→</span>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {activeView === 'flyer' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          {imageUrl ? (
            <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 bg-white dark:border-zinc-800 dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-sm">
              <div
                className="group relative aspect-[16/9] sm:aspect-[21/9] w-full cursor-pointer overflow-hidden bg-zinc-950"
                onClick={() => setIsLightboxOpen(true)}
              >
                <Image src={imageUrl} alt={monthLabel} fill unoptimized sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, 640px" className="object-cover transition-transform duration-300 group-hover:scale-105" />
                <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-end justify-end p-3 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/60 via-transparent to-transparent">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-black/75 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white backdrop-blur-sm">
                    <Eye className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                    <span>{t('tap_to_enlarge', 'Tap flyer to enlarge')}</span>
                  </span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 p-3 border-t border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800">
                <button type="button" onClick={handleDownloadFlyer} className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:bg-zinc-800 py-2.5 px-3 text-xs font-bold transition-colors">
                  <Download className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Download Flyer</span>
                </button>
                <button type="button" onClick={handleShareFlyer} className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white py-2.5 px-3 text-xs font-bold transition-colors shadow-xs">
                  <Share2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                  <span>Share Flyer</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="p-8 text-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/30">
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">No flyer image uploaded for this month.</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Day Drawer / Modal */}
      {dayDrawerDate && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4 bg-black/60 backdrop-blur-xs animate-in fade-in duration-150">
          <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg max-h-[85vh] rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-2xl overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-5 py-4 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                  📅 {dayDrawerDate}
                </h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                  {dayDrawerEvents.length} meeting{dayDrawerEvents.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} scheduled
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setDayDrawerDate(null)}
                className="p-1.5 rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-600 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 sm:p-5 space-y-2.5">
              {dayDrawerEvents.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-8 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500">
                  No meetings scheduled for this date.
                </div>
              ) : (
                dayDrawerEvents.map((ev) => {
                  const titleStr = getLocalized(ev.title);
                  return (
                    <div
                      key={ev.id}
                      onClick={() => {
                        setSelectedEvent(ev);
                      }}
                      className="cursor-pointer p-3.5 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-850 hover:border-amber-400 hover:shadow-xs transition-all flex items-start justify-between gap-3"
                    >
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                          <span className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400">
                            {ev.time}
                          </span>
                          {ev.is_modified && (
                            <span className="px-1.5 py-0.2 text-[9px] font-bold rounded bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 dark:bg-purple-950 dark:text-purple-300">
                              Rescheduled
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 leading-snug">
                          {titleStr}
                        </h4>
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 flex-wrap">
                          {ev.speaker && <span>🎙️ {ev.speaker}</span>}
                          {ev.venue && <span>📍 {ev.venue}</span>}
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <span className="self-center text-amber-500 text-xs font-bold">
                        View →
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  );
                })
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <EventDetailsModal isOpen={Boolean(selectedEvent)} onClose={() => setSelectedEvent(null)} event={selectedEvent} />
      {imageUrl && <ImageLightbox isOpen={isLightboxOpen} onClose={() => setIsLightboxOpen(false)} imageUrl={imageUrl} title={monthLabel} />}
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/Header.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useMemo, useState, useRef, useEffect, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useTheme } from 'next-themes';
import { Sun, Moon, Lock, ChevronDown, Check } from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import { AALogo } from '@/components/AALogo';
import type { LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';

export { AALogo };

export function Header() {
  const { lang, setLang, t, dictionary } = useApp();
  const { theme, setTheme, resolvedTheme } = useTheme();
  const [langOpen, setLangOpen] = useState(false);
  const dropdownRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);

  const mounted = useSyncExternalStore(
    () => () => {},
    () => true,
    () => false
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleClickOutside(event: MouseEvent) {
      if (dropdownRef.current && !dropdownRef.current.contains(event.target as Node)) {
        setLangOpen(false);
      }
    }
    if (langOpen) {
      document.addEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    }
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('mousedown', handleClickOutside);
    };
  }, [langOpen]);

  // Show Tamil pill only if Tamil dictionary entries are populated
  const hasTamil = useMemo(() => {
    return Object.values(dictionary).some(
      (item) => typeof item.ta === 'string' && item.ta.trim().length > 0
    );
  }, [dictionary]);

  const languages: { code: LanguageCode; label: string; fullLabel: string }[] = useMemo(() => [
    { code: 'en', label: 'EN', fullLabel: 'EN (English)' },
    { code: 'bm', label: 'BM', fullLabel: 'BM (Bahasa Melayu)' },
    { code: 'cn', label: '中文', fullLabel: '中文 (Chinese)' },
    ...(hasTamil ? [{ code: 'ta' as LanguageCode, label: 'தமிழ்', fullLabel: 'தமிழ் (Tamil)' }] : []),
  ], [hasTamil]);

  const currentOption = useMemo(() => {
    return languages.find((l) => l.code === lang) || languages[0];
  }, [languages, lang]);

  const isDark = mounted && (theme === 'dark' || resolvedTheme === 'dark');

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme(isDark ? 'light' : 'dark');
  };

  return (
    <header className="sticky top-0 z-30 w-full border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/95 dark:bg-zinc-950/95 backdrop-blur-md transition-colors">
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6">
        {/* App Title */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
          <AALogo size="md" />
          <h1 className="truncate text-lg font-bold tracking-tight text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 sm:text-xl">
            {t('app_title', 'Team Hub')}
          </h1>
        </div>

        {/* Right Section: Compact Dropdown Language Switcher + Theme Toggle + Discreet Lock */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 sm:gap-2">
          {/* Single Compact Button Dropdown Language Selector */}
          <div className="relative" ref={dropdownRef}>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => setLangOpen((prev) => !prev)}
              aria-label="Select Application Language"
              aria-expanded={langOpen}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-bold text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-200 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 transition-colors active:scale-95 shadow-2xs select-none"
            >
              <span>{currentOption?.label || 'EN'}</span>
              <span className={`text-[11px] text-zinc-500 transition-transform duration-200 ${langOpen ? 'rotate-180 text-amber-500' : ''}`}>▾</span>
            </button>

            {/* Popover Menu */}
            {langOpen && (
              <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-48 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-1.5 shadow-xl z-50 animate-in fade-in zoom-in-95 duration-150">
                <div className="px-3 py-1.5 text-[10px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-zinc-400 border-b border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 mb-1">
                  Language / 语言
                </div>
                {languages.map((opt) => {
                  const isActive = lang === opt.code;
                  return (
                    <button
                      key={opt.code}
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setLang(opt.code);
                        setLangOpen(false);
                      }}
                      className={`flex w-full items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl transition-all ${
                        isActive
                          ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white font-bold shadow-xs'
                          : 'text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <span>{opt.fullLabel}</span>
                      {isActive && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />}
                    </button>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Dark / Light Mode Toggle */}
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={toggleTheme}
            aria-label={isDark ? 'Switch to light mode' : 'Switch to dark mode'}
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 hover:text-zinc-950 dark:hover:text-zinc-100 transition-colors"
          >
            {mounted ? (
              isDark ? (
                <Sun className="h-4 w-4" />
              ) : (
                <Moon className="h-4 w-4" />
              )
            ) : (
              <span className="h-4 w-4" />
            )}
          </button>

          {/* Discreet Lock Icon linking to /admin */}
          <Link
            href="/admin"
            aria-label="Admin Portal"
            className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-500 dark:hover:text-zinc-200 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-900 transition-colors"
          >
            <Lock className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="tests/unit.test.mjs">
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

// Test 1: getLocalized function logic
function getLocalized(obj, targetLang, activeLang = 'en') {
  if (!obj) return '';
  const langToUse = targetLang || activeLang;
  const val = obj[langToUse]?.trim();
  if (val) return val;

  if (obj.en?.trim()) return obj.en.trim();
  if (obj.bm?.trim()) return obj.bm.trim();
  if (obj.cn?.trim()) return obj.cn.trim();
  if (obj.ta?.trim()) return obj.ta.trim();
  return '';
}

test('getLocalized: returns requested language when present', () => {
  const obj = {
    en: 'Welcome',
    bm: 'Selamat Datang',
    cn: '欢迎',
    ta: 'வரவேற்பு',
  };

  assert.equal(getLocalized(obj, 'bm'), 'Selamat Datang');
  assert.equal(getLocalized(obj, 'cn'), '欢迎');
  assert.equal(getLocalized(obj, 'ta'), 'வரவேற்பு');
  assert.equal(getLocalized(obj, 'en'), 'Welcome');
});

test('getLocalized: falls back to EN when target language is empty', () => {
  const obj = {
    en: 'English Title',
    bm: '',
    cn: '',
    ta: '',
  };

  assert.equal(getLocalized(obj, 'cn'), 'English Title');
  assert.equal(getLocalized(obj, 'bm'), 'English Title');
  assert.equal(getLocalized(obj, 'ta'), 'English Title');
});

test('getLocalized: falls back to BM then CN when EN is empty', () => {
  const objWithBm = {
    en: '',
    bm: 'Malay Only Title',
    cn: '',
    ta: '',
  };
  assert.equal(getLocalized(objWithBm, 'cn'), 'Malay Only Title');

  const objWithCn = {
    en: '',
    bm: '',
    cn: '中文标题',
    ta: '',
  };
  assert.equal(getLocalized(objWithCn, 'bm'), '中文标题');
});

test('getLocalized: returns empty string when object is undefined or empty', () => {
  assert.equal(getLocalized(undefined), '');
  assert.equal(getLocalized(null), '');
  assert.equal(getLocalized({ en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' }), '');
});

// Test 2: t(key) dictionary lookup logic
function createTranslator(dictionary, currentLang) {
  return function t(key, fallback) {
    const row = dictionary[key];
    if (row) {
      const activeVal = row[currentLang]?.trim();
      if (activeVal) return activeVal;
      const enVal = row.en?.trim();
      if (enVal) return enVal;
      const bmVal = row.bm?.trim();
      if (bmVal) return bmVal;
      const cnVal = row.cn?.trim();
      if (cnVal) return cnVal;
      const taVal = row.ta?.trim();
      if (taVal) return taVal;
    }
    return fallback !== undefined ? fallback : key;
  };
}

test('t(key): looks up active language from dictionary', () => {
  const dictionary = {
    install_app: {
      key: 'install_app',
      en: 'Install App',
      bm: 'Pasang Aplikasi',
      cn: '安装应用',
      ta: 'செயலியை நிறுவு',
    },
    only_english: {
      key: 'only_english',
      en: 'Only English Text',
      bm: '',
      cn: '',
      ta: '',
    },
  };

  const tCn = createTranslator(dictionary, 'cn');
  assert.equal(tCn('install_app'), '安装应用');
  assert.equal(tCn('only_english'), 'Only English Text'); // fallback to English
  assert.equal(tCn('missing_key', 'Fallback Text'), 'Fallback Text');
  assert.equal(tCn('missing_key'), 'missing_key');

  const tBm = createTranslator(dictionary, 'bm');
  assert.equal(tBm('install_app'), 'Pasang Aplikasi');
  assert.equal(tBm('only_english'), 'Only English Text');
});

// Test 3: toggleSavedTool logic
test('toggleSavedTool: adds and removes tool IDs correctly', () => {
  let savedToolIds = [];

  function toggleSavedTool(id) {
    const exists = savedToolIds.includes(id);
    savedToolIds = exists ? savedToolIds.filter((item) => item !== id) : [...savedToolIds, id];
    return savedToolIds;
  }

  toggleSavedTool('tool-1');
  assert.deepEqual(savedToolIds, ['tool-1']);

  toggleSavedTool('tool-2');
  assert.deepEqual(savedToolIds, ['tool-1', 'tool-2']);

  toggleSavedTool('tool-1');
  assert.deepEqual(savedToolIds, ['tool-2']);

  toggleSavedTool('tool-2');
  assert.deepEqual(savedToolIds, []);
});

// Test 5: Tamil language pill visibility based on populated dictionary
test('language switcher: only includes Tamil when ta entries are populated', () => {
  function getSupportedLangs(dictionary) {
    const hasTamil = Object.values(dictionary).some(
      (item) => typeof item.ta === 'string' && item.ta.trim().length > 0
    );
    return [
      { code: 'en', label: 'EN' },
      { code: 'bm', label: 'BM' },
      { code: 'cn', label: '中文' },
      ...(hasTamil ? [{ code: 'ta', label: 'தமிழ்' }] : []),
    ];
  }

  const dictWithoutTamil = {
    app_title: { key: 'app_title', en: 'Team Hub', bm: 'Hab Pasukan', cn: '团队中心', ta: '' },
    nav_tools: { key: 'nav_tools', en: 'Tools', bm: 'Alat', cn: '工具箱', ta: '   ' },
  };
  const langs1 = getSupportedLangs(dictWithoutTamil);
  assert.equal(langs1.length, 3);
  assert.equal(langs1.some((l) => l.code === 'ta'), false);

  const dictWithTamil = {
    app_title: { key: 'app_title', en: 'Team Hub', bm: 'Hab Pasukan', cn: '团队中心', ta: 'குழு மையம்' },
  };
  const langs2 = getSupportedLangs(dictWithTamil);
  assert.equal(langs2.length, 4);
  assert.equal(langs2.some((l) => l.code === 'ta'), true);
});

// Test 6: Featured tools sorting (always sort featured tools to the top)
test('tool sorting: featured tools always sort to the top of the section', () => {
  const tools = [
    { id: '1', title: { en: 'Tool A' }, is_featured: false, sort_order: 1 },
    { id: '2', title: { en: 'Tool B' }, is_featured: true, sort_order: 3 },
    { id: '3', title: { en: 'Tool C' }, is_featured: false, sort_order: 2 },
    { id: '4', title: { en: 'Tool D' }, is_featured: true, sort_order: 1 },
  ];

  const sorted = [...tools].sort((a, b) => {
    if (a.is_featured !== b.is_featured) {
      return a.is_featured ? -1 : 1;
    }
    return (a.sort_order ?? 0) - (b.sort_order ?? 0);
  });

  assert.equal(sorted[0].id, '4'); // Featured, sort_order 1
  assert.equal(sorted[1].id, '2'); // Featured, sort_order 3
  assert.equal(sorted[2].id, '1'); // Unfeatured, sort_order 1
  assert.equal(sorted[3].id, '3'); // Unfeatured, sort_order 2
});

// Test 7: Real-time search filter across Title, Description, Speaker, and Topics in all languages
test('filterTools: searches in real-time across Title, Description, Speaker, and Topics in all languages', () => {
  const sampleTools = [
    {
      id: 'tool-1',
      title: { en: 'Elevator Pitch', bm: 'Skrip Pengenalan', cn: '电梯演讲', ta: '' },
      description: { en: 'How to pitch in 30 seconds', bm: 'Cara bermula', cn: '开场白', ta: '' },
      speaker_name: 'Marcus Vance',
      topics: { en: ['Cold Market', 'Pitch'], bm: ['Pasaran Dingin'], cn: ['开场'], ta: [] },
    },
    {
      id: 'tool-2',
      title: { en: 'Objection Clinic', bm: 'Klinik Bantahan', cn: '异议拆解', ta: '' },
      description: { en: 'Handle money objections', bm: 'Bantahan duit', cn: '没钱异议', ta: '' },
      speaker_name: 'Sarah Lim',
      topics: { en: ['Closing'], bm: ['Closing'], cn: ['成交'], ta: [] },
    },
  ];

  function filterByQuery(tools, query) {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return tools;
    return tools.filter((tool) => {
      const matchSpeaker = tool.speaker_name?.toLowerCase().includes(q);
      const matchTitle = Object.values(tool.title || {}).some(
        (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
      const matchDesc = Object.values(tool.description || {}).some(
        (v) => typeof v === 'string' && v.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
      const matchTopics = Object.values(tool.topics || {}).some(
        (arr) => Array.isArray(arr) && arr.some((item) => item?.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      );
      return matchSpeaker || matchTitle || matchDesc || matchTopics;
    });
  }

  // Match by English Speaker
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, 'Marcus').length, 1);
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, 'marcus')[0].id, 'tool-1');

  // Match by Chinese Title
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, '异议').length, 1);
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, '异议')[0].id, 'tool-2');

  // Match by Malay Description
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, 'duit').length, 1);
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, 'duit')[0].id, 'tool-2');

  // Match by Malay Topic
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, 'Pasaran').length, 1);
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, 'Pasaran')[0].id, 'tool-1');

  // Non-matching query
  assert.equal(filterByQuery(sampleTools, 'NonExistentXYZ').length, 0);
});

// Test 8: WhatsApp share text formulation
test('whatsapp share: formats localized script and url correctly', () => {
  function formatWhatsAppText(script, title, url) {
    if (script && script.trim()) {
      return `${script.trim()}\n\n${title}\n${url}`;
    }
    return `${title}\n${url}`;
  }

  const withScript = formatWhatsAppText(
    'Check out this video:',
    'Pitch 101',
    'https://example.com/pitch'
  );
  assert.equal(withScript, 'Check out this video:\n\nPitch 101\nhttps://example.com/pitch');

  const withoutScript = formatWhatsAppText('', 'Pitch 101', 'https://example.com/pitch');
  assert.equal(withoutScript, 'Pitch 101\nhttps://example.com/pitch');
});


// Test 9: Admin Role Authorization Matrix
test('adminActions: role-based authorization matrix enforces correct permissions', () => {
  const ROLES = {
    EDITOR: 'editor',
    ADMIN: 'admin',
    SUPERADMIN: 'superadmin',
  };

  const PERMISSIONS = {
    sections: ['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'],
    tools: ['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'],
    mediaUpload: ['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'],
    calendar: ['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin'],
    dictionary: ['admin', 'superadmin'],
    pins: ['superadmin'],
  };

  function isAuthorized(userRole, feature) {
    const allowed = PERMISSIONS[feature];
    return allowed ? allowed.includes(userRole) : false;
  }

  // Editor permissions
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.EDITOR, 'sections'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.EDITOR, 'tools'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.EDITOR, 'mediaUpload'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.EDITOR, 'calendar'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.EDITOR, 'dictionary'), false);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.EDITOR, 'pins'), false);

  // Admin permissions
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.ADMIN, 'sections'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.ADMIN, 'tools'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.ADMIN, 'calendar'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.ADMIN, 'dictionary'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.ADMIN, 'pins'), false);

  // Superadmin permissions
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.SUPERADMIN, 'sections'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.SUPERADMIN, 'tools'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.SUPERADMIN, 'calendar'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.SUPERADMIN, 'dictionary'), true);
  assert.equal(isAuthorized(ROLES.SUPERADMIN, 'pins'), true);
});

// Test 10: PIN Session serialization and self-deletion restriction
test('adminActions: session serialization and self-deletion guard', () => {
  function serializeSession(pinRecord) {
    if (!pinRecord.is_active) {
      throw new Error('PIN is not active');
    }
    const validRoles = ['superadmin', 'admin', 'editor'];
    if (!validRoles.includes(pinRecord.role)) {
      throw new Error('Invalid role');
    }
    return JSON.stringify({
      id: pinRecord.id,
      label: pinRecord.label,
      role: pinRecord.role,
    });
  }

  const activeRecord = {
    id: 'pin-123',
    label: 'Agency Leader',
    role: 'superadmin',
    is_active: true,
  };

  const serialized = serializeSession(activeRecord);
  const parsed = JSON.parse(serialized);
  assert.equal(parsed.id, 'pin-123');
  assert.equal(parsed.role, 'superadmin');
  assert.equal(parsed.label, 'Agency Leader');

  // Deactivated PIN should fail
  assert.throws(
    () => serializeSession({ ...activeRecord, is_active: false }),
    /PIN is not active/
  );

  // Self-deletion check
  function canDeletePin(currentSessionId, targetPinId) {
    if (currentSessionId === targetPinId) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Cannot delete own active PIN session' };
    }
    return { success: true };
  }

  assert.equal(canDeletePin('pin-123', 'pin-123').success, false);
  assert.equal(canDeletePin('pin-123', 'pin-456').success, true);
});

// Test 11: Tool upsert payload schema and language fallback
test('adminActions: tool upsert payload formatting with all new fields', () => {
  function buildToolPayload(tool) {
    const validLangCodes = ['en', 'bm', 'cn', 'ta'];
    const filteredLangs = Array.isArray(tool.available_languages)
      ? tool.available_languages.filter((l) => validLangCodes.includes(l))
      : [];
    const safeLanguages = filteredLangs.length > 0 ? filteredLangs : ['en'];

    return {
      section_id: tool.section_id,
      type: tool.type || 'link',
      title: {
        en: tool.title?.en?.trim() || '',
        bm: tool.title?.bm?.trim() || '',
        cn: tool.title?.cn?.trim() || '',
        ta: tool.title?.ta?.trim() || '',
      },
      speaker_name: tool.speaker_name?.trim() || '',
      duration: tool.duration?.trim() || '',
      available_languages: safeLanguages,
      topics: {
        en: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.en) ? tool.topics.en : [],
        bm: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.bm) ? tool.topics.bm : [],
        cn: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.cn) ? tool.topics.cn : [],
        ta: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.ta) ? tool.topics.ta : [],
      },
      url: {
        en: tool.url?.en?.trim() || '',
        bm: tool.url?.bm?.trim() || '',
        cn: tool.url?.cn?.trim() || '',
        ta: tool.url?.ta?.trim() || '',
      },
      whatsapp_script: {
        en: tool.whatsapp_script?.en?.trim() || '',
        bm: tool.whatsapp_script?.bm?.trim() || '',
        cn: tool.whatsapp_script?.cn?.trim() || '',
        ta: tool.whatsapp_script?.ta?.trim() || '',
      },
      thumbnail_url: tool.thumbnail_url?.trim() || '',
      is_featured: Boolean(tool.is_featured),
      sort_order: typeof tool.sort_order === 'number' ? tool.sort_order : 0,
      is_visible: tool.is_visible !== false,
    };
  }

  const payload = buildToolPayload({
    section_id: 'sec-1',
    title: { en: 'Audio Pitch', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' },
    speaker_name: 'David',
    duration: '4:20',
    available_languages: ['en', 'bm', 'invalid_code'],
    is_featured: true,
    topics: { en: ['Cold Calling'] },
    whatsapp_script: { en: 'Hi, take a look at this' },
    url: { en: 'https://youtu.be/xyz' },
  });

  assert.equal(payload.speaker_name, 'David');
  assert.equal(payload.duration, '4:20');
  assert.deepEqual(payload.available_languages, ['en', 'bm']);
  assert.equal(payload.is_featured, true);
  assert.deepEqual(payload.topics.en, ['Cold Calling']);
  assert.deepEqual(payload.topics.cn, []);
  assert.equal(payload.whatsapp_script.en, 'Hi, take a look at this');
  assert.equal(payload.url.en, 'https://youtu.be/xyz');
});

// Test 12: Admin Dashboard Dictionary search filter
test('admin: dictionary filtering matches key, EN, BM, CN, or TA', () => {
  const dictionary = [
    { key: 'btn_save', en: 'Save', bm: 'Simpan', cn: '保存', ta: 'சேமிக்க' },
    { key: 'btn_cancel', en: 'Cancel', bm: 'Batal', cn: '取消', ta: 'ரத்து செய்' },
    { key: 'btn_share', en: 'Share Flyer', bm: 'Kongsi', cn: '分享海报', ta: '' },
  ];

  function filterDictionary(dict, query, filterMissing) {
    return dict.filter((item) => {
      if (filterMissing) {
        const hasMissing = !item.bm?.trim() || !item.cn?.trim() || !item.ta?.trim();
        if (!hasMissing) return false;
      }
      if (!query.trim()) return true;
      const q = query.toLowerCase();
      return (
        item.key.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.en?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.bm?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.cn?.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        item.ta?.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      );
    });
  }

  assert.equal(filterDictionary(dictionary, 'simpan', false).length, 1);
  assert.equal(filterDictionary(dictionary, 'simpan', false)[0].key, 'btn_save');
  assert.equal(filterDictionary(dictionary, '取消', false).length, 1);
  assert.equal(filterDictionary(dictionary, '', true).length, 1);
  assert.equal(filterDictionary(dictionary, '', true)[0].key, 'btn_share');
});

// Test 13: Section slug auto-generation fallback
test('admin: section slug generation produces sanitized URL-safe string', () => {
  function generateSlug(titleEn) {
    return (
      titleEn
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/^-|-$/g, '') || `section-${Date.now()}`
    );
  }

  assert.equal(generateSlug('Prospecting & Scripts!'), 'prospecting-scripts');
  assert.equal(generateSlug('Quick 10-Minute Launch'), 'quick-10-minute-launch');
  assert.ok(generateSlug('   ').startsWith('section-'));
});


// Test 14: YouTube ID extraction with space stripping and 10-12 char support
test('youtube: extracts 10 to 12-char ID and strips spaces from various YouTube URL formats', () => {
  function getYouTubeId(rawUrl) {
    if (!rawUrl) return null;
    const cleanUrl = rawUrl.replace(/\s+/g, '');
    const match = cleanUrl.match(
