      /(?:youtu\.be\/|youtube\.com\/(?:embed\/|v\/|watch\?.*[?&]v=|watch\?v=|shorts\/))([\w-]{10,12})/
    ) || cleanUrl.match(/(?:youtu\.be\/)([\w-]{10,12})/);
    return match ? match[1] : null;
  }

  assert.equal(getYouTubeId('https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dQw4w9WgXcQ'), 'dQw4w9WgXcQ');
  assert.equal(getYouTubeId('https://youtu.be/dQw4w9WgXcQ'), 'dQw4w9WgXcQ');
  assert.equal(getYouTubeId(' https:// youtu.be / dQw4w9WgXcQ '), 'dQw4w9WgXcQ'); // accidental spaces stripped
  assert.equal(getYouTubeId('https://youtu.be/1234567890'), '1234567890'); // 10 chars
  assert.equal(getYouTubeId('https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=123456789012'), '123456789012'); // 12 chars
  assert.equal(getYouTubeId('https://www.youtube.com/shorts/dQw4w9WgXcQ'), 'dQw4w9WgXcQ');
  assert.equal(getYouTubeId('https://www.youtube.com/embed/dQw4w9WgXcQ'), 'dQw4w9WgXcQ');
  assert.equal(getYouTubeId('https://example.com/not-youtube'), null);
});

// Test 15: Super Admin Master PIN 371983 fallback
test('admin: PIN 371983 always returns superadmin master session', () => {
  function verifyPinMock(pin) {
    const trimmed = (pin || '').trim();
    if (trimmed === '371983') {
      return {
        success: true,
        session: {
          id: 'master-superadmin',
          label: 'Master Super Admin',
          role: 'superadmin',
        },
      };
    }
    return { success: false, error: 'Invalid PIN' };
  }

  const res = verifyPinMock('  371983  ');
  assert.equal(res.success, true);
  assert.equal(res.session.role, 'superadmin');
  assert.equal(res.session.id, 'master-superadmin');

  assert.equal(verifyPinMock('wrong').success, false);
});

// Test 16: WhatsApp full section formatting
test('whatsapp: full section format outputs clean list of tools', () => {
  function formatSectionWhatsApp(sectionTitle, sectionDesc, tools) {
    const header = `🔥 *${sectionTitle}*${sectionDesc ? `\n_${sectionDesc}_\n` : '\n'}\n`;
    const list = tools
      .map((tool, idx) => {
        const speaker = tool.speaker ? ` (${tool.speaker})` : '';
        return `${idx + 1}. *${tool.title}*${speaker}\n👉 ${tool.url}`;
      })
      .join('\n\n');
    return `${header}${list}\n\n_Shared via AA Team Hub_`;
  }

  const text = formatSectionWhatsApp('Onboarding', 'Step 1 for new partners', [
    { title: 'Welcome Video', speaker: 'Marcus', url: 'https://youtu.be/abc' },
    { title: 'Playbook PDF', speaker: '', url: 'https://cdn.com/playbook.pdf' },
  ]);

  assert.ok(text.includes('🔥 *Onboarding*'));
  assert.ok(text.includes('1. *Welcome Video* (Marcus)'));
  assert.ok(text.includes('2. *Playbook PDF*'));
  assert.ok(text.includes('👉 https://cdn.com/playbook.pdf'));
});

// Test 17: Admin tool payload fallback to English for empty BM/CN/TA
test('admin: tool upsert falls back to English title and URL when localized fields are blank', () => {
  function prepareToolPayload(tool) {
    const enTitle = tool.title?.en?.trim() || '';
    const enUrl = tool.url?.en?.trim() || '';
    return {
      title: {
        en: enTitle,
        bm: tool.title?.bm?.trim() || enTitle,
        cn: tool.title?.cn?.trim() || enTitle,
        ta: tool.title?.ta?.trim() || enTitle,
      },
      url: {
        en: enUrl,
        bm: tool.url?.bm?.trim() || enUrl,
        cn: tool.url?.cn?.trim() || enUrl,
        ta: tool.url?.ta?.trim() || enUrl,
      },
    };
  }

  const payload = prepareToolPayload({
    title: { en: 'Product Masterclass', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' },
    url: { en: 'https://youtu.be/masterclass', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' },
  });

  assert.equal(payload.title.bm, 'Product Masterclass');
  assert.equal(payload.title.cn, 'Product Masterclass');
  assert.equal(payload.url.bm, 'https://youtu.be/masterclass');
  assert.equal(payload.url.cn, 'https://youtu.be/masterclass');
});
// Test 18: Dynamic calendar recurring rules expansion and blackout filtering
test('calendar: expands recurring rules, filters blackout dates, and merges special events', async () => {
  const { generateMonthSchedule } = await import('../src/lib/calendarGenerator.ts');

  const recurringRules = [
    {
      id: 'dcc',
      title: 'Daily DCC',
      frequency: 'daily',
      time: '7:30 AM',
      venue: 'Zoom',
      address_or_link: 'https://zoom.us/dcc',
      is_active: true,
    },
    {
      id: 'asap',
      title: 'Weekly Thursday ASAP',
      frequency: 'weekly',
      day_of_week: 4, // Thursday
      time: '8:30 PM',
      venue: 'Nu Skin Plaza',
      address_or_link: 'Kuala Lumpur',
      is_active: true,
    },
    {
      id: 'bse',
      title: 'Monthly BSE 1',
      frequency: 'monthly_nth_weekday',
      nth_week: 3,
      day_of_week: 6, // 3rd Saturday
      time: '10:00 AM',
      venue: 'Wisma Central',
      address_or_link: 'KL',
      is_active: true,
    },
  ];

  const blackoutDates = [
    {
      id: 'bo-1',
      date: '2026-11-21', // 3rd Saturday of Nov 2026
      reason: 'Replaced by Rally',
      rule_id: 'bse',
    },
  ];

  const specialEvents = [
    {
      id: 'rally-2026',
      date: '2026-11-21',
      time: '2:00 PM',
      title: { en: 'AA Annual Rally', bm: 'Rali AA', cn: '年度大聚会', ta: 'Rally' },
      speaker: 'Presidential Council',
      location_or_link: 'Sunway Pyramid',
    },
  ];

  const result = generateMonthSchedule(2026, 11, recurringRules, blackoutDates, specialEvents);

  // November 2026 has 30 days
  // Daily DCC should appear on all 30 days
  const dccEvents = result.filter((e) => e.rule_id === 'dcc');
  assert.equal(dccEvents.length, 30);

  // November 2026 Thursdays: Nov 5, 12, 19, 26 -> 4 Thursdays
  const asapEvents = result.filter((e) => e.rule_id === 'asap');
  assert.equal(asapEvents.length, 4);

  // BSE was blacked out on 2026-11-21
  const bseEvents = result.filter((e) => e.rule_id === 'bse');
  assert.equal(bseEvents.length, 0);

  // Special Rally event should be present on 2026-11-21
  const rally = result.find((e) => e.id === 'rally-2026');
  assert.ok(rally);
  assert.equal(rally.date, '2026-11-21');
  assert.equal(rally.is_recurring, false);

  // Results should be strictly sorted chronologically
  for (let i = 0; i < result.length - 1; i++) {
    assert.ok(result[i].date <= result[i + 1].date);
  }
});

// Test 19: isValidUuid helper prevents UUID syntax errors on mock data IDs
test('admin: isValidUuid helper correctly distinguishes UUIDs from mock string IDs', () => {
  const isValidUuid = (str) =>
    Boolean(str && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(str));

  assert.equal(isValidUuid('cebbb725-ce7c-43c6-8d33-ea19d948bae0'), true);
  assert.equal(isValidUuid('8305a7da-8972-4cc7-a44e-b2d9e1faaa35'), true);
  assert.equal(isValidUuid('tool-wow'), false);
  assert.equal(isValidUuid('1'), false);
  assert.equal(isValidUuid(''), false);
  assert.equal(isValidUuid(undefined), false);
});

// Test 20: Meeting series exception overrides (modify & reschedule to another date, or cancel)
test('calendar: exception overrides can reschedule an occurrence to another date or cancel it', async () => {
  const { generateMonthSchedule } = await import('../src/lib/calendarGenerator.ts');

  const meetingSeries = [
    {
      id: 'bse',
      title: 'BSE 1',
      frequency: 'monthly_nth_weekday',
      nth_week: 3,
      day_of_week: 6, // 3rd Saturday: Nov 21, 2026
      time: '10:00 AM',
      venue: 'Wisma Central',
      address_or_link: 'KL',
      is_active: true,
    },
  ];

  // Reschedule 3rd Saturday (Nov 21) to 4th Saturday (Nov 28) for Nov 2026 only!
  const exceptions = [
    {
      id: 'ex-1',
      series_id: 'bse',
      original_date: '2026-11-21',
      action: 'modify',
      new_date: '2026-11-28',
      new_time: '11:00 AM',
      new_title: 'BSE 1 (Special Date)',
      reason: 'Moved for this month only',
    },
  ];

  const schedule = generateMonthSchedule(2026, 11, meetingSeries, [], [], exceptions);

  // Original date (Nov 21) should NOT have BSE
  const nov21 = schedule.find((e) => e.date === '2026-11-21');
  assert.equal(nov21, undefined);

  // New date (Nov 28) should have rescheduled BSE with updated fields
  const nov28 = schedule.find((e) => e.date === '2026-11-28');
  assert.ok(nov28);
  assert.equal(nov28.title.en, 'BSE 1 (Special Date)');
  assert.equal(nov28.time, '11:00 AM');
  assert.equal(nov28.is_modified, true);
});

// Test 21: Auto-cleanup of one-time series and exceptions older than 2 months prior
test('calendar: pruneCalendarData cleans up records older than 2 months prior', async () => {
  const { pruneCalendarData, getTwoMonthsPriorCutoff } = await import('../src/lib/calendarGenerator.ts');

  const cutoff = getTwoMonthsPriorCutoff(new Date('2026-11-15'));
  assert.equal(cutoff, '2026-09-01');

  const calendarData = {
    recurring_rules: [
      { id: '1', title: 'Recurring Rule', frequency: 'weekly' },
      { id: '2', title: 'Old One-Time Event', frequency: 'once', date: '2026-08-15' }, // older than 2026-09-01 -> pruned!
      { id: '3', title: 'Recent One-Time Event', frequency: 'once', date: '2026-10-01' }, // kept
    ],
    exceptions: [
      { id: 'e1', series_id: '1', original_date: '2026-07-01', action: 'cancel' }, // pruned
      { id: 'e2', series_id: '1', original_date: '2026-10-15', action: 'cancel' }, // kept
    ],
  };

  const pruned = pruneCalendarData(calendarData);
  assert.equal(pruned.recurring_rules.length, 2);
  assert.equal(pruned.recurring_rules.some((r) => r.id === '2'), false);
  assert.equal(pruned.exceptions.length, 1);
  assert.equal(pruned.exceptions[0].id, 'e2');
});

// Test 22: splitUpcomingMeetings returns next 2 upcoming meetings from today onward
test('calendar: splitUpcomingMeetings separates the next 2 meetings from the rest', async () => {
  const { splitUpcomingMeetings } = await import('../src/lib/calendarGenerator.ts');

  const events = [
    { id: 'e1', date: '2026-11-01', time: '10:00 AM' }, // past
    { id: 'e2', date: '2026-11-10', time: '10:00 AM' }, // upcoming 1
    { id: 'e3', date: '2026-11-15', time: '10:00 AM' }, // upcoming 2
    { id: 'e4', date: '2026-11-20', time: '10:00 AM' }, // remaining 1
    { id: 'e5', date: '2026-11-25', time: '10:00 AM' }, // remaining 2
  ];

  const { nextTwo, remaining } = splitUpcomingMeetings(events, '2026-11-06');
  assert.equal(nextTwo.length, 2);
  assert.equal(nextTwo[0].id, 'e2');
  assert.equal(nextTwo[1].id, 'e3');

  assert.equal(remaining.length, 2);
  assert.equal(remaining[0].id, 'e4');
  assert.equal(remaining[1].id, 'e5');
});
</file>

<file path="src/app/actions/adminActions.ts">
'use server';

import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache';
import { supabaseAdmin, getSupabaseAdminClient } from '@/lib/supabase';
import {
  DEFAULT_SECTIONS,
  DEFAULT_TOOLS,
  DEFAULT_CALENDAR,
} from '@/lib/mockData';
import { DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY } from '@/lib/dictionary';
import { pruneCalendarData, unpackCalendar } from '@/lib/calendarGenerator';
import type {
  AccessPin,
  AccessPinRole,
  Database,
  LanguageCode,
  Section,
  TeamCalendar,
  Tool,
  UiDictionary,
  AdminSession,
  AdminDashboardData,
  ActionResponse,
} from '@/types/app';
import { PIN_SESSION_COOKIE } from '@/types/app';
import type { SupabaseClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

const isValidUuid = (str?: string) =>
  Boolean(str && /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(str));

function getAdminClient(): SupabaseClient<Database> {
  try {
    return getSupabaseAdminClient();
  } catch {
    return supabaseAdmin;
  }
}

function safeRevalidate(paths: string[] = ['/', '/admin']) {
  try {
    for (const p of paths) {
      revalidatePath(p);
    }
  } catch {
    // Revalidation ignored in testing or non-standard environments
  }
}

async function requireSession(allowedRoles?: AccessPinRole[]): Promise<AdminSession> {
  const cookieStore = await cookies();
  const sessionCookie = cookieStore.get(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);

  if (!sessionCookie?.value) {
    throw new Error('Authentication required: No active PIN session found.');
  }

  let session: AdminSession;
  try {
    session = JSON.parse(sessionCookie.value);
  } catch {
    cookieStore.delete(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);
    throw new Error('Invalid session format. Please log in again.');
  }

  if (
    !session?.id ||
    !session?.role ||
    !['superadmin', 'admin', 'editor'].includes(session.role)
  ) {
    cookieStore.delete(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);
    throw new Error('Corrupt session data. Please log in again.');
  }

  // Master Super Admin session bypasses DB query check
  if (session.id === 'master-superadmin') {
    session.role = 'superadmin';
    session.label = session.label || 'Master Super Admin';
    return session;
  }

  const client = getAdminClient();
  const { data: pinRow, error } = await client
    .from('access_pins')
    .select('id, label, role, is_active')
    .eq('id', session.id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (error) {
    console.warn('[requireSession] Database query error when verifying session:', error.message);
  } else if (!pinRow || !pinRow.is_active) {
    cookieStore.delete(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);
    throw new Error('Session has expired or PIN access has been deactivated.');
  } else {
    session.role = pinRow.role as AccessPinRole;
    session.label = pinRow.label;
  }

  if (allowedRoles && allowedRoles.length > 0 && !allowedRoles.includes(session.role)) {
    throw new Error(
      `Access denied: Role '${session.role}' is not authorized to perform this operation.`
    );
  }

  return session;
}
// =============================================================================
// 1. PIN AUTHENTICATION & SESSION MANAGEMENT
// =============================================================================

export async function verifyPinAction(pin: string): Promise<ActionResponse<AdminSession>> {
  try {
    if (!pin || typeof pin !== 'string' || !pin.trim()) {
      return { success: false, error: 'PIN is required' };
    }

    const trimmedPin = pin.trim();

    // Guaranteed master fallback for Super Admin PIN '371983'
    if (trimmedPin === '371983') {
      const masterSession: AdminSession = {
        id: 'master-superadmin',
        label: 'Master Super Admin',
        role: 'superadmin',
      };

      const cookieStore = await cookies();
      cookieStore.set(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE, JSON.stringify(masterSession), {
        httpOnly: true,
        secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
        sameSite: 'lax',
        path: '/',
        maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
      });

      return {
        success: true,
        session: masterSession,
        data: masterSession,
      };
    }

    const client = getAdminClient();

    const { data: pinRecord, error } = await client
      .from('access_pins')
      .select('id, label, pin_code, role, is_active')
      .eq('pin_code', trimmedPin)
      .eq('is_active', true)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) {
      console.error('[verifyPinAction] Supabase query error:', error);
      return { success: false, error: 'Authentication service error. Please try again.' };
    }

    if (!pinRecord) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Invalid or inactive PIN' };
    }

    const validRoles: AccessPinRole[] = ['superadmin', 'admin', 'editor'];
    if (!validRoles.includes(pinRecord.role as AccessPinRole)) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized role configured for this PIN' };
    }

    const sessionPayload: AdminSession = {
      id: pinRecord.id,
      label: pinRecord.label,
      role: pinRecord.role as AccessPinRole,
    };

    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    cookieStore.set(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE, JSON.stringify(sessionPayload), {
      httpOnly: true,
      secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
      sameSite: 'lax',
      path: '/',
      maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 7,
    });

    return {
      success: true,
      session: sessionPayload,
      data: sessionPayload,
    };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Authentication failed';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function getSessionAction(): Promise<ActionResponse<AdminSession | null>> {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    const sessionCookie = cookieStore.get(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);

    if (!sessionCookie?.value) {
      return { success: true, session: null, data: null };
    }

    let session: AdminSession;
    try {
      session = JSON.parse(sessionCookie.value);
    } catch {
      cookieStore.delete(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);
      return { success: true, session: null, data: null };
    }

    if (
      !session?.id ||
      !session?.role ||
      !['superadmin', 'admin', 'editor'].includes(session.role)
    ) {
      cookieStore.delete(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);
      return { success: true, session: null, data: null };
    }

    if (session.id === 'master-superadmin') {
      return { success: true, session, data: session };
    }

    const client = getAdminClient();
    const { data: pinRecord, error } = await client
      .from('access_pins')
      .select('id, label, role, is_active')
      .eq('id', session.id)
      .maybeSingle();

    if (error) {
      console.warn('[getSessionAction] DB check warning:', error.message);
      return { success: true, session, data: session };
    }

    if (!pinRecord || !pinRecord.is_active) {
      cookieStore.delete(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);
      return {
        success: false,
        session: null,
        data: null,
        error: 'Session expired or PIN deactivated',
      };
    }

    const verifiedSession: AdminSession = {
      id: pinRecord.id,
      label: pinRecord.label,
      role: pinRecord.role as AccessPinRole,
    };

    return {
      success: true,
      session: verifiedSession,
      data: verifiedSession,
    };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to retrieve session';
    return { success: false, error: message, session: null, data: null };
  }
}

export async function logoutPinAction(): Promise<ActionResponse<void>> {
  try {
    const cookieStore = await cookies();
    cookieStore.delete(PIN_SESSION_COOKIE);
    return { success: true, message: 'Logged out successfully' };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Logout failed';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function getAdminDashboardDataAction(): Promise<ActionResponse<AdminDashboardData>> {
  try {
    const session = await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    const client = getAdminClient();

    const [secRes, toolRes, calRes, dictRes] = await Promise.all([
      client.from('sections').select('*').order('sort_order', { ascending: true }),
      client.from('tools').select('*').order('sort_order', { ascending: true }),
      client.from('team_calendar').select('*').limit(1),
      client.from('ui_dictionary').select('*').order('key', { ascending: true }),
    ]);

    const sections: Section[] =
      secRes.data && secRes.data.length > 0 ? (secRes.data as Section[]) : DEFAULT_SECTIONS;
    const tools: Tool[] =
      toolRes.data && toolRes.data.length > 0 ? (toolRes.data as Tool[]) : DEFAULT_TOOLS;
    const calendar: TeamCalendar =
      calRes.data && calRes.data.length > 0
        ? unpackCalendar(calRes.data[0] as TeamCalendar)
        : DEFAULT_CALENDAR;

    const dictMap: Record<string, UiDictionary> = { ...DEFAULT_UI_DICTIONARY };
    if (dictRes.data && dictRes.data.length > 0) {
      for (const d of dictRes.data as UiDictionary[]) {
        dictMap[d.key] = d;
      }
    }
    const dictionary: UiDictionary[] = Object.values(dictMap);

    let pins: AccessPin[] | undefined;
    if (session.role === 'superadmin') {
      const pinsRes = await client
        .from('access_pins')
        .select('*')
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      pins = !pinsRes.error && pinsRes.data ? (pinsRes.data as AccessPin[]) : [];
    }

    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        session,
        sections,
        tools,
        calendar,
        dictionary,
        pins,
      },
    };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to retrieve admin data';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// 2. ROLE-GUARDED ACTIONS: EDITOR, ADMIN, SUPERADMIN
// =============================================================================

export async function upsertSectionAction(
  section: Partial<Section>
): Promise<ActionResponse<Section>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    const client = getAdminClient();

    const enTitle = section.title?.en?.trim() || '';
    if (!enTitle) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Section title (EN) is required' };
    }

    const generatedSlug =
      section.slug?.trim() ||
      enTitle
        .toLowerCase()
        .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-')
        .replace(/^-|-$/g, '') ||
      `section-${Date.now()}`;

    const payload = {
      slug: generatedSlug,
      icon_name: section.icon_name || 'Folder',
      sort_order: typeof section.sort_order === 'number' ? section.sort_order : 0,
      is_visible: section.is_visible !== false,
      title: {
        en: enTitle,
        bm: section.title?.bm?.trim() || enTitle,
        cn: section.title?.cn?.trim() || enTitle,
        ta: section.title?.ta?.trim() || enTitle,
      },
      description: {
        en: section.description?.en?.trim() || '',
        bm: section.description?.bm?.trim() || section.description?.en?.trim() || '',
        cn: section.description?.cn?.trim() || section.description?.en?.trim() || '',
        ta: section.description?.ta?.trim() || section.description?.en?.trim() || '',
      },
    };

    let result;
    if (isValidUuid(section.id)) {
      result = await client
        .from('sections')
        .update(payload)
        .eq('id', section.id!)
        .select()
        .single();
    } else {
      result = await client
        .from('sections')
        .insert(payload)
        .select()
        .single();
    }

    if (result.error) {
      throw new Error(result.error.message);
    }

    safeRevalidate();
    return { success: true, data: result.data as Section };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to save section';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function deleteSectionAction(id: string): Promise<ActionResponse<void>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    if (!id) return { success: false, error: 'Section ID is required' };

    const client = getAdminClient();

    // Remove associated tools
    await client.from('tools').delete().eq('section_id', id);

    const { error } = await client.from('sections').delete().eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

    safeRevalidate();
    return { success: true, message: 'Section deleted successfully' };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete section';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function reorderSectionsAction(
  items: { id: string; sort_order: number }[] | string[]
): Promise<ActionResponse<void>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: 'No sections provided to reorder' };
    }

    const client = getAdminClient();
    const normalized: { id: string; sort_order: number }[] = items.map((item, index) => {
      if (typeof item === 'string') {
        return { id: item, sort_order: index };
      }
      return { id: item.id, sort_order: item.sort_order ?? index };
    });

    const updates = normalized.map((item) =>
      client.from('sections').update({ sort_order: item.sort_order }).eq('id', item.id)
    );

    const results = await Promise.all(updates);
    const firstError = results.find((r) => r.error);
    if (firstError?.error) {
      throw new Error(firstError.error.message);
    }

    safeRevalidate();
    return { success: true, message: 'Sections reordered successfully' };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to reorder sections';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function reorderToolsAction(
  items: { id: string; sort_order: number }[] | string[]
): Promise<ActionResponse<void>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: 'No tools provided to reorder' };
    }

    const client = getAdminClient();
    const normalized: { id: string; sort_order: number }[] = items.map((item, index) => {
      if (typeof item === 'string') {
        return { id: item, sort_order: index };
      }
      return { id: item.id, sort_order: item.sort_order ?? index };
    });

    const updates = normalized.map((item) =>
      client.from('tools').update({ sort_order: item.sort_order }).eq('id', item.id)
    );

    const results = await Promise.all(updates);
    const firstError = results.find((r) => r.error);
    if (firstError?.error) {
      throw new Error(firstError.error.message);
    }

    safeRevalidate();
    return { success: true, message: 'Tools reordered successfully' };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to reorder tools';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function upsertToolAction(tool: Partial<Tool>): Promise<ActionResponse<Tool>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    const client = getAdminClient();

    if (!tool.section_id) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Section ID is required for a tool' };
    }
    const enTitle = tool.title?.en?.trim() || '';
    if (!enTitle) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Tool title (EN) is required' };
    }

    const enUrl = tool.url?.en?.trim() || '';
    const enDesc = tool.description?.en?.trim() || '';

    const validLangCodes: LanguageCode[] = ['en', 'bm', 'cn', 'ta'];
    const filteredLangs = Array.isArray(tool.available_languages)
      ? tool.available_languages.filter((l): l is LanguageCode =>
          validLangCodes.includes(l as LanguageCode)
        )
      : [];
    const safeLanguages: LanguageCode[] = filteredLangs.length > 0 ? filteredLangs : ['en'];

    const payload = {
      section_id: tool.section_id,
      type: tool.type || 'link',
      title: {
        en: enTitle,
        bm: tool.title?.bm?.trim() || enTitle,
        cn: tool.title?.cn?.trim() || enTitle,
        ta: tool.title?.ta?.trim() || enTitle,
      },
      description: {
        en: enDesc,
        bm: tool.description?.bm?.trim() || enDesc,
        cn: tool.description?.cn?.trim() || enDesc,
        ta: tool.description?.ta?.trim() || enDesc,
      },
      speaker_name: tool.speaker_name?.trim() || '',
      duration: tool.duration?.trim() || '',
      available_languages: safeLanguages,
      topics: {
        en: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.en) ? tool.topics.en : [],
        bm: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.bm) ? tool.topics.bm : [],
        cn: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.cn) ? tool.topics.cn : [],
        ta: Array.isArray(tool.topics?.ta) ? tool.topics.ta : [],
      },
      url: {
        en: enUrl,
        bm: tool.url?.bm?.trim() || enUrl,
        cn: tool.url?.cn?.trim() || enUrl,
        ta: tool.url?.ta?.trim() || enUrl,
      },
      whatsapp_script: {
        en: tool.whatsapp_script?.en?.trim() || '',
        bm: tool.whatsapp_script?.bm?.trim() || tool.whatsapp_script?.en?.trim() || '',
        cn: tool.whatsapp_script?.cn?.trim() || tool.whatsapp_script?.en?.trim() || '',
        ta: tool.whatsapp_script?.ta?.trim() || tool.whatsapp_script?.en?.trim() || '',
      },
      thumbnail_url: tool.thumbnail_url?.trim() || '',
      is_featured: Boolean(tool.is_featured),
      sort_order: typeof tool.sort_order === 'number' ? tool.sort_order : 0,
      is_visible: tool.is_visible !== false,
    };

    let result;
    if (isValidUuid(tool.id)) {
      result = await client
        .from('tools')
        .update(payload)
        .eq('id', tool.id!)
        .select()
        .single();
    } else {
      result = await client
        .from('tools')
        .insert(payload)
        .select()
        .single();
    }

    if (result.error) {
      throw new Error(result.error.message);
    }

    safeRevalidate();
    return { success: true, data: result.data as Tool };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to save tool';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function deleteToolAction(id: string): Promise<ActionResponse<void>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    if (!id) return { success: false, error: 'Tool ID is required' };

    const client = getAdminClient();
    const { error } = await client.from('tools').delete().eq('id', id);
    if (error) throw new Error(error.message);

    safeRevalidate();
    return { success: true, message: 'Tool deleted successfully' };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete tool';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function uploadMediaAction(formData: FormData): Promise<ActionResponse<{ url: string; path: string }>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);

    const file = (formData.get('file') || formData.get('media')) as File | null;
    if (!file || typeof file === 'string' || file.size === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: 'No valid file provided for upload' };
    }

    const client = getAdminClient();
    const bucketName = 'media';

    const sanitizedFileName = file.name.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9.-]/g, '_');
    const filePath = `uploads/${Date.now()}_${sanitizedFileName}`;

    const arrayBuffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
    const buffer = Buffer.from(arrayBuffer);

    const { error: uploadError } = await client.storage
      .from(bucketName)
      .upload(filePath, buffer, {
        contentType: file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
        upsert: true,
      });

    if (uploadError) {
      throw new Error(`Upload failed: ${uploadError.message}`);
    }

    const { data: urlData } = client.storage.from(bucketName).getPublicUrl(filePath);

    return {
      success: true,
      url: urlData.publicUrl,
      path: filePath,
      data: { url: urlData.publicUrl, path: filePath },
    };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Media upload failed';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// 3. ROLE-GUARDED ACTIONS: ADMIN AND SUPERADMIN ONLY
// =============================================================================

export async function updateCalendarAction(
  calendarData: Partial<TeamCalendar>
): Promise<ActionResponse<TeamCalendar>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['editor', 'admin', 'superadmin']);
    const client = getAdminClient();

    // Auto-cleanup records older than 2 months prior
    const cleanedCalendar = pruneCalendarData(calendarData);

    const recurringRules = Array.isArray(cleanedCalendar.recurring_rules)
      ? cleanedCalendar.recurring_rules
      : [];
    const exceptions = Array.isArray(cleanedCalendar.exceptions)
      ? cleanedCalendar.exceptions
      : [];
    const blackoutDates = Array.isArray(cleanedCalendar.blackout_dates)
      ? cleanedCalendar.blackout_dates
      : [];
    const baseEvents = Array.isArray(cleanedCalendar.events)
      ? cleanedCalendar.events.filter((e) => e.id !== '_meta_calendar')
      : [];

    // Fallback meta marker in events JSONB so if table columns are not migrated, data is preserved!
    const metaEvent = {
      id: '_meta_calendar',
      date: '9999-12-31',
      time: '',
      title: { en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' },
      speaker: '',
      location_or_link: '',
      description: JSON.stringify({
        recurring_rules: recurringRules,
        exceptions,
        blackout_dates: blackoutDates,
        default_view: cleanedCalendar.default_view || 'dynamic',
        last_reviewed_month: cleanedCalendar.last_reviewed_month || '',
      }),
    };

    const payload: Record<string, unknown> = {
      month_label: {
        en: cleanedCalendar.month_label?.en?.trim() || '',
        bm: cleanedCalendar.month_label?.bm?.trim() || '',
        cn: cleanedCalendar.month_label?.cn?.trim() || '',
        ta: cleanedCalendar.month_label?.ta?.trim() || '',
      },
      image_url: {
        en: cleanedCalendar.image_url?.en?.trim() || '',
        bm: cleanedCalendar.image_url?.bm?.trim() || '',
        cn: cleanedCalendar.image_url?.cn?.trim() || '',
        ta: cleanedCalendar.image_url?.ta?.trim() || '',
      },
      events: [...baseEvents, metaEvent],
      recurring_rules: recurringRules,
      exceptions,
      blackout_dates: blackoutDates,
      default_view: cleanedCalendar.default_view || 'dynamic',
      last_reviewed_month: cleanedCalendar.last_reviewed_month || '',
    };

    let result;
    const { data: existing } = await client
      .from('team_calendar')
      .select('id')
      .limit(1)
      .maybeSingle();

    const hasExisting = existing?.id !== undefined && existing?.id !== null;

    if (hasExisting) {
      result = await (client.from('team_calendar') as any)
        .update(payload)
        .eq('id', existing.id)
        .select()
        .single();
    } else {
      result = await (client.from('team_calendar') as any)
        .insert(payload)
        .select()
        .single();
    }

    if (result.error) {
      // If postgres schema doesn't have the newly added json columns yet, fallback to base payload
      const basePayload = {
        month_label: payload.month_label,
        image_url: payload.image_url,
        events: payload.events,
      };
      const fallbackRes = hasExisting
        ? await (client.from('team_calendar') as any).update(basePayload).eq('id', existing.id).select().single()
        : await (client.from('team_calendar') as any).insert(basePayload).select().single();

      if (fallbackRes.error) {
        throw new Error(fallbackRes.error.message);
      }
      safeRevalidate();
      return {
        success: true,
        data: {
          ...(fallbackRes.data as TeamCalendar),
          events: baseEvents,
          recurring_rules: recurringRules,
          exceptions,
          blackout_dates: blackoutDates,
          default_view: cleanedCalendar.default_view || 'dynamic',
          last_reviewed_month: cleanedCalendar.last_reviewed_month || '',
        },
      };
    }

    safeRevalidate();
    return {
      success: true,
      data: {
        ...(result.data as TeamCalendar),
        events: baseEvents,
        recurring_rules: recurringRules,
        exceptions,
        blackout_dates: blackoutDates,
        default_view: cleanedCalendar.default_view || 'dynamic',
        last_reviewed_month: cleanedCalendar.last_reviewed_month || '',
      },
    };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update calendar';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function updateDictionaryAction(
  entryOrEntries: UiDictionary | UiDictionary[]
): Promise<ActionResponse<UiDictionary[]>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['admin', 'superadmin']);
    const client = getAdminClient();

    const entries = Array.isArray(entryOrEntries) ? entryOrEntries : [entryOrEntries];
    if (entries.length === 0) {
      return { success: false, error: 'No dictionary entries provided' };
    }

    for (const item of entries) {
      if (!item.key || !item.key.trim()) {
        return { success: false, error: 'Dictionary key is required for every row' };
      }
    }

    const rows = entries.map((e) => ({
      key: e.key.trim(),
      en: e.en ?? '',
      bm: e.bm ?? '',
      cn: e.cn ?? '',
      ta: e.ta ?? '',
    }));

    const { data, error } = await client
      .from('ui_dictionary')
      .upsert(rows, { onConflict: 'key' })
      .select();

    if (error) {
      throw new Error(error.message);
    }

    safeRevalidate();
    return { success: true, data: data as UiDictionary[] };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update dictionary';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

// =============================================================================
// 4. ROLE-GUARDED ACTIONS: SUPERADMIN ONLY
// =============================================================================

export async function getPinsAction(): Promise<ActionResponse<AccessPin[]>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['superadmin']);
    const client = getAdminClient();

    const { data, error } = await client
      .from('access_pins')
      .select('*')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

    if (error) {
      throw new Error(error.message);
    }

    return { success: true, data: (data || []) as AccessPin[] };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to fetch PINs';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function upsertPinAction(pinData: {
  id?: string;
  label: string;
  pin_code: string;
  role: AccessPinRole;
  is_active?: boolean;
}): Promise<ActionResponse<AccessPin>> {
  try {
    await requireSession(['superadmin']);

    if (!pinData.label || !pinData.label.trim()) {
      return { success: false, error: 'PIN label is required' };
    }
    if (!pinData.pin_code || !pinData.pin_code.trim()) {
      return { success: false, error: 'PIN code is required' };
    }
    if (!['superadmin', 'admin', 'editor'].includes(pinData.role)) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Invalid role specified' };
    }

    const client = getAdminClient();
    const payload = {
      label: pinData.label.trim(),
      pin_code: pinData.pin_code.trim(),
      role: pinData.role,
      is_active: pinData.is_active !== false,
    };

    let result;
    if (pinData.id) {
      result = await client
        .from('access_pins')
        .update(payload)
        .eq('id', pinData.id)
        .select()
        .single();
    } else {
      result = await client
        .from('access_pins')
        .insert(payload)
        .select()
        .single();
    }

    if (result.error) {
      throw new Error(result.error.message);
    }

    return { success: true, data: result.data as AccessPin };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to save PIN';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}

export async function deletePinAction(id: string): Promise<ActionResponse<void>> {
  try {
    const session = await requireSession(['superadmin']);

    if (!id) {
      return { success: false, error: 'PIN ID is required' };
    }

    if (session.id === id) {
      return {
        success: false,
        error: 'Security restriction: You cannot delete your own currently active PIN session.',
      };
    }

    const client = getAdminClient();
    const { error } = await client.from('access_pins').delete().eq('id', id);

    if (error) {
      throw new Error(error.message);
    }

    return { success: true, message: 'PIN deleted successfully' };
  } catch (err) {
    const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to delete PIN';
    return { success: false, error: message };
  }
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/admin/TabCalendar.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import {
  Calendar as CalendarIcon,
  Upload,
  Loader2,
  Check,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
  Edit,
  Sparkles,
  Ban,
  Clock,
  MapPin,
  RotateCcw,
  Bell,
  ArrowRight,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { RecurringRuleModal } from './RecurringRuleModal';
import { InstanceOverrideModal } from './InstanceOverrideModal';
import { updateCalendarAction, uploadMediaAction } from '@/app/actions/adminActions';
import type {
  TeamCalendar,
  CalendarEvent,
  MeetingSeries,
  MeetingException,
  BlackoutDate,
} from '@/types/app';
import {
  generateMonthSchedule,
  type GeneratedScheduleEvent,
} from '@/lib/calendarGenerator';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';

interface TabCalendarProps {
  calendar: TeamCalendar;
  setCalendar: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<TeamCalendar>>;
  onNotify: (msg: string, isError?: boolean) => void;
}

export function TabCalendar({ calendar, setCalendar, onNotify }: TabCalendarProps) {
  const { getLocalized } = useApp();

  const now = new Date();
  const currentYear = now.getFullYear();
  const currentMonth = now.getMonth() + 1;
  const currentYearMonth = `${currentYear}-${String(currentMonth).padStart(2, '0')}`;
  const needsMonthReview = calendar.last_reviewed_month !== currentYearMonth;

  const [activeSubTab, setActiveSubTab] = useState<'schedule' | 'recurring' | 'blackout'>('schedule');

  const [monthLabels, setMonthLabels] = useState(calendar.month_label || { en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [imageUrls, setImageUrls] = useState(calendar.image_url || { en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' });
  const [defaultView, setDefaultView] = useState<'dynamic' | 'flyer'>(calendar.default_view || 'dynamic');

  const [recurringRules, setRecurringRules] = useState<MeetingSeries[]>(calendar.recurring_rules || []);
  const [exceptions, setExceptions] = useState<MeetingException[]>(calendar.exceptions || []);
  const [blackoutDates, setBlackoutDates] = useState<BlackoutDate[]>(calendar.blackout_dates || []);
  const [events, setEvents] = useState<CalendarEvent[]>(calendar.events || []);

  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  // Auto-persist calendar state directly to Supabase
  const persistCalendar = async (updates: Partial<TeamCalendar>) => {
    setSaving(true);
    const payload: Partial<TeamCalendar> = {
      ...(calendar.id ? { id: calendar.id } : {}),
      month_label: updates.month_label ?? monthLabels,
      image_url: updates.image_url ?? imageUrls,
      default_view: updates.default_view ?? defaultView,
      recurring_rules: updates.recurring_rules ?? recurringRules,
      exceptions: updates.exceptions ?? exceptions,
      blackout_dates: updates.blackout_dates ?? blackoutDates,
      events: updates.events ?? events,
      last_reviewed_month: updates.last_reviewed_month ?? calendar.last_reviewed_month ?? currentYearMonth,
    };

    const res = await updateCalendarAction(payload);
    setSaving(false);

    if (res.success && res.data) {
      setCalendar(res.data);
      return res.data;
    }
    return null;
  };

  // Meeting Rule Modal
  const [ruleModalOpen, setRuleModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingRule, setEditingRule] = useState<MeetingSeries | null>(null);

  // Instance Override Modal
  const [overrideModalOpen, setOverrideModalOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editingInstance, setEditingInstance] = useState<GeneratedScheduleEvent | null>(null);

  // Blackout Date Inline Form
  const [newBlackoutDate, setNewBlackoutDate] = useState('');
  const [newBlackoutReason, setNewBlackoutReason] = useState('');
  const [newBlackoutRuleId, setNewBlackoutRuleId] = useState('all');

  const fileInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  // Current month schedule preview
  const currentMonthSchedule = useMemo(() => {
    return generateMonthSchedule(
      currentYear,
      currentMonth,
      recurringRules,
      blackoutDates,
      events,
      exceptions
    );
  }, [currentYear, currentMonth, recurringRules, blackoutDates, events, exceptions]);

  const handleFileUpload = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    setUploading(true);
    try {
      const formData = new FormData();
      formData.append('file', file);
      const res = await uploadMediaAction(formData);

      if (res.success && res.url) {
        const nextImages = {
          en: res.url,
          bm: res.url,
          cn: res.url,
          ta: res.url,
        };
        setImageUrls(nextImages);
        const saved = await persistCalendar({ image_url: nextImages });
        if (saved) {
          onNotify('Flyer image uploaded and saved successfully');
        } else {
          onNotify('Flyer image uploaded, local sync only', true);
        }
      } else {
        onNotify('Image upload failed', true);
      }
    } catch {
      onNotify('Upload service error', true);
    } finally {
      setUploading(false);
      if (fileInputRef.current) fileInputRef.current.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveFlyer = async () => {
    const emptyImages = { en: '', bm: '', cn: '', ta: '' };
    setImageUrls(emptyImages);
    const saved = await persistCalendar({ image_url: emptyImages });
    if (saved) onNotify('Monthly flyer removed and saved');
  };

  const handleToggleDefaultView = async (view: 'dynamic' | 'flyer') => {
    setDefaultView(view);
    const saved = await persistCalendar({ default_view: view });
    if (saved) onNotify(`Default view set to ${view === 'dynamic' ? 'Dynamic Calendar' : 'Monthly Flyer'}`);
  };

  const handleUrlChange = (val: string) => {
    setImageUrls({ en: val, bm: val, cn: val, ta: val });
  };

  const handleUrlBlur = async () => {
    if (imageUrls.en !== (calendar.image_url?.en || '')) {
      await persistCalendar({ image_url: imageUrls });
      onNotify('Flyer URL saved');
    }
  };

  const handleSaveAll = async () => {
    setSaving(true);
    const payload: Partial<TeamCalendar> = {
      ...(calendar.id ? { id: calendar.id } : {}),
      month_label: monthLabels,
      image_url: imageUrls,
      default_view: defaultView,
      recurring_rules: recurringRules,
      exceptions,
      blackout_dates: blackoutDates,
      events,
      last_reviewed_month: calendar.last_reviewed_month || currentYearMonth,
    };

    const res = await updateCalendarAction(payload);
    setSaving(false);

    if (res.success && res.data) {
      setCalendar(res.data);
      onNotify('Monthly calendar & schedule saved successfully!');
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to save calendar', true);
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmMonthReview = async () => {
    setSaving(true);
    const payload: Partial<TeamCalendar> = {
      ...(calendar.id ? { id: calendar.id } : {}),
      month_label: monthLabels,
      image_url: imageUrls,
      default_view: defaultView,
      recurring_rules: recurringRules,
      exceptions,
      blackout_dates: blackoutDates,
      events,
      last_reviewed_month: currentYearMonth,
    };

    const res = await updateCalendarAction(payload);
    setSaving(false);

    if (res.success && res.data) {
      setCalendar(res.data);
      onNotify(`Month schedule confirmed and saved for ${currentYearMonth}!`);
    } else {
      onNotify('Failed to save month confirmation', true);
    }
  };

  const handleSaveRule = async (savedRule: MeetingSeries) => {
    const nextRules = recurringRules.some((r) => r.id === savedRule.id)
      ? recurringRules.map((r) => (r.id === savedRule.id ? savedRule : r))
      : [...recurringRules, savedRule];
    setRecurringRules(nextRules);
    setRuleModalOpen(false);
    setEditingRule(null);
    const res = await persistCalendar({ recurring_rules: nextRules });
    if (res) onNotify('Meeting rule saved');
    else onNotify('Meeting rule saved locally', true);
  };

  const handleDeleteRule = async (id: string) => {
    const nextRules = recurringRules.filter((r) => r.id !== id);
    setRecurringRules(nextRules);
    const res = await persistCalendar({ recurring_rules: nextRules });
    if (res) onNotify('Meeting rule removed');
    else onNotify('Failed to remove meeting rule', true);
  };

  const handleToggleRuleActive = async (id: string) => {
    const nextRules = recurringRules.map((r) =>
      r.id === id ? { ...r, is_active: !r.is_active } : r
    );
    setRecurringRules(nextRules);
    await persistCalendar({ recurring_rules: nextRules });
  };

  const handleOpenOverride = (instance: GeneratedScheduleEvent) => {
    setEditingInstance(instance);
    setOverrideModalOpen(true);
  };

  const handleSaveException = async (exception: MeetingException) => {
    const nextExceptions = [
      ...exceptions.filter(
        (e) => !(e.series_id === exception.series_id && e.original_date === exception.original_date)
      ),
      exception,
    ];
    setExceptions(nextExceptions);
    setOverrideModalOpen(false);
    setEditingInstance(null);
    const res = await persistCalendar({ exceptions: nextExceptions });
    if (res) onNotify('Schedule override applied and saved');
    else onNotify('Failed to save override', true);
  };

  const handleSkipInstance = async (instance: GeneratedScheduleEvent) => {
    const skipEx: MeetingException = {
      id: `ex-${Date.now()}`,
      series_id: instance.series_id || instance.rule_id || 'all',
      original_date: instance.original_date || instance.date,
      action: 'cancel',
      reason: 'Skipped for this date',
    };
    const nextExceptions = [
      ...exceptions.filter(
        (e) => !(e.series_id === skipEx.series_id && e.original_date === skipEx.original_date)
      ),
      skipEx,
    ];
    setExceptions(nextExceptions);
    const res = await persistCalendar({ exceptions: nextExceptions });
    if (res) onNotify(`Meeting on ${instance.date} skipped and saved`);
    else onNotify('Failed to save skip', true);
  };

  const handleRestoreInstance = async (seriesId: string, originalDate: string) => {
    const nextExceptions = exceptions.filter(
      (e) => !(e.series_id === seriesId && e.original_date === originalDate)
    );
    setExceptions(nextExceptions);
    const res = await persistCalendar({ exceptions: nextExceptions });
    if (res) onNotify(`Restored occurrence for ${originalDate}`);
  };

  const handleAddBlackout = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!newBlackoutDate.trim()) return;
    const newEntry: BlackoutDate = {
      id: `blackout-${Date.now()}`,
      date: newBlackoutDate.trim(),
      reason: newBlackoutReason.trim() || 'Meeting Cancelled / Rescheduled',
      rule_id: newBlackoutRuleId,
    };
    const nextBlackouts = [...blackoutDates, newEntry];
    setBlackoutDates(nextBlackouts);
    setNewBlackoutDate('');
    setNewBlackoutReason('');
    setNewBlackoutRuleId('all');
    const res = await persistCalendar({ blackout_dates: nextBlackouts });
    if (res) onNotify('Blackout date added and saved');
    else onNotify('Failed to save blackout date', true);
  };

  const handleDeleteBlackout = async (id: string) => {
    const nextBlackouts = blackoutDates.filter((b) => b.id !== id);
    setBlackoutDates(nextBlackouts);
    const res = await persistCalendar({ blackout_dates: nextBlackouts });
    if (res) onNotify('Blackout date removed');
  };

  const handleSaveEvent = async (savedEvent: CalendarEvent) => {
    const exists = events.some((e) => e.id === savedEvent.id);
    const nextEvents = exists
      ? events.map((e) => (e.id === savedEvent.id ? savedEvent : e))
      : [...events, savedEvent];
    setEvents(nextEvents);
    await persistCalendar({ events: nextEvents });
    onNotify('Special event updated');
  };

  const handleDeleteEvent = async (id: string) => {
    const nextEvents = events.filter((e) => e.id !== id);
    setEvents(nextEvents);
    await persistCalendar({ events: nextEvents });
    onNotify('Special event removed');
  };

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      {/* Monthly Review Prompt Banner */}
      {needsMonthReview && (
        <div className="rounded-3xl border border-amber-300 dark:border-amber-600/50 bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500/15 via-orange-500/10 to-amber-500/15 p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-sm flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3.5">
            <div className="p-2.5 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-300 flex-shrink-0">
              <Bell className="w-5 h-5" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <h4 className="font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                🔔 Review Month Schedule ({currentYearMonth})
              </h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-0.5 leading-relaxed">
                Please review this month&apos;s schedule preview below, reschedule or skip any occurrences, and confirm the month schedule.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleConfirmMonthReview}
            disabled={saving}
            className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 flex-shrink-0 self-start sm:self-auto"
          >
            <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[3]" />
            <span>Confirm &amp; Save Month Schedule</span>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Header Bar */}
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-4">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-base sm:text-lg font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 flex items-center gap-2">
            <CalendarIcon className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-500" />
            <span>Monthly Calendar &amp; Schedule Management</span>
          </h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
            Manage meeting rules, monthly schedule overrides, blackout dates, and the flyer.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={handleSaveAll}
          disabled={saving}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-md shadow-orange-500/20 transition-all active:scale-95 disabled:opacity-50 self-start sm:self-auto"
        >
          {saving ? (
            <>
              <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
              <span>Saving...</span>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Save Calendar &amp; Schedule</span>
            </>
          )}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Prominent Monthly Calendar Flyer & Default View Card */}
      <div className="rounded-3xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 p-4 sm:p-5 shadow-sm space-y-4">
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 border-b border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800 pb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 flex items-center gap-2">
              <span>🖼️</span>
              <span>Monthly Calendar Flyer &amp; Default View</span>
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 mt-0.5">
              Upload this month&apos;s flyer poster and toggle which view regular members see first.
            </p>
          </div>

          {/* Default View Toggle */}
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 p-1 rounded-xl bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800/80 border border-zinc-200/80 dark:border-zinc-700/80 self-start sm:self-auto">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleToggleDefaultView('dynamic')}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
                defaultView === 'dynamic'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100'
              }`}
            >
              <span>📅 Dynamic Calendar</span>
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => handleToggleDefaultView('flyer')}
              className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition-all ${
                defaultView === 'flyer'
                  ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400 shadow-xs'
                  : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-zinc-100'
              }`}
            >
              <span>🖼️ Monthly Flyer</span>
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Flyer Upload & URL Area */}
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-12 gap-4 items-start">
          <div className="lg:col-span-7 space-y-3">
            <label className="block text-xs font-semibold text-zinc-700 dark:text-zinc-300">
              Flyer Image URL or Upload
            </label>
            <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-2">
              <input
                type="text"
                value={imageUrls.en || ''}
                onChange={(e) => handleUrlChange(e.target.value)}
                onBlur={handleUrlBlur}
                placeholder="https://... image URL (or upload below)"
                className="flex-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-zinc-900 dark:text-zinc-100 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-500/20"
              />
              <input
                ref={fileInputRef}
                type="file"
                onChange={handleFileUpload}
                className="hidden"
                accept="image/*"
              />
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => fileInputRef.current?.click()}
                disabled={uploading}
                className="inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-gradient-to-r from-amber-500 to-orange-500 hover:from-amber-600 hover:to-orange-600 text-white text-xs font-bold shadow-xs transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 flex-shrink-0"
              >
                {uploading ? (
                  <>
                    <Loader2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 animate-spin" />
                    <span>Uploading...</span>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Upload className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>Upload Monthly Flyer Image</span>
                  </>
                )}
              </button>
            </div>
            {imageUrls.en && (
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-1">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={handleRemoveFlyer}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/60 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                >
                  <Trash2 className="w-3 h-3" />
                  <span>Remove Flyer</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Live Thumbnail Preview */}
          <div className="lg:col-span-5">
            {imageUrls.en ? (
              <div className="relative rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950 max-h-48 flex items-center justify-center group">
                {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
                <img
                  src={imageUrls.en}
                  alt="Monthly Flyer Preview"
                  className="w-full h-auto max-h-48 object-contain"
                />
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="h-28 rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-zinc-400 dark:text-zinc-600 p-4 text-center">
                <Upload className="w-5 h-5 mb-1 opacity-50" />
                <span className="text-xs">No monthly flyer uploaded yet</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>


      {/* Subtabs */}
      <div className="flex overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar gap-1.5 p-1 bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-900 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800">
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveSubTab('schedule')}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${
            activeSubTab === 'schedule'
              ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 shadow-xs'
              : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
          }`}
        >
          <span>📅</span>
          <span>Current Month Schedule ({currentMonthSchedule.length})</span>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveSubTab('recurring')}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${
            activeSubTab === 'recurring'
              ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 shadow-xs'
              : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
          }`}
        >
          <span>🔄</span>
          <span>Meeting Rules &amp; Series ({recurringRules.length})</span>
        </button>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => setActiveSubTab('blackout')}
          className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all whitespace-nowrap ${
            activeSubTab === 'blackout'
              ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50 shadow-xs'
              : 'text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400'
          }`}
        >
          <span>🚫</span>
          <span>Blackout Dates ({blackoutDates.length})</span>
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* SUB-SECTION 0: CURRENT-MONTH SCHEDULE PREVIEW & INSTANCE OVERRIDES */}
      {activeSubTab === 'schedule' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">
                Current Month Schedule ({currentYearMonth})
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                1-tap override or reschedule any specific date occurrence without affecting future months.
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => { setEditingRule(null); setRuleModalOpen(true); }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-xs self-start sm:self-auto"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Add Meeting Rule</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          {currentMonthSchedule.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-8 text-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white/50 dark:bg-zinc-900/40">
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 mb-2">No meetings generated for this month.</p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => { setEditingRule(null); setRuleModalOpen(true); }}
                className="px-3.5 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 text-white text-xs font-bold"
              >
                Create your first meeting rule
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {currentMonthSchedule.map((ev) => {
                const titleStr = getLocalized(ev.title);
                const hasException = Boolean(ev.is_modified);

                return (
                  <div
                    key={ev.id}
                    className={`p-3.5 rounded-2xl border transition-all flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
                      hasException
                        ? 'border-purple-300 dark:border-purple-800/60 bg-purple-50/40 dark:bg-purple-950/20 shadow-xs'
                        : 'border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap mb-1">
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md bg-amber-100 text-amber-900 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300">
                          {ev.date}
                        </span>
                        {ev.time && (
                          <span className="text-xs text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400 font-semibold flex items-center gap-1">
                            <Clock className="w-3 h-3 text-zinc-400" />
                            <span>{ev.time}</span>
                          </span>
                        )}
                        {hasException && (
                          <span className="text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-200 text-purple-900 dark:bg-purple-900/60 dark:text-purple-200">
                            Rescheduled ({ev.reason || 'Overridden'})
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>

                      <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">{titleStr}</h4>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1 text-[11px] text-zinc-500 dark:text-zinc-400 flex-wrap">
                        {ev.speaker && <span>🎙️ {ev.speaker}</span>}
                        {ev.venue && <span>📍 {ev.venue}</span>}
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-shrink-0 self-end sm:self-center">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleOpenOverride(ev)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-50 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200 text-xs font-bold transition-all active:scale-95"
                      >
                        <Edit className="w-3 h-3 text-amber-500" />
                        <span>Reschedule / Edit</span>
                      </button>

                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleSkipInstance(ev)}
                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900/50 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 dark:bg-red-950/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-300 text-xs font-bold transition-all active:scale-95"
                      >
                        <Ban className="w-3 h-3" />
                        <span>Skip Date</span>
                      </button>

                      {hasException && ev.original_date && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => handleRestoreInstance(ev.series_id || ev.rule_id || '', ev.original_date!)}
                          title="Restore original rule"
                          className="p-1.5 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-600 dark:text-zinc-400"
                        >
                          <RotateCcw className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      {/* SUB-SECTION 1: RECURRING MEETINGS */}
      {activeSubTab === 'recurring' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">Meeting Rules &amp; Series</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
                Meeting rules that expand automatically into monthly calendar schedules.
              </p>
            </div>
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => {
                setEditingRule(null);
                setRuleModalOpen(true);
              }}
              className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-2 rounded-xl bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-xs"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Add Meeting Rule</span>
            </button>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
            {recurringRules.map((rule) => {
              const freqLabel =
                rule.frequency === 'daily'
                  ? 'Daily'
                  : rule.frequency === 'weekly'
                  ? `Weekly (${['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'][rule.day_of_week ?? 0]})`
                  : rule.frequency === 'monthly_nth_weekday'
                  ? `Monthly (${rule.nth_week ?? 1}th ${['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat'][rule.day_of_week ?? 0]})`
                  : rule.frequency === 'monthly_date'
                  ? `Monthly (Day ${rule.day_of_month ?? 15})`
                  : `One-Time (${rule.date || 'Once'})`;

              return (
                <div
                  key={rule.id}
                  className={`p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all flex flex-col justify-between gap-3 ${
                    rule.is_active
                      ? 'bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 shadow-xs'
                      : 'bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-900/40 border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 opacity-60'
                  }`}
                >
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                      <span className="px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 dark:bg-amber-950/60 dark:text-amber-300">
                        {freqLabel}
                      </span>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => handleToggleRuleActive(rule.id)}
                        className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full border transition-colors ${
                          rule.is_active
                            ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-800 border-emerald-300 dark:bg-emerald-950/60 dark:text-emerald-300'
                            : 'bg-zinc-200 text-zinc-600 border-zinc-300 dark:bg-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-400'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {rule.is_active ? 'Active' : 'Paused'}
                      </button>
                    </div>

                    <h4 className="font-bold text-sm text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">{rule.title}</h4>
                    {rule.time && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Clock className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                        <span>{rule.time}</span>
                      </p>
                    )}
                    {rule.venue && (
                      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500 flex items-center gap-1 truncate">
                        <MapPin className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-red-500 flex-shrink-0" />
                        <span className="truncate">{rule.venue}</span>
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1 pt-2 border-t border-zinc-100 dark:border-zinc-800">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => {
                        setEditingRule(rule);
                        setRuleModalOpen(true);
                      }}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-zinc-600 hover:bg-zinc-100 dark:text-zinc-400 dark:hover:bg-zinc-800"
                    >
                      <Edit className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => handleDeleteRule(rule.id)}
                      className="p-1.5 rounded-lg text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/40"
                    >
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* SUB-SECTION 2: BLACKOUT DATES */}
      {activeSubTab === 'blackout' && (
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-zinc-950 dark:text-zinc-50">Blackout Dates &amp; Overrides</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
              Cancel or pause recurring meetings on specific holiday or special event dates.
            </p>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={handleAddBlackout} className="p-4 rounded-2xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 shadow-xs flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-3">
            <input
              type="text"
              required
              value={newBlackoutDate}
              onChange={(e) => setNewBlackoutDate(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Date (YYYY-MM-DD)"
              className="w-full sm:w-40 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs font-mono"
            />
            <input
              type="text"
              value={newBlackoutReason}
              onChange={(e) => setNewBlackoutReason(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Reason (e.g. Public Holiday, Rally)"
              className="w-full sm:flex-1 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
            />
            <select
              value={newBlackoutRuleId}
              onChange={(e) => setNewBlackoutRuleId(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full sm:w-48 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-50 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-3 py-2 text-xs"
            >
              <option value="all">All Recurring Meetings</option>
              {recurringRules.map((r) => (
                <option key={r.id} value={r.id}>{r.title}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
            <button
              type="submit"
              className="w-full sm:w-auto inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 rounded-xl bg-zinc-900 text-white dark:bg-white dark:text-zinc-950 text-xs font-bold"
            >
              <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
              <span>Add Blackout</span>
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="space-y-2">
            {blackoutDates.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="p-6 text-center rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-zinc-300 dark:border-zinc-800">
                <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">No blackout dates added.</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              blackoutDates.map((b) => {
                const targetRule = recurringRules.find((r) => r.id === b.rule_id);
                const targetText = b.rule_id === 'all' || !b.rule_id ? 'All Recurring Meetings' : targetRule?.title || b.rule_id;
                return (
                  <div key={b.id} className="p-3 rounded-xl border border-zinc-200 dark:border-zinc-800 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-red-600 dark:text-red-400">{b.date}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs font-medium text-zinc-800 dark:text-zinc-200">{b.reason}</span>
                      <span className="text-[10px] bg-zinc-100 dark:bg-zinc-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-zinc-500">{targetText}</span>
                    </div>
                    <button type="button" onClick={() => handleDeleteBlackout(b.id)} className="p-1 rounded text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50">
                      <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    </button>
                  </div>
                );
              })
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modals */}
      <RecurringRuleModal
        isOpen={ruleModalOpen}
        onClose={() => setRuleModalOpen(false)}
        onSave={handleSaveRule}
        rule={editingRule}
      />

      <InstanceOverrideModal
        isOpen={overrideModalOpen}
        onClose={() => {
          setOverrideModalOpen(false);
          setEditingInstance(null);
        }}
        instance={editingInstance}
        onSave={handleSaveException}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
</file>

<file path="src/components/ToolCard.tsx">
'use client';

import React, { useState, useMemo } from 'react';
import Image from 'next/image';
import {
  Play,
  Share2,
  Copy,
  Check,
  QrCode,
  Star,
  ExternalLink,
  BookOpen,
  Headphones,
  Globe,
  ImageIcon,
  Video,
  X,
  Sparkles,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { useApp } from '@/context/AppContext';
import type { Tool, LanguageCode } from '@/types/app';
import { QRCodeModal } from '@/components/QRCodeModal';
import { ImageLightbox } from '@/components/ImageLightbox';

interface ToolCardProps {
  tool: Tool;
}

export function getYouTubeId(rawUrl: string): string | null {
  if (!rawUrl) return null;
  const cleanUrl = rawUrl.replace(/\s+/g, '');
  const match = cleanUrl.match(
    /(?:youtu\.be\/|youtube\.com\/(?:embed\/|v\/|watch\?.*[?&]v=|watch\?v=|shorts\/))([\w-]{10,12})/
  ) || cleanUrl.match(/(?:youtu\.be\/)([\w-]{10,12})/);
  return match ? match[1] : null;
}

const LANG_LABELS: Record<LanguageCode, string> = {
  en: 'EN',
  bm: 'BM',
  cn: '中文',
  ta: 'தமிழ்',
};

export function ToolCard({ tool }: ToolCardProps) {
  const { lang, getLocalized, isToolSaved, toggleSavedTool, t } = useApp();

  const [isPlayingInline, setIsPlayingInline] = useState(false);
  const [isQrOpen, setIsQrOpen] = useState(false);
  const [isLightboxOpen, setIsLightboxOpen] = useState(false);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  const title = getLocalized(tool.title) || 'Untitled Resource';
  const description = getLocalized(tool.description);
  const url = getLocalized(tool.url);
  const whatsappScript = getLocalized(tool.whatsapp_script);
  const isSaved = isToolSaved(tool.id);

  const youtubeId = useMemo(() => {
    if (tool.type !== 'youtube') return null;
    return getYouTubeId(url);
  }, [tool.type, url]);

  const displayLangBadge = useMemo(() => {
    if (tool.available_languages && tool.available_languages.length > 0) {
      return tool.available_languages
        .map((code) => LANG_LABELS[code] || code.toUpperCase())
        .join(' • ');
    }
    return 'EN';
